nd

	local toggleCount = 0
	local function SetVisbility(newVisibility)
		this.Visible = newVisibility
		if this.ChatWindowWidget then
			this.ChatWindowWidget:ToggleVisibility(this.Visible)
			if this.Visible then
				toggleCount = toggleCount + 1
				local thisToggle = toggleCount
				local thisFocusCount = focusCount
				this.ChatWindowWidget:FadeIn()
				this.ChatWindowWidget:SetFadeLock(true)
				delay(5, function()
					if thisToggle == toggleCount and thisFocusCount == focusCount then
						this.ChatWindowWidget:SetFadeLock(false)
					end
				end)
			end
		end
		if this.ChatBarWidget then
			this.ChatBarWidget:ToggleVisibility(this.Visible)
			if this.Visible then
				this.ChatBarWidget:FadeIn()
			end
			if InputService.VREnabled and not this.Visible then
				this.ChatBarWidget:RemoveFocus()
			end
		end
		if InputService.VREnabled then
			local Panel3D = require(RobloxGui.Modules.VR.Panel3D)

			local panel = Panel3D.Get(thisModuleName)
			if this.Visible then
				local topbarPanel = Panel3D.Get("Topbar3D")
				panel.localCF = topbarPanel.localCF * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-5), 0, 0) * CFrame.new(0, 4, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(-15), 0, 0)
				panel:SetVisible(true)
				panel:ForceShowUntilLookedAt()

				VRHub:FireModuleOpened(thisModuleName)
			else
				panel:SetVisible(this.Visible)

				VRHub:FireModuleClosed(thisModuleName)
			end
		end
		this.VisibilityStateChanged:fire(this.Visible)
	end

	function this:ToggleVisibility()
		SetVisbility(not self.Visible)
	end

	function this:SetVisible(visible)
		SetVisbility(visible)
	end

	function this:FocusChatBar()
		if self.ChatBarWidget and this.Visible then
			self.ChatBarWidget:FocusChatBar()
		end
	end

	function this:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
		if not self.ChatBarWidget then return false end
		return self.ChatBarWidget:IsFocused() or (useWasFocused and self.ChatBarWidget:WasFocused())
	end

	function this:GetCurrentWindowMessageCount()
		if this.ChatWindowWidget then
			return this.ChatWindowWidget:GetMessageCount()
		end
		return 0
	end

	function this:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
		topbarEnabled = enabled
		-- Update coregui to reflect new topbar status
		local enabled = true
		pcall(function()
			enabled = StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
		end)
		self:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, enabled)
	end

	function this:Initialize()
		--[[ Developer Customization API ]]--
		if not NON_CORESCRIPT_MODE then
			pcall(function()
				StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage", 	function(informationTable)
					if this.ChatWindowWidget then
						this.ChatWindowWidget:AddDeveloperSystemChatMessage(informationTable)
					end
				end)
				local function isUDim2Value(value)
					return typeof(value) == "UDim2" and value or nil
				end

				local function isBubbleChatOn()
					return IsBubbleChatOnly()
				end

				StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("ChatWindowPosition", 	function(value)
					if this.ChatWindowWidget and this.ChatBarWidget then
						value = isUDim2Value(value)
						if value ~= nil and not isBubbleChatOn() then
							chatRepositioned = true -- Prevent chat from moving back to the original position on screen resolution change
							this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position = value
							this.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Position = value + UDim2.new(0, 0, this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Scale, this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Offset + 2)
						end
					end
				end)

				StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("ChatWindowSize", 	function(value)
					if this.ChatWindowWidget and this.ChatBarWidget then
						value = isUDim2Value(value)
						if value ~= nil and not isBubbleChatOn() then
							chatRepositioned = true
							this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size = value
							this.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Size = UDim2.new(this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.X.Scale, this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.X.Offset, this.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Size.Y.Scale, this.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Size.Y.Offset)
							this.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Position = this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position + UDim2.new(0, 0, this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Scale, this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Offset + 2)
						end
					end
				end)

				StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("ChatWindowPosition", 	function()
					if this.ChatWindowWidget then
						return this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position
					else
						return nil
					end
				end)

				StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("ChatWindowSize", 	function()
					if this.ChatWindowWidget then
						return this.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size
					else
						return nil
					end
				end)

				StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("ChatBarDisabled", 	function(value)
					if this.ChatBarWidget then
						if type(value) == "boolean" then
							chatBarDisabled = value
							if value == true then
								this.ChatBarWidget:ToggleVisibility(false)
							end
						end
					end
				end)

				StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("ChatBarDisabled", function() return chatBarDisabled end)
			end)
		end

		this:OnPlayerAdded(Player)
		-- Upsettingly, it seems everytime a player is added, you have to redo the connection
		-- NOTE: PlayerAdded only fires on the server, hence ChildAdded is used here
		PlayersService.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA('Player') then
				this:OnPlayerAdded(child)
			end
		end)
		this:CreateGUI()


		pcall(function()
			this:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat))
		end)
		this.CoreGuiChangedConn = Util.DisconnectEvent(this.CoreGuiChangedConn)
		pcall(function()
			this.CoreGuiChangedConn = StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal:connect(
				function(coreGuiType, enabled)
					this:CoreGuiChanged(coreGuiType, enabled)
				end
			)
		end)

		if not NON_CORESCRIPT_MODE then
			this:PrintWelcome()
		end

		--SetVisbility(true)
	end

	return this
end

local moduleApiTable = {}
-- Main Entry Point
do
	moduleApiTable.ModuleName = thisModuleName
	moduleApiTable.KeepVRTopbarOpen = true
	moduleApiTable.VRIsExclusive = true
	moduleApiTable.VRClosesNonExclusive = false
	VRHub:RegisterModule(moduleApiTable)

	VRHub.ModuleOpened.Event:connect(function(moduleName)
		if moduleName ~= thisModuleName then
			local module = VRHub:GetModule(moduleName)
			if module.VRIsExclusive then
				moduleApiTable:SetVisible(false)
			end
		end
	end)

	local ChatInstance = CreateChat()
	ChatInstance:Initialize()
	DisplayChatError = function(message)
		if ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget then
			ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget:AddSystemChatMessage(tostring(message))
		else
			warn("[Chat] " .. tostring(message))
		end
	end

	-- The historical widgets are the frontend for NewChat. BindableEvents keep
	-- the existing ChatSelector/Topbar API intact, while TextChatService remains
	-- the only message transport.
	local FrontendConnectionsFolder = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("LegacyChatFrontendConnections")
	if not FrontendConnectionsFolder then
		FrontendConnectionsFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
		FrontendConnectionsFolder.Name = "LegacyChatFrontendConnections"
		FrontendConnectionsFolder.Parent = RobloxGui
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(FrontendConnectionsFolder:GetChildren()) do
		child:Destroy()
	end

	local function MakeBindable(className, name)
		local instance = Instance.new(className)
		instance.Name = name
		instance.Parent = FrontendConnectionsFolder
		return instance
	end

	local ChatWindowConnections = {
		ToggleVisibility = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ToggleVisibility"),
		SetVisible = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "SetVisible"),
		FocusChatBar = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "FocusChatBar"),
		TopbarEnabledChanged = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "TopbarEnabledChanged"),
		SpecialKeyPressed = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "SpecialKeyPressed"),
		CoreGuiEnabled = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "CoreGuiEnabled"),
		ChatBarFocusChanged = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ChatBarFocusChanged"),
		VisibilityStateChanged = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "VisibilityStateChanged"),
		GetVisibility = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "GetVisibility"),
		GetMessageCount = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "GetMessageCount"),
		IsFocused = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "IsFocused"),
	}

	local SetCoreConnections = {
		ChatMakeSystemMessage = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ChatMakeSystemMessage"),
		ChatWindowPosition = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ChatWindowPosition"),
		ChatWindowSize = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ChatWindowSize"),
		ChatBarDisabled = MakeBindable("BindableEvent", "ChatBarDisabled"),
	}

	local GetCoreConnections = {
		ChatWindowPosition = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "GetChatWindowPosition"),
		ChatWindowSize = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "GetChatWindowSize"),
		ChatBarDisabled = MakeBindable("BindableFunction", "GetChatBarDisabled"),
	}

	ChatWindowConnections.ToggleVisibility.Event:Connect(function()
		ChatInstance:ToggleVisibility()
	end)
	ChatWindowConnections.SetVisible.Event:Connect(function(visible)
		ChatInstance:SetVisible(visible == true)
	end)
	ChatWindowConnections.FocusChatBar.Event:Connect(function()
		if not ChatInstance.Visible then
			ChatInstance:SetVisible(true)
		end
		ChatInstance:FocusChatBar()
	end)
	ChatWindowConnections.TopbarEnabledChanged.Event:Connect(function(enabled)
		ChatInstance:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled == true)
	end)
	ChatWindowConnections.CoreGuiEnabled.Event:Connect(function(enabled)
		ChatInstance:CoreGuiChanged(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, enabled == true)
	end)
	ChatWindowConnections.SpecialKeyPressed.Event:Connect(function(keyCode)
		if keyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Slash then
			if not ChatInstance.Visible then
				ChatInstance:SetVisible(true)
			end
			ChatInstance:FocusChatBar()
		end
	end)

	ChatWindowConnections.GetVisibility.OnInvoke = function()
		return ChatInstance.Visible
	end
	ChatWindowConnections.GetMessageCount.OnInvoke = function()
		return ChatInstance:GetCurrentWindowMessageCount()
	end
	ChatWindowConnections.IsFocused.OnInvoke = function(useWasFocused)
		return ChatInstance:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	end

	ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged:connect(function(focused)
		ChatWindowConnections.ChatBarFocusChanged:Fire(focused == true)
	end)
	ChatInstance.VisibilityStateChanged:connect(function(visible)
		ChatWindowConnections.VisibilityStateChanged:Fire(visible == true)
	end)

	SetCoreConnections.ChatMakeSystemMessage.Event:Connect(function(informationTable)
		if ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget then
			ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget:AddDeveloperSystemChatMessage(informationTable)
		end
	end)
	SetCoreConnections.ChatWindowPosition.Event:Connect(function(value)
		if typeof(value) == "UDim2" and ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget and ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget
			and not IsBubbleChatOnly() then
			chatRepositioned = true
			ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position = value
			ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Position = value + UDim2.new(
				0, 0,
				ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Scale,
				ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size.Y.Offset + 2
			)
		end
	end)
	SetCoreConnections.ChatWindowSize.Event:Connect(function(value)
		if typeof(value) == "UDim2" and ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget and ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget
			and not IsBubbleChatOnly() then
			chatRepositioned = true
			ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size = value
			ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget.ChatBarContainer.Position = ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position + UDim2.new(
				0, 0,
				value.Y.Scale,
				value.Y.Offset + 2
			)
		end
	end)
	SetCoreConnections.ChatBarDisabled.Event:Connect(function(value)
		if type(value) == "boolean" then
			chatBarDisabled = value
			if ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget then
				ChatInstance.ChatBarWidget:CalculateVisibility()
			end
		end
	end)

	GetCoreConnections.ChatWindowPosition.OnInvoke = function()
		return ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget and ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Position or nil
	end
	GetCoreConnections.ChatWindowSize.OnInvoke = function()
		return ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget and ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget.ChatContainer.Size or nil
	end
	GetCoreConnections.ChatBarDisabled.OnInvoke = function()
		return chatBarDisabled
	end

	if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.RegisterCoreGuiConnections) == "function" then
		local registerOK, registered = pcall(function()
			return ChatBackend:RegisterCoreGuiConnections({
				ChatWindow = ChatWindowConnections,
				SetCore = SetCoreConnections,
				GetCore = GetCoreConnections,
			})
		end)
		if not registerOK or registered ~= true then
			warn("[Chat] Could not register historical renderer with NewChat: " .. tostring(registered))
		else
			print("[Chat] Historical chat renderer registered with NewChat.")
		end
	else
		warn("[Chat] NewChat backend is unavailable; the historical GUI can render but cannot send through TextChatService.")
	end

	-- Convert filtered TextChatService messages into historical chat rows.
	-- No Player.Chatted / Players.PlayerChatted / Players:Chat fallback is used.
	local function OnBackendMessage(message)
		if not ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget or not message then
			return
		end

		local ok, status = pcall(function() return message.Status end)
		if ok and status and Enum.TextChatMessageStatus and status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
			return
		end

		local text = nil
		local source = nil
		local channel = nil
		pcall(function()
			text = message.Text
			source = message.TextSource
			channel = message.TextChannel
		end)
		if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then
			return
		end

		if source then
			local sender = nil
			pcall(function()
				sender = PlayersService:GetPlayerByUserId(source.UserId)
			end)
			if not sender then
				return
			end

			local chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.All
			local channelName = ""
			pcall(function()
				channelName = string.lower(channel and channel.Name or "")
			end)
			if string.find(channelName, "team", 1, true) then
				chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.Team
			elseif string.find(channelName, "whisper", 1, true) then
				chatType = Enum.PlayerChatType.Whisper
			end

			ChatInstance:OnPlayerChatted(chatType, sender, text, nil)
		else
			ChatInstance.ChatWindowWidget:AddSystemChatMessage(text)
		end
	end

	if ChatBackend and ChatBackend.IncomingMessage then
		local incomingSignal = ChatBackend.IncomingMessage
		local incomingConnect = incomingSignal.Connect or incomingSignal.connect
		if type(incomingConnect) == "function" then
			local incomingOK, incomingConnection = pcall(incomingConnect, incomingSignal, OnBackendMessage)
			if not incomingOK then
				warn("[Chat] Could not attach NewChat incoming-message signal: " .. tostring(incomingConnection))
			end
		end
	else
		warn("[Chat] NewChat.IncomingMessage is unavailable; incoming messages will not be rendered.")
	end

	function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility()
		ChatInstance:ToggleVisibility()
	end

	function moduleApiTable:SetVisible(visible)
		ChatInstance:SetVisible(visible)
	end

	function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBar()
		ChatInstance:FocusChatBar()
	end

	function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility()
		return ChatInstance.Visible
	end

	function moduleApiTable:GetMessageCount()
		return ChatInstance:GetCurrentWindowMessageCount()
	end

	function moduleApiTable:TopbarEnabledChanged(...)
		return ChatInstance:TopbarEnabledChanged(...)
	end

	function moduleApiTable:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
		return ChatInstance:IsFocused(useWasFocused)
	end

	function moduleApiTable:ClassicChatEnabled()
		return HasTextChatBackend()
	end

	function moduleApiTable:IsBubbleChatOnly()
		return IsBubbleChatOnly()
	end

	function moduleApiTable:IsDisabled()
		if ChatBackend and type(ChatBackend.IsDisabled) == "function" then
			local ok, disabled = pcall(function()
				return ChatBackend:IsDisabled()
			end)
			return ok and disabled == true
		end
		return not HasTextChatBackend()
	end

	moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged = ChatInstance.ChatBarFocusChanged
	moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged = ChatInstance.VisibilityStateChanged
	moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged = ChatInstance.CurrentWindowMessageCountChanged

end

                            <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    CoreScriptsRoot/StarterScript.lua

    Historical Roblox CoreScripts startup coordinator.

    Runtime:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui

    Expected hierarchy:
        NewRobloxGui (ScreenGui)
        ├── CoreScripts
        ├── Libraries
        ├── Modules
        ├── Sounds
        ├── LoadingScript
        └── StarterScript

    Responsibilities:
        - Require historical ModuleScript entry points.
        - Preserve the historical startup order.
        - Isolate errors so one failed module does not abort startup.
        - Initialize touch-specific and VR-specific components.

    This script does NOT:
        - Copy, clone, move, or destroy payload resources.
        - Create or replace NewRobloxGui.
        - Use ScriptContext:AddCoreScriptLocal.
        - Use executor-only APIs.
        - Force-enable or force-disable native CoreGui.

    The executor/bootstrap is responsible for installing the
    payload and requiring this module.
]]

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

-- ============================================================
-- ENVIRONMENT
-- ============================================================

assert(
	RunService:IsClient(),
	"[StarterScript] This module must run on the client."
)

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer

assert(
	LocalPlayer,
	"[StarterScript] Players.LocalPlayer is unavailable."
)

-- The executor must create/install this GUI before requiring
-- StarterScript.
local RobloxGui = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)

assert(
	RobloxGui,
	"[StarterScript] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui was not found."
)

assert(
	RobloxGui:IsA("ScreenGui"),
	"[StarterScript] NewRobloxGui must be a ScreenGui. "
		.. "Do not rename a Folder or ModuleScript to NewRobloxGui."
)

-- This module should itself be installed directly beneath the
-- runtime GUI root so every historical path remains predictable.
assert(
	script.Parent == RobloxGui,
	"[StarterScript] StarterScript must be a direct child of "
		.. "CoreGui.NewRobloxGui. Install the contents of "
		.. "CoreScriptsRoot under NewRobloxGui without an extra "
		.. "CoreScriptsRoot wrapper."
)

-- ============================================================
-- HISTORICAL RESOURCE ROOTS
-- These already exist. Never copy them from another location.
-- ============================================================

local CoreScripts = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("CoreScripts")
local Modules = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local Libraries = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Libraries")

assert(
	CoreScripts and CoreScripts:IsA("Folder"),
	"[StarterScript] NewRobloxGui.CoreScripts is missing."
)

assert(
	Modules and Modules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[StarterScript] NewRobloxGui.Modules is missing."
)

if not Libraries then
	warn("[StarterScript] Libraries folder is missing.")
end

-- ============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS
-- ============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[StarterScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnScript(message)
	warn("[StarterScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceback(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end

	return tostring(message)
end

-- ============================================================
-- SOUNDS
-- Preserve the historical Sounds folder convention.
-- This creates the folder only if it is absent. It copies nothing.
-- ============================================================

local soundFolder = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Sounds")

if not soundFolder then
	soundFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
	soundFolder.Name = "Sounds"
	soundFolder.Parent = RobloxGui
elseif not soundFolder:IsA("Folder") then
	warnScript("Sounds exists but is not a Folder.")
end

-- ============================================================
-- PATH RESOLUTION
-- Supports imported instance names with or without ".lua".
-- ============================================================

local function findPath(root, relativePath)
	if not root then
		return nil
	end

	local current = root

	for segment in string.gmatch(relativePath, "[^/]+") do
		local child = current:FindFirstChild(segment)

		if not child then
			child = current:FindFirstChild(segment .. ".lua")
		end

		if not child then
			local baseName = segment:gsub("%.lua$", "")

			for _, candidate in ipairs(current:GetChildren()) do
				local candidateName =
					candidate.Name:gsub("%.lua$", "")

				if candidateName == baseName then
					child = candidate
					break
				end
			end
		end

		if not child then
			return nil
		end

		current = child
	end

	return current
end

-- ============================================================
-- SAFE REQUIRE
-- Only ModuleScripts can be required.
-- A nil module return is valid if require itself succeeds.
-- ============================================================

local function safeRequire(moduleScript)
	if not moduleScript then
		return false, "ModuleScript was not found."
	end

	if not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local message = string.format(
			"Expected ModuleScript, got %s: %s",
			moduleScript.ClassName,
			moduleScript:GetFullName()
		)

		warnScript(message)
		return false, message
	end

	local success, result = xpcall(function()
		return require(moduleScript)
	end, traceback)

	if not success then
		warnScript(
			"Failed to require "
				.. moduleScript:GetFullName()
				.. ":\n"
				.. tostring(result)
		)

		return false, result
	end

	return true, result
end

local function safeRequirePath(root, relativePath)
	local target = findPath(root, relativePath)

	if not target then
		warnScript("Missing module: " .. relativePath)
		return false
	end

	local success = safeRequire(target)

	if success then
		log("Initialized " .. relativePath)
	end

	return success
end

-- ============================================================
-- CORESCRIPT ENTRY POINTS
--
-- Unlike AddCoreScriptLocal, this directly requires a
-- ModuleScript already installed in the historical tree.
--
-- LocalScripts cannot be started with require(). If an entry
-- point is a LocalScript, the bootstrap/execution host must
-- handle its lifecycle; this function will not clone it.
-- ============================================================

local function startCoreScript(relativePath)
	local entry = findPath(CoreScripts, relativePath)

	if not entry then
		warnScript(
			"CoreScript entry point missing: CoreScripts/"
				.. relativePath
		)

		return false
	end

	if entry:IsA("LocalScript") then
		warnScript(
			"Entry point CoreScripts/"
				.. relativePath
				.. " is a LocalScript, not a ModuleScript. "
				.. "It cannot be started with require(). "
				.. "Its lifecycle must be handled by the bootstrap."
		)

		return false
	end

	if not entry:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warnScript(
			"Unsupported entry point type for CoreScripts/"
				.. relativePath
				.. ": "
				.. entry.ClassName
		)

		return false
	end

	local success = safeRequire(entry)

	if success then
		log("Started CoreScripts/" .. relativePath)
	end

	return success
end

-- ============================================================
-- MAIN STARTUP ORDER
-- Kept aligned with the historical StarterScript.
-- ============================================================

log("Starting historical CoreScripts.")

-- Topbar
startCoreScript("Topbar")

-- Dialogs' Lua component
startCoreScript("MainBotChatScript2")

-- In-game notifications
startCoreScript("NotificationScript2")

-- Performance stats
startCoreScript("PerformanceStatsManagerScript")



-- Purchase prompts
startCoreScript("PurchasePromptScript2")

-- Block and friend prompts
startCoreScript("BlockPlayerPrompt")
startCoreScript("FriendPlayerPrompt")

-- Avatar context menu
startCoreScript("AvatarContextMenu")


-- Vehicle HUD
startCoreScript("VehicleHud")

-- Gamepad menu

if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
	startCoreScript("ContextActionTouch")

	task.spawn(function()
		local controlFrame = RobloxGui:WaitForChild(
			"ControlFrame",
			10
		)

		if not controlFrame then
			warnScript("ControlFrame was not found.")
			return
		end

		local bottomLeft = controlFrame:WaitForChild(
			"BottomLeftControl",
			10
		)

		if not bottomLeft then
			warnScript("BottomLeftControl was not found.")
			return
		end

		if bottomLeft:IsA("GuiObject") then
			bottomLeft.Visible = false
		end
	end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- VR
-- ============================================================

local vrInitialized = false
local vrConnection = nil

local function initializeVR()
	if not VRService.VREnabled or vrInitialized then
		return
	end

	vrInitialized = true

	safeRequirePath(Modules, "VR/VirtualKeyboard")
	safeRequirePath(Modules, "VR/UserGui")

	log("VR modules initialized.")
end

local vrSuccess, vrError = xpcall(function()
	initializeVR()

	vrConnection = VRService
		:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled")
		:Connect(function()
			if not VRService.VREnabled then
				return
			end

			local success, err = xpcall(
				initializeVR,
				traceback
			)

			if not success then
				warnScript(
					"VR initialization failed: " .. tostring(err)
				)
			end
		end)
end, traceback)

if not vrSuccess then
	warnScript(
		"Could not initialize VR monitoring: "
			.. tostring(vrError)
	)
end

-- RETURN
-- ============================================================

log("Startup sequence completed.")

return {
	RobloxGui = RobloxGui,
	Modules = Modules,
	Libraries = Libraries,

	Disconnect = function()
		if vrConnection then
			vrConnection:Disconnect()
			vrConnection = nil
		end
	end,
}</ProtectedString>
                            <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-445</int64>

                            <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    LoadingScript.lua
    Historical CoreScriptsRoot loading screen

    Reworked for ordinary client-side execution.

    Retained:
        - Dark gray loading background
        - Place thumbnail
        - Place name and creator name
        - Rotating Roblox loading image
        - Fade-out when the loader finishes

    Removed:
        - Feature flags and internal settings
        - CoreGui.RobloxGui dependencies
        - Internal CoreScript modules
        - Internal GuiService events
        - Xbox-only cancellation actions
        - Connection-health statistics
        - AssetService thumbnail polling
        - Infinite retry loops

    Usage:
        local LoadingScreen = require(LoadingScript)

        -- Initialize the remaining CoreScripts...

        LoadingScreen:Stop()

    Run from a client-side context.
]]

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ReplicatedFirst = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
local MarketplaceService = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local LoadingScreen = {}

-- ============================================================
-- CONFIGURATION
-- ============================================================

local BACKGROUND_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(45, 45, 45)
local TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

local SPINNER_IMAGE =
	"rbxasset://textures/loading/robloxTilt.png"

local MINIMUM_DISPLAY_TIME = 0.75
local FADE_DURATION = 0.35
local STARTUP_TIMEOUT = 90

local SPINNER_ROTATION_TIME = 2

-- ============================================================
-- STATE
-- ============================================================

local startedAt = os.clock()

local screenGui = nil
local backgroundFrame = nil
local spinnerImage = nil
local placeIcon = nil
local placeLabel = nil
local creatorLabel = nil

local renderConnection = nil

local stopping = false
local stopped = false

-- ============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS
-- ============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[LoadingScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnLoading(message)
	warn("[LoadingScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

-- ============================================================
-- INSTANCE CREATION
-- ============================================================

local function create(className, properties, parent)
	local object = Instance.new(className)

	for property, value in pairs(properties) do
		object[property] = value
	end

	object.Parent = parent

	return object
end

-- ============================================================
-- CONTROLLER
-- ============================================================

local function disconnectRendering()
	if renderConnection then
		renderConnection:Disconnect()
		renderConnection = nil
	end
end

local function destroyImmediately()
	disconnectRendering()

	if screenGui then
		screenGui:Destroy()
		screenGui = nil
	end

	backgroundFrame = nil
	spinnerImage = nil
	placeIcon = nil
	placeLabel = nil
	creatorLabel = nil

	stopped = true
end

function LoadingScreen:Stop(immediate)
	if stopping or stopped then
		return
	end

	stopping = true

	task.spawn(function()
		-- Prevent the GUI flashing away if initialization is fast.
		if not immediate then
			local elapsed = os.clock() - startedAt
			local remaining = MINIMUM_DISPLAY_TIME - elapsed

			if remaining &gt; 0 then
				task.wait(remaining)
			end
		end

		if immediate then
			destroyImmediately()
			log("Loading screen removed.")
			return
		end

		disconnectRendering()

		if not screenGui or not screenGui.Parent then
			destroyImmediately()
			return
		end

		local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(
			FADE_DURATION,
			Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
			Enum.EasingDirection.Out
		)

		-- Fade the background.
		if backgroundFrame and backgroundFrame.Parent then
			TweenService:Create(
				backgroundFrame,
				tweenInfo,
				{
					BackgroundTransparency = 1,
				}
			):Play()
		end

		-- Fade all visible descendants without any CoreGui APIs.
		for _, object in ipairs(screenGui:GetDescendants()) do
			local properties = {}

			if object:IsA("TextLabel") then
				properties.TextTransparency = 1
				properties.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				properties.BackgroundTransparency = 1

			elseif object:IsA("ImageLabel") then
				properties.ImageTransparency = 1
				properties.BackgroundTransparency = 1

			elseif object:IsA("Frame") then
				properties.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			end

			if next(properties) then
				local success, err = pcall(function()
					TweenService:Create(
						object,
						tweenInfo,
						properties
					):Play()
				end)

				if not success then
					warnLoading(
						"Could not fade "
							.. object:GetFullName()
							.. ": "
							.. tostring(err)
					)
				end
			end
		end

		task.wait(FADE_DURATION + 0.05)

		destroyImmediately()
		log("Loading screen removed.")
	end)
end

-- ============================================================
-- ENVIRONMENT VALIDATION
-- ============================================================

if not RunService:IsClient() then
	warnLoading(
		"This module requires a client-side execution context."
	)

	stopped = true
	return LoadingScreen
end

if not localPlayer then
	warnLoading(
		"Players.LocalPlayer is unavailable."
	)

	stopped = true
	return LoadingScreen
end

local playerGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)

if not playerGui then
	warnLoading(
		"PlayerGui did not become available within 10 seconds."
	)

	stopped = true
	return LoadingScreen
end

-- ============================================================

-- ============================================================

task.spawn(function()
	if placeId &lt;= 0 then
		return
	end

	local success, productInfo = pcall(function()
		return MarketplaceService:GetProductInfo(placeId)
	end)

	if not success then
		warnLoading(
			"Place metadata unavailable: "
				.. tostring(productInfo)
		)

		return
	end

	if stopping or stopped or not screenGui or not screenGui.Parent then
		return
	end

	if type(productInfo.Name) == "string"
		and productInfo.Name ~= "" then

		placeLabel.Text = productInfo.Name
	end

	local creator = productInfo.Creator

	if type(creator) == "table"
		and type(creator.Name) == "string"
		and creator.Name ~= "" then

		creatorLabel.Text = "By " .. creator.Name
	end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- SPINNER ANIMATION
-- ============================================================

local animationTime = 0

renderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
	if stopping or stopped then
		return
	end

	if not screenGui or not screenGui.Parent then
		disconnectRendering()
		return
	end

	if not spinnerImage or not spinnerImage.Parent then
		return
	end

	animationTime = animationTime + deltaTime

	local phase = (animationTime % SPINNER_ROTATION_TIME)
		/ SPINNER_ROTATION_TIME

	-- Smooth in/out cubic rotation, matching the old loading motion.
	local alpha

	if phase &lt; 0.5 then
		alpha = 4 * phase * phase * phase
	else
		local shifted = 2 * phase - 2

		alpha = 0.5 * shifted * shifted * shifted + 1
	end

	spinnerImage.Rotation = alpha * 360
end)

-- ============================================================
-- STARTUP FAILSAFE
-- ============================================================

task.delay(STARTUP_TIMEOUT, function()
	if not stopping and not stopped then
		warnLoading(
			"Startup timeout reached. Removing the loading screen "
				.. "to prevent it remaining visible indefinitely."
		)

		LoadingScreen:Stop()
	end
end)

log("Loading screen created.")

return LoadingScreen</ProtectedString>
                            <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-446</int64>
                            <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                        </Properties>
                    </Item>
                    <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX546">
                        <Properties>
                            <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                            <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                            <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                            <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                <null />
                            </ContentId>
                            <string name="Name">MergeSort</string>
                            <string name="ScriptGuid">{1B2F61E0-1686-4BB0-B517-6B93B1CF3F44}</string>
                            <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                            <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    MergeSort.lua
    Historical CoreScripts utility

    Bottom-up stable merge sort.

    Usage:
        local mergeSort = require(path.To.MergeSort)

        local sorted = mergeSort(items, function(a, b)
            return a &lt; b
        end)

    If no comparator is provided, elements are sorted in
    ascending order using the &lt; operator.

    The input table is not modified.
]]

local function bottomupmerge(comp, source, target, left, mid, right)
	local i = left
	local j = mid

	for k = left, right do
		if i &lt; mid and (j &gt; right or not comp(source[j], source[i])) then
			target[k] = source[i]
			i = i + 1
		else
			target[k] = source[j]
			j = j + 1
		end
	end
end

local function mergesort(arr, comp)
	assert(type(arr) == "table", "MergeSort expects an array table.")

	if comp == nil then
		comp = function(a, b)
			return a &lt; b
		end
	end

	assert(type(comp) == "function", "Comparator must be a function.")

	local n = #arr
	local source = {}
	local target = {}

	-- Copy the original array so sorting never changes its contents.
	for i = 1, n do
		source[i] = arr[i]
	end

	local width = 1

	while width &lt; n do
		for left = 1, n, width * 2 do
			-- mid is the first index of the right half.
			-- right is the final included index.
			local mid = math.min(left + width, n + 1)
			local right = math.min(left + width * 2 - 1, n)

			bottomupmerge(
				comp,
				source,
				target,
				left,
				mid,
				right
			)
		end

		source, target = target, source
		width = width * 2
	end

	return source
end

-- Return the sorting function itself.
-- Do NOT call mergesort(...) here: require() supplies no arguments.
return mergesort</ProtectedString>
                            <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-447</int64>
                            <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                        </Properties>
                    </Item>
                    <Item class="Script" referent="RBX547">
                        <Properties>
                            <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                            <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                            <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                            <bool name="Disabled">false</bool>
                            <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                <null />
                            </ContentId>
                            <string name="Name">ServerStarterScript</string>
                            <token name="RunContext">0</token>
                            <string name="ScriptGuid">{2D542B24-DFBB-4302-95C2-81296542E9A8}</string>
                            <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                            <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: ServerStarterScript.lua
    Version: 1.1.0
    Description:
        Patched historical CoreScripts server startup.

    Requirements:
        - Must execute in a server context.
        - May be a ModuleScript required by a server Script.
        - Server-side source must be accessible to the server.

    Removed:
        - ScriptContext:AddCoreScriptLocal
        - Internal RobloxReplicatedStorage dependency
        - Internal feature flag access
        - Internal CoreGui.RobloxGui module dependencies
        - Obsolete UserSettings feature checks
]]

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

-- ============================================================
-- ENVIRONMENT
-- ============================================================

if not RunService:IsServer() then
	warn(
		"[ServerStarterScript] Server-only initialization skipped. "
			.. "This module must be required from a server context."
	)

	return {
		Started = false,
		Reason = "Not running on the server",
	}
end

-- ============================================================
-- SERVICES
-- ============================================================

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

-- ============================================================
-- DIAGNOSTICS
-- ============================================================

local function log(message)
	print("[ServerStarterScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function warnScript(message)
	warn("[ServerStarterScript] " .. tostring(message))
end

local function traceback(message)
	if debug and debug.traceback then
		return debug.traceback(tostring(message), 2)
	end

	return tostring(message)
end

-- ============================================================
-- CREATE OR FIND A SHARED REMOTE
--
-- Standard RemoteEvents and RemoteFunctions are placed in
-- ReplicatedStorage so the server and clients can access them.
-- ============================================================

local function getOrCreateRemote(name, className)
	local existing = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)

	if existing then
		if existing.ClassName ~= className then
			warnScript(
				"Cannot initialize "
					.. name
					.. ": expected "
					.. className
					.. ", found "
					.. existing.ClassName
			)

			return nil
		end

		return existing
	end

	local remote = Instance.new(className)
	remote.Name = name
	remote.Parent = ReplicatedStorage

	return remote
end

-- ============================================================
-- REMOTE EVENTS
-- ============================================================

local RemoteEvent_SetDialogInUse =
	getOrCreateRemote("SetDialogInUse", "RemoteEvent")

local RemoteFunction_GetServerVersion =
	getOrCreateRemote("GetServerVersion", "RemoteFunction")

-- ============================================================
-- DIALOG COMPATIBILITY
-- ============================================================

-- Modern Roblox manages Dialog usage itself. The old
-- Dialog:SetPlayerIsUsing API is no longer available through
-- the documented Dialog API.
--
-- Retain the historical RemoteEvent name for compatibility,
-- but only invoke the legacy method when it actually exists.
-- Otherwise, report the limitation instead of crashing.

local playerDialogMap = {}
local warnedLegacyDialogAPI = false

local function setDialogInUse(player, dialog, value, waitTime)
	-- RemoteEvent supplies the real requesting Player.
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance"
		or not player:IsA("Player")
		or player.Parent ~= Players then
		return
	end

	if typeof(dialog) ~= "Instance"
		or not dialog:IsA("Dialog") then
		return
	end

	-- The requested dialog must belong to the server's world.
	if not dialog:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
		return
	end

	if type(value) ~= "boolean" then
		return
	end

	if waitTime ~= nil then
		if type(waitTime) ~= "number"
			or waitTime ~= waitTime
			or math.abs(waitTime) == math.huge then
			return
		end
	else
		waitTime = 0
	end

	-- Avoid unbounded client-requested delays.
	waitTime = math.clamp(waitTime, 0, 5)

	local function apply()
		-- Revalidate everything after a possible delay.
		if player.Parent ~= Players then
			return
		end

		if not dialog:IsDescendantOf(Workspace) then
			return
		end

		local success, err = pcall(function()
			dialog:SetPlayerIsUsing(player, value)
		end)

		if success then
			playerDialogMap[player] = value and dialog or nil
			return
		end

		if not warnedLegacyDialogAPI then
			warnedLegacyDialogAPI = true

			warnScript(
				"The historical Dialog:SetPlayerIsUsing API "
					.. "is unavailable. Dialog usage is managed by "
					.. "the modern Roblox engine; legacy usage requests "
					.. "cannot change its read-only InUse state. "
					.. "First error: "
					.. tostring(err)
			)
		end
	end

	if waitTime &gt; 0 then
		task.delay(waitTime, apply)
	else
		apply()
	end
end

if RemoteEvent_SetDialogInUse then
	RemoteEvent_SetDialogInUse.OnServerEvent:Connect(
		setDialogInUse
	)
end

-- ============================================================
-- SERVER VERSION
-- ============================================================

local function getServerVersion()
	if RunService:IsStudio() then
		return "ROBLOX Studio"
	end

	local success, rawVersion = pcall(function()
		return version()
	end)

	if success
		and type(rawVersion) == "string"
		and rawVersion ~= "" then
		return rawVersion
	end

	return "UNKNOWN_SERVER"
end

if RemoteFunction_GetServerVersion then
	RemoteFunction_GetServerVersion.OnServerInvoke = function(_player)
		return getServerVersion()
	end
end

-- ============================================================
-- PLAYER CLEANUP
-- ============================================================

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	local dialog = playerDialogMap[player]

	if dialog then
		pcall(function()
			dialog:SetPlayerIsUsing(player, false)
		end)

		playerDialogMap[player] = nil
	end
end)

-- ============================================================
-- HISTORICAL SERVER CORESCRIPT
-- ============================================================

-- The original implementation used the internal
-- ScriptContext:AddCoreScriptLocal API to launch ServerSocialScript.
-- That API isn't available for normal scripts.
--
-- If ServerSocialScript has already been installed as a normal
-- server Script in the server's runtime hierarchy, let the
-- engine execute it naturally.
--
-- If it is a ModuleScript, it can be explicitly required here.

local function startServerSocialModule()
	local root = script.Parent
	local serverCoreScripts = root:FindFirstChild("ServerCoreScripts")

	if not serverCoreScripts then
		warnScript(
			"ServerCoreScripts folder not found beside "
				.. "ServerStarterScript. ServerSocialScript was not "
				.. "explicitly initialized."
		)

		return
	end

	local socialScript =
		serverCoreScripts:FindFirstChild("ServerSocialScript")

	if not socialScript then
		warnScript("ServerSocialScript was not found.")
		return
	end

	if socialScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		local success, err = xpcall(function()
			require(socialScript)
		end, traceback)

		if not success then
			warnScript(
				"ServerSocialScript failed: " .. tostring(err)
			)
		else
			log("ServerSocialScript module initialized.")
		end

	elseif socialScript:IsA("Script") then
		-- Don't clone or re-execute an ordinary Script.
		-- Its parent and RunContext determine its lifecycle.
		log(
			"ServerSocialScript is a Script. "
				.. "Its execution is managed by Roblox."
		)

	else
		warnScript(
			"Unsupported ServerSocialScript type: "
				.. socialScript.ClassName
		)
	end
end

startServerSocialModule()

local GuiRoot = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)

local topbarEnabled = true
local settingsActive = false
local canChat = true
local syncYekuChatHotkey
local nativeChatEnabled = true
local nativeBackpackEnabled = true
local nativePlayerListEnabled = true
local nativeHealthEnabled = true
-- Only CoreGuiChangedSignal(Enum.CoreGuiType.All, ...) controls this mode.
-- Individual CoreGui flags never imply that the aggregate All switch was used.
local coreGuiAllState = true
local allCoreGuiDisabled = false

local function coreGuiChatAllowed()
    local ok, enabled = pcall(function()
        return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
    end)
    if ok and type(enabled) == "boolean" then
        nativeChatEnabled = enabled
    end
    return nativeChatEnabled == true and not allCoreGuiDisabled
end
local function customChatEnabled()
    -- The loader suppresses Roblox's native Chat CoreGui while keeping Yeku's
    -- replacement Chat.lua active. Only this explicit attribute disables Yeku.
    return GuiRoot:GetAttribute("YekuCustomChatEnabled") ~= false
end

local function log(message) print("[Topbar] " .. tostring(message)) end
local function warnTopbar(message) warn("[Topbar] " .. tostring(message)) end

local function safeRequire(path, waitTimeout, quiet)
	local current = Modules
	local deadline = waitTimeout and (os.clock() + waitTimeout) or nil
	for segment in string.gmatch(path, "[^/]+") do
		local child = current and current:FindFirstChild(segment)
		if not child and current and deadline then
			local remaining = deadline - os.clock()
			if remaining &gt; 0 then
				child = current:WaitForChild(segment, remaining)
			end
		end
		current = child
		if not current then
			if not quiet then warnTopbar("Missing module path Modules/" .. path) end
			return nil
		end
	end
	if not current:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		if not quiet then warnTopbar(path .. " is not a ModuleScript") end
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(require, current)
	if not ok then
		if not quiet then warnTopbar("Failed requiring " .. path .. ": " .. tostring(result)) end
		return nil
	end
	return result
end

local function connectSignal(signal, callback)
	if not signal then return nil end
	if typeof(signal) == "Instance" and signal:IsA("BindableEvent") then signal = signal.Event end
	local ok, connection = pcall(function()
		if signal.Connect then return signal:Connect(callback) end
		if signal.connect then return signal:connect(callback) end
		error("Signal does not expose Connect/connect")
	end)
	return ok and connection or nil
end

local function disconnect(connection)
	if connection then pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end) end
end

local GameTranslator = safeRequire("GameTranslator")
-- Do not require ChatSelector here: it asynchronously boots NewChat and routes UI
-- commands into the registered ChatScript frontend. This topbar controls the
-- existing Yeku UI directly through Modules.Chat instead.
-- Load Modules.Chat asynchronously. It constructs the legacy chat window
-- and can yield while its GUI/backend dependencies initialize; the topbar
-- itself must not wait for that work before becoming visible.
local ChatModule = nil
local PlayerlistModule = nil
local PlayerlistModuleInitialized = false
local BackpackModule = nil
local SettingsModule = nil

local function isTouchDevice() return UserInputService.TouchEnabled end

	local ok, result = pcall(function() return GuiService:IsTenFootInterface() end)
	return ok and result == true
end

local function smallTouchScreen()
	if not isTouchDevice() then return false end
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then return false end
	local size = camera.ViewportSize
	return size.X &lt; 700 or size.Y &lt; 500
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT API COMPATIBILITY
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function chatCall(name, ...)
	if not ChatModule then return false, "Modules.Chat is unavailable" end
	local method = ChatModule[name]
	if type(method) ~= "function" then return false, "Chat API method " .. name .. " is missing" end
	local args = table.pack(...)
	return pcall(function() return method(ChatModule, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n)) end)
end

local function invokeChatbarFocusVisual()
    -- Always call the live Chat module first. The GuiRoot BindableFunction can
    -- survive a module reload and point at a stale, hidden ChatBar.
    local apiOK, apiResult = chatCall("ChatbarFocusVisual")
    if apiOK and apiResult == true then return true end

    -- Compatibility fallback for older Chat modules that expose only the bridge.
    local folder = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild("LegacyChatFrontendConnections")
    local bridge = folder and folder:FindFirstChild("ChatbarFocusVisual")
    if bridge and bridge:IsA("BindableFunction") then
        local bridgeOK, bridgeResult = pcall(function() return bridge:Invoke() end)
        if bridgeOK and bridgeResult == true then return true end
    end
    return false
end

local function findYekuChatGui()
    -- Resolve from the currently active Chat module first. A previous GUI name
    -- lookup can return a hidden duplicate left behind by a module reload.
    local ok, ownedGui = chatCall("GetChatGui")
    if ok and typeof(ownedGui) == "Instance" and ownedGui:IsA("ScreenGui")
        and ownedGui.Parent ~= nil then
        return ownedGui
    end
    local coreGuiChat = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("YekuLegacyChatGui")
    if coreGuiChat and coreGuiChat:IsA("ScreenGui") and coreGuiChat.Enabled then
        return coreGuiChat
    end
    local playerGui = Player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local gui = playerGui and (playerGui:FindFirstChild("YekuLegacyChatGui") or playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat"))
    if gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then return gui end
    return coreGuiChat
end

local function findYekuChatTextBox()
    -- Trust the TextBox returned by the live Chat module. Requiring it to be a
    -- descendant of a separately resolved ScreenGui can reject the active box
    -- after the 2015/2016 frontend has reparented it during a visibility change.
    local apiOK, ownedTextBox = chatCall("GetChatBarTextBox")
    if apiOK and typeof(ownedTextBox) == "Instance"
        and ownedTextBox:IsA("TextBox") and ownedTextBox.Parent ~= nil then
        return ownedTextBox
    end

    local chatGui = findYekuChatGui()
    local textBox = chatGui and chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatBar", true)
    if textBox and textBox:IsA("TextBox") and textBox.Parent ~= nil then
        return textBox
    end
    return nil
end

local function isLegacyChatTextBox(textBox)
	if not textBox or not textBox:IsA("TextBox") then return false end
	if textBox.Name ~= "ChatBar" then return false end
	local current = textBox.Parent
	while current do
		if current.Name == "Chat" or current.Name == "ChatWindow"
			or current.Name == "YekuLegacyChatGui" or current.Name == "NewRobloxGui" then
			return true
		end
		current = current.Parent
	end
	return false
end

local function chatVisible()
	-- Chat.lua owns the logical visibility state. Read it before the live
	-- ChatWindow.Visible property, which remains true during the 0.16s fade.
	if ChatModule then
		local stateOK, state = chatCall("GetVisibility")
		if stateOK and type(state) == "boolean" then return state end
	end
	local gui = findYekuChatGui()
	if gui then
		if not gui.Enabled then return false end
		local bubbleOnly = false
		if ChatModule then
			local ok, result = chatCall("IsBubbleChatOnly")
			bubbleOnly = ok and result == true
		end
		if bubbleOnly then
			local barFrame = gui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarOverlay", true)
			return barFrame ~= nil and barFrame:IsA("GuiObject") and barFrame.Visible
		end
		local window = gui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
		if window and window:IsA("GuiObject") then return window.Visible end
		return true
	end
	if not ChatModule then return false end
	local ok, result = chatCall("GetVisibility")
	if ok and type(result) == "boolean" then return result end
	if type(ChatModule.Visible) == "boolean" then return ChatModule.Visible end
	return false
end

local function chatDisabled()
	if not ChatModule then return true end
	local ok, result = chatCall("IsDisabled")
	if ok and type(result) == "boolean" then return result end
	return false
end

local function isChatBarActuallyFocused(textBox)
    if not textBox or not textBox:IsA("TextBox") then return false end
    local focused = false
    pcall(function() focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox end)
    if not focused then pcall(function() focused = textBox:IsFocused() end) end
    if not focused or not textBox.Active or not textBox.TextEditable then return false end
    local current = textBox
    while current do
        if current:IsA("GuiObject") and not current.Visible then return false end
        if current:IsA("ScreenGui") and not current.Enabled then return false end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    return true
end

local function isChatBarVisuallyFocused(textBox)
    if not isChatBarActuallyFocused(textBox) then return false end
    -- On touch, the desktop ChatBar is deliberately kept transparent/offscreen.
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then return true end
    local textVisible = false
    pcall(function() textVisible = textBox.TextTransparency &lt;= 0.05 end)
    if not textVisible then return false end
    local chatGui = findYekuChatGui()
    local background = chatGui and chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarInnerBackground", true)
    if background and background:IsA("GuiObject") then
        local backgroundVisible = false
        pcall(function() backgroundVisible = background.BackgroundTransparency &lt;= 0.1 end)
        if not backgroundVisible then return false end
    end
    return true
end

local function canRouteSlash()
	if not customChatEnabled() or not coreGuiChatAllowed() then return false end
	-- ChatMain may be inserted after Topbar; allow the hotkey to bind while it loads.
	return ChatModule == nil or not chatDisabled()
end

local function chatBubbleOnly()
	if not ChatModule then return false end
	local ok, result = chatCall("IsBubbleChatOnly")
	if ok and type(result) == "boolean" then return result end
	return ChatModule.BubbleChatOnly == true
end

local function chatHistoryVisible()
	if not ChatModule then return true end
	local ok, result = chatCall("IsChatHistoryVisible")
	if ok and type(result) == "boolean" then return result end
	return true
end

-- Visibility can be propagated by older ChatSelector implementations on a
-- later frame. Wait for the requested state before invoking the fallback API,
-- otherwise a fast fallback toggle can undo a successful SetVisible call.
local function waitForChatVisibility(expected, frameCount)
	for _ = 1, frameCount or 2 do
		if chatVisible() == expected then
			return true
		end
		RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
	end
	return chatVisible() == expected
end

local function chatSetVisible(visible)
	if not ChatModule then return false end
	visible = visible == true

	if visible and (not customChatEnabled() or not coreGuiChatAllowed()) then
		local textBox = findYekuChatTextBox()
		if textBox and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox then
			pcall(function() textBox:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
		end
		pcall(function() chatCall("SetVisible", false) end)
		local chatGui = findYekuChatGui()
		if chatGui then
			chatGui.Enabled = false
			local window = chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
			local bar = chatGui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarOverlay", true)
			if window then window.Visible = false end
			if bar then bar.Visible = false end
		end
		return false
	end

	local before = chatVisible()
	if before == visible then return true end

	local setOk, setResult = chatCall("SetVisible", visible)
	if setOk and waitForChatVisibility(visible, 2) then
		return true
	end

	-- Only use ToggleVisibility if SetVisible left the state unchanged.
	-- Never issue a second toggle once the requested state has been reached.
	if chatVisible() == before then
		local toggleOk, toggleResult = chatCall("ToggleVisibility")
		if not toggleOk then
			warnTopbar("ToggleVisibility failed: " .. tostring(toggleResult))
			return false
		end
		if waitForChatVisibility(visible, 2) then
			return true
		end
	end

	warnTopbar(
		"Could not set chat visibility to " .. tostring(visible)
			.. " (before=" .. tostring(before)
			.. ", after=" .. tostring(chatVisible())
			.. ", SetVisible=" .. tostring(setOk)
			.. ", result=" .. tostring(setResult) .. ")"
	)
	return chatVisible() == visible
end

local updateChatIcon, updateUnread

local function chatFocus()
	if not canRouteSlash() then
		return false, "chat is disabled by the topbar/CoreGui state"
	end

	-- Chat.lua owns the atomic operation: it opens the window, restores the
	-- focused ChatBar visuals, and captures the real TextBox in one route.
	-- This is essential when slash is pressed while the window is closed.
	local combinedOK, combinedResult = chatCall("FocusChatBar")
	if combinedOK and combinedResult == true then
		updateChatIcon()
		updateUnread()
		return true
	end
	local reason = combinedOK and ("FocusChatBar returned " .. tostring(combinedResult))
		or ("FocusChatBar bridge failed: " .. tostring(combinedResult))
	local opened = chatSetVisible(true)
	if not opened then
		-- Retry the authoritative Chat.lua API once before treating this as failure.
		local retryOK, retryResult = chatCall("SetVisible", true)
		opened = retryOK
		if not opened then reason = tostring(retryResult) end
	end
	local liveGui = findYekuChatGui()
	local liveWindow = liveGui and liveGui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
	local liveBarFrame = liveGui and liveGui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarOverlay", true)
	if liveGui and opened then
		pcall(function()
			liveGui.Enabled = true
			if not chatBubbleOnly() and liveWindow and liveWindow:IsA("GuiObject") then
				liveWindow.Visible = chatHistoryVisible()
			end
			if liveBarFrame and liveBarFrame:IsA("GuiObject") then liveBarFrame.Visible = true end
		end)
	end
	if not opened or not waitForChatVisibility(true, 2.5) then
		return false, "Chat.lua SetVisible(true) did not open the live ChatWindow"
	end

	local focused = false
	for attempt = 1, 8 do
		local folder = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild("LegacyChatFrontendConnections")
		local visualFunction = folder and folder:FindFirstChild("ChatbarFocusVisual")
		if visualFunction and visualFunction:IsA("BindableFunction") then
			local ok, result = pcall(function() return visualFunction:Invoke() end)
			if ok and result == true then
				focused = true
			else
				reason = ok and ("ChatbarFocusVisual returned " .. tostring(result))
					or ("ChatbarFocusVisual bridge failed: " .. tostring(result))
			end
			break
		end
		if attempt &lt; 8 then RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() end
	end

	-- Support a Chat.lua that was already running when this updated Topbar was
	-- inserted, then fall back to its older combined focus function.
	if not focused then
		local apiOK, apiResult = chatCall("ChatbarFocusVisual")
		if apiOK and apiResult == true then
			focused = true
		else
			local combinedOK, combinedResult = chatCall("FocusChatBar")
			if combinedOK and combinedResult == true then
				focused = true
			else
				local oldFolder = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild("LegacyChatFrontendConnections")
				local oldBridge = oldFolder and oldFolder:FindFirstChild("FocusChatBarVisible")
			if oldBridge and oldBridge:IsA("BindableFunction") then
				local oldOK, oldResult = pcall(function() return oldBridge:Invoke() end)
				focused = oldOK and oldResult == true
				if not focused then reason = tostring(oldResult) end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if not focused then
		-- Last-resort live GUI path; never type the slash into the TextBox.
		local gui = findYekuChatGui()
		local window = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
		local barFrame = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarOverlay", true)
		local target = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatBar", true)
		if gui and window and barFrame and target and target:IsA("TextBox") then
			pcall(function()
				gui.Enabled = true
				window.Visible = not chatBubbleOnly() and chatHistoryVisible()
				barFrame.Visible = true
				target.Visible = true
				target.Active = true
				local prompt = gui:FindFirstChild("ClickToChat", true)
				if prompt then prompt.Visible = false end
				local inner = gui:FindFirstChild("InnerBackground", true)
				if inner and inner:IsA("GuiObject") then inner.BackgroundTransparency = 0 end
			end)
			for _ = 1, 8 do
				pcall(function() target:CaptureFocus() end)
				RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
				if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == target then
					focused = true
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local gui = findYekuChatGui()
	local window = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
	local target = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatBar", true)
	local barFrame = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatBarOverlay", true)
	local visible = gui and gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled
		and window and window:IsA("GuiObject") and window.Visible == (not chatBubbleOnly() and chatHistoryVisible())
		and barFrame and barFrame:IsA("GuiObject") and barFrame.Visible
	local ownsFocus = target and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == target
	if focused and visible and ownsFocus then
		updateChatIcon()
		updateUnread()
		return true
	end

	warnTopbar("ChatbarFocusVisual failed: visible=" .. tostring(visible)
		.. ", textboxFocused=" .. tostring(ownsFocus) .. ", reason=" .. tostring(reason))
	return false, reason or "focused ChatBar did not become visible and active"
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TOPBAR ROOT / CONTROL EVENTS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function getOrCreateBindable(name, className)
	local found = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild(name)
	if found then
		if found.ClassName == className then return found end
		warnTopbar(name .. " exists with class " .. found.ClassName .. ", expected " .. className)
		return nil
	end
	local object = Instance.new(className)
	object.Name = name
	object.Parent = GuiRoot
	return object
end

local SetTopbarEnabledEvent = getOrCreateBindable("SetTopbarEnabled", "BindableEvent")
local GetTopbarEnabledFunction = getOrCreateBindable("GetTopbarEnabled", "BindableFunction")
local topbarEnabledChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

local oldTopbar = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild("TopBarContainer")
if oldTopbar then oldTopbar:Destroy() end

local topbar = Instance.new("Frame")
topbar.Name = "TopBarContainer"
topbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT)
topbar.BackgroundTransparency = TOPBAR_TRANSLUCENT
topbar.BackgroundColor3 = TOPBAR_COLOR
topbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
topbar.Active = true
topbar.ZIndex = 10
topbar.Parent = GuiRoot

local function updateTopbarPosition()
	local ignoreInset = false
	pcall(function() ignoreInset = GuiRoot.IgnoreGuiInset end)

	-- When the ordinary topbar is disabled, Yeku intentionally leaves the
	-- hamburger button visible. The global inset is zero in that state, so the
	-- transparent menu-only bar must sit at the top of the screen, not at -36.
	local useZeroInset = ignoreInset or not topbarEnabled or allCoreGuiDisabled
		or VRService.VREnabled or TEN_FOOT
	topbar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, useZeroInset and 0 or -TOPBAR_HEIGHT)
end
updateTopbarPosition()
GuiRoot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("IgnoreGuiInset"):Connect(function()
	updateTopbarPosition()
end)

shadow.Name = "TopBarShadow"
shadow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 3)
shadow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)

shadow.Visible = false

local function addLayout(parent, alignment)
	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
	layout.HorizontalAlignment = alignment
	layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
	layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, ITEM_SPACING)
	layout.Parent = parent
end
addLayout(left, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left)
addLayout(right, Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right)

local function makeButton(parent, name, image, order)
	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = name
	button.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT)
	button.LayoutOrder = order
	button.Image = ""
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.ZIndex = 12
	button.Parent = parent

	local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	icon.Name = name .. "Icon"
	icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28)
	icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0.5, -14)
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.BorderSizePixel = 0
	icon.Image = image or ""
	icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
	icon.ZIndex = 13
	icon.Parent = button
	return button, icon
end

local function setButtonShown(button, shown)
	if not button then return end
	button.Visible = shown == true
	if shown then
		if not button.Parent then button.Parent = left end
	elseif button.Parent then
		button.Parent = nil
	end
end

local function translate(text)
	-- 2015-2017 are intentionally English-only.
	return text
end

local function computeTransparency()
	-- Enum.CoreGuiType.All=false makes the background completely transparent;
	-- it does not alter the per-button CoreGui flags or infer All from one flag.
	if not topbarEnabled or allCoreGuiDisabled or VRService.VREnabled then return 1 end
	local playerGui = Player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
	if playerGui then
		local ok, value = pcall(function() return playerGui:GetTopbarTransparency() end)
		if ok and type(value) == "number" then return math.clamp(value, 0, 1) end
	end
	return TOPBAR_TRANSLUCENT
end

local function updateTransparency()
	local inVR = VRService.VREnabled
	local controlsCanRender = topbarEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled and not inVR

	-- Keep the root and LeftFrame alive in menu-only mode. updateCoreGuiState()
	-- removes every optional button, leaving only the Settings/hamburger button.
	-- Hiding LeftFrame here used to hide the hamburger along with every other icon.
	topbar.Visible = not inVR
	if allCoreGuiDisabled or not topbarEnabled or inVR then
		topbar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	else
		topbar.BackgroundTransparency = settingsActive and TOPBAR_OPAQUE or computeTransparency()
	end

	shadow.Visible = controlsCanRender and topbar.BackgroundTransparency == 0
	if left then left.Visible = not inVR end
	if right then right.Visible = controlsCanRender end
end

local function updateInset()
	local hideInset = VRService.VREnabled or TEN_FOOT or not topbarEnabled or allCoreGuiDisabled
	if hideInset then
		pcall(function() GuiService:SetGlobalGuiInset(0, 0, 0, 0) end)
	else
		pcall(function() GuiService:SetGlobalGuiInset(0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT, 0, 0) end)
	end
	local event = GuiRoot:FindFirstChild("GuiInsetChanged")
	if event and event:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		pcall(function()
			if hideInset then event:Fire(0, 0, 0, 0)
			else event:Fire(0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT, 0, 0) end
		end)
	end

	-- Keep the transparent menu-only topbar aligned after SetGlobalGuiInset changes.
	updateTopbarPosition()
end

		warnTopbar("SettingsHub is unavailable")
		return

	if not ok then warnTopbar("Settings toggle failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
end)

	connectSignal(SettingsModule.SettingsShowSignal, function(active)
		settingsActive = active == true
		updateSettingsIcon()
		updateTransparency()
	end)
end

-- CHAT BUTTONS (independent focus and visibility states)

local selectedYekuYear = tonumber(GuiRoot:GetAttribute("YekuSelectedYear"))
local is2015Mobile = isTouchDevice() and selectedYekuYear == 2015
local chatButton, chatIcon = makeButton(left, "Chat", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/Chat.png", 4)
local unreadBadge = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
unreadBadge.Name = "ChatCounter"
unreadBadge.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18)
unreadBadge.Position = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, -4)
unreadBadge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
unreadBadge.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/MessageCounter.png"
unreadBadge.Visible = false
unreadBadge.ZIndex = 14
unreadBadge.Parent = chatIcon
local unreadText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
unreadText.Name = "ChatCounterText"
unreadText.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 9)
unreadText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -7, 0.5, -7)
unreadText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
unreadText.BorderSizePixel = 0
unreadText.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
unreadText.TextSize = 12
unreadText.TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR
unreadText.Text = ""
unreadText.ZIndex = 15
unreadText.Parent = unreadBadge

local mobileChatButton, mobileChatIcon
if isTouchDevice() then
	mobileChatButton, mobileChatIcon = makeButton(left, "ChatVisible", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/ToggleChat.png", 3)
end
if is2015Mobile then
	-- The 2015 mobile topbar used larger, more generously spaced chat controls.
	chatButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT)
	chatIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 36)
	chatIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0.5, -18)
	if mobileChatButton then mobileChatButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, TOPBAR_HEIGHT) end
	if mobileChatIcon then
		mobileChatIcon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 36)
		mobileChatIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0.5, -18)
	end
	unreadBadge.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20)
	unreadText.TextSize = 13
end

-- The two mobile chat buttons represent different states:
--   Chat.png / ChatDown.png       = the chat input is actually focused.
--   ToggleChat.png / ToggleChatDown.png = the chat window is visible.
-- Opening the window must never light the focus/ellipsis icon by itself.
local function chatBarIsFocused()
	local textBox = findYekuChatTextBox()
	if textBox then
		local focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox
		if not focused then
			local ok, value = pcall(function() return textBox:IsFocused() end)
			focused = ok and value == true
		end
		if focused then return true end
	end
	-- Chat.lua also maintains its own focus flag. Read it as a second source
	-- so the pressed-chat artwork updates on the same slash action, even when
	-- the executor's GetFocusedTextBox() snapshot is one input frame behind.
	if ChatModule then
		local ok, value = chatCall("IsFocused")
		if ok and value == true then return true end
	end
	return false
end

updateChatIcon = function()
	local focused = chatBarIsFocused()
	local visible = chatVisible()

	-- The desktop layout has one Chat button; it is active whenever the chat
	-- window is open, even when the input is not focused. Touch layouts keep the
	-- two states independent: ellipsis=input focus, ToggleChat=window visibility.
	local primaryChatActive
	if mobileChatButton then
		primaryChatActive = focused
	else
		primaryChatActive = visible
	end
	chatIcon.Image = primaryChatActive
		and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/ChatDown.png"
		or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/Chat.png"
	if mobileChatIcon then
		mobileChatIcon.Image = visible
			and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/ToggleChatDown.png"
			or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/ToggleChat.png"
	end
end

local lastMessageCount = 0
local lastToggleTime = 0
updateUnread = function(_, value)
	local count = value
	if type(count) ~= "number" and ChatModule then
		local ok, result = chatCall("GetMessageCount")
		if ok and type(result) == "number" then count = result end
	end
	if type(count) ~= "number" then return end
	if chatVisible() then
		lastMessageCount = count
		unreadBadge.Visible = false
		unreadText.Text = ""
		return
	end
	local unread = math.max(0, count - lastMessageCount)
	unreadBadge.Visible = unread &gt; 0
	unreadText.Text = unread == 0 and "" or unread &lt; 100 and tostring(unread) or "!"
end

local function toggleChat()
	if not customChatEnabled() or not nativeChatEnabled or allCoreGuiDisabled
		or not ChatModule or chatDisabled() then
		return
	end

	local desired = not chatVisible()
	-- Use SetVisible(desired), not ToggleVisibility followed by a second
	-- fallback. If ChatWindow.Visible and PlayerGui.Chat.Enabled ever disagree,
	-- blindly toggling the logical flag can close the window we meant to open.
	local changed = chatSetVisible(desired)
	local actual = chatVisible()
	updateChatIcon(actual)
	updateUnread()
	if not changed or actual ~= desired then
		warnTopbar("Chat button failed to set visibility (wanted="
			.. tostring(desired) .. ", actual=" .. tostring(actual) .. ")")
		return
	end

end
local function activateChatButton()
	if isTouchDevice() then
		-- Chat.png is the mobile ellipsis: open/focus, never toggle closed.
		local focused, focusReason = chatFocus()
		if not focused then warnTopbar("Mobile ellipsis focus failed: " .. tostring(focusReason)) end
		updateChatIcon()
		updateUnread()
		return
	end
	-- Desktop has one Chat button, so it remains a visibility toggle.
	toggleChat()
end
chatButton.Activated:Connect(activateChatButton)
if mobileChatButton then
	mobileChatButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if not customChatEnabled() or not ChatModule then return end
		-- ToggleChat.png is visibility-only; it never captures focus.
		toggleChat()
	end)
end

local chatModuleSignalsConnected = false
local function connectChatModuleSignals()
	if not ChatModule or chatModuleSignalsConnected then return end
	chatModuleSignalsConnected = true
	connectSignal(ChatModule.VisibilityStateChanged, function(visible)
		updateChatIcon(visible)
		updateUnread()
	end)
	connectSignal(ChatModule.MessagesChanged, updateUnread)
	connectSignal(ChatModule.ChatBarFocusChanged, function()
		-- Re-read the actual focused TextBox. Window visibility is not focus.
		task.defer(updateChatIcon)
	end)
	connectSignal(ChatModule.ChatDisabled, function()
		canChat = false
		if syncYekuChatHotkey then syncYekuChatHotkey() end
		setButtonShown(chatButton, false)
		if mobileChatButton then setButtonShown(mobileChatButton, false) end
	end)
	updateChatIcon()
	updateUnread()
end
connectChatModuleSignals()

-- Track the actual Chat.lua ScreenGui/window as a second, authoritative signal.
-- This covers direct SetVisible/UI updates and makes the desktop Chat button's
-- highlight follow the open window instead of requiring a TextBox focus event.
local watchedChatGui
local watchedChatWindow
local chatUiStateConnections = {}
local function clearChatUiStateConnections()
	for _, connection in ipairs(chatUiStateConnections) do
		pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
	end
	table.clear(chatUiStateConnections)
end
local function watchChatUiVisibility()
	local gui = findYekuChatGui()
	local window = gui and gui:FindFirstChild("ChatWindow", true)
	if gui == watchedChatGui and window == watchedChatWindow then
		updateChatIcon()
		return
	end
	clearChatUiStateConnections()
	watchedChatGui = gui
	watchedChatWindow = window
	if gui then
		table.insert(chatUiStateConnections, gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
			task.defer(updateChatIcon)
			updateUnread()
		end))
	end
	if window and window:IsA("GuiObject") then
		table.insert(chatUiStateConnections, window:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
			task.defer(updateChatIcon)
			updateUnread()
		end))
	end
	updateChatIcon()
end
watchChatUiVisibility()
CoreGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
	if descendant.Name == "YekuLegacyChatGui"
		or descendant.Name == "ChatWindow"
		or descendant.Name == "ChatBar" then
		task.defer(watchChatUiVisibility)
	end
end)

-- Also track focus changes that occur outside the ChatMain signal path (for
-- example, tapping the TextBox directly or releasing focus with Escape).
UserInputService.TextBoxFocused:Connect(function(textBox)
	if isLegacyChatTextBox(textBox) then
		task.defer(updateChatIcon)
	end
end)
UserInputService.TextBoxFocusReleased:Connect(function(textBox)
	if isLegacyChatTextBox(textBox) then
		task.defer(updateChatIcon)
	end
end)
