local leaderstats = Instance.new("Frame")
leaderstats.Name = "LeaderstatsContainer"
leaderstats.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
leaderstats.LayoutOrder = 2
leaderstats.BackgroundTransparency = 1
leaderstats.BorderSizePixel = 0
leaderstats.ZIndex = 12
leaderstats.Parent = right
local statsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
statsLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
statsLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right
statsLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
statsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
statsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, STAT_SPACING)
statsLayout.Parent = leaderstats
local statColumns = {}

-- Fallback for places where PlayerlistModule.GetStats() initializes before the
-- local player's replicated leaderstats folder exists.
local function readLocalPlayerStats()
	local result = {}
	local folder = Player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	if not folder then return result end
	for _, valueObject in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if valueObject:IsA("ValueBase") then
			table.insert(result, {
				Name = valueObject.Name,
				Text = tostring(valueObject.Value),
			})
		end
	end
	return result
end

local function clearStats()
	for _, object in pairs(statColumns) do object:Destroy() end
	table.clear(statColumns)
	leaderstats.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
end
local function setStats(columns)
	clearStats()
	if type(columns) ~= "table" or #columns == 0 then
		columns = readLocalPlayerStats()
	end
	if type(columns) ~= "table" then return end
	local count = 0
	for index, data in ipairs(columns) do
		if smallTouchScreen() and index &gt; 1 then break end
		local key = tostring(data.Name or "")
		local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
		frame.Name = "Column" .. index
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, COLUMN_WIDTH, 1, 0)
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
		frame.LayoutOrder = index
		frame.ZIndex = 12
		frame.Parent = leaderstats
		local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		nameLabel.Name = "ColumnName"
		nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
		nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4)
		nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		nameLabel.TextSize = 14
		nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		nameLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
		nameLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR
		nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		nameLabel.Text = key
		nameLabel.ZIndex = 13
		nameLabel.Parent = frame
		local valueLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		valueLabel.Name = "ColumnValue"
		valueLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
		valueLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 19)
		valueLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
		valueLabel.TextSize = 14
		valueLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		valueLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
		valueLabel.TextColor3 = TEXT_COLOR
		valueLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
		valueLabel.Text = tostring(data.Text or "")
		valueLabel.ZIndex = 13
		valueLabel.Parent = frame
		statColumns[key] = frame
		count += 1
	end
	leaderstats.Size = UDim2.new(0, COLUMN_WIDTH * count + STAT_SPACING * math.max(0, count - 1), 1, 0)
end
local function updateStat(name, value)
	local frame = statColumns[name]
	local label = frame and frame:FindFirstChild("ColumnValue")
	if label and label:IsA("TextLabel") then label.Text = tostring(value) end
end

local function refreshStatsFromSource()
	local columns
	if PlayerlistModule and type(PlayerlistModule.GetStats) == "function" then
		local ok, result = pcall(PlayerlistModule.GetStats, PlayerlistModule)
		if not ok or type(result) ~= "table" then
			ok, result = pcall(PlayerlistModule.GetStats)
		end
		if ok and type(result) == "table" and #result &gt; 0 then
			columns = result
		end
	end
	if not columns then columns = readLocalPlayerStats() end
	setStats(columns)
end

local localStatsConnections = {}
local function clearLocalStatsConnections()
	for _, connection in ipairs(localStatsConnections) do
		disconnect(connection)
	end
	table.clear(localStatsConnections)
end
local function watchLocalStatsFolder(folder)
	clearLocalStatsConnections()
	if not folder then
		refreshStatsFromSource()
		return
	end
	local function refreshSoon()
		task.defer(refreshStatsFromSource)
	end
	local function watchValue(valueObject)
		if valueObject:IsA("ValueBase") then
			table.insert(localStatsConnections, valueObject:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Connect(refreshSoon))
		end
	end
	table.insert(localStatsConnections, folder.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		watchValue(child)
		refreshSoon()
	end))
	table.insert(localStatsConnections, folder.ChildRemoved:Connect(refreshSoon))
	for _, valueObject in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		watchValue(valueObject)
	end
	refreshStatsFromSource()
end

if PlayerlistModule then
	if type(PlayerlistModule.GetStats) == "function" then refreshStatsFromSource() end
	connectSignal(PlayerlistModule.OnLeaderstatsChanged, function(columns)
		if type(columns) == "table" and #columns &gt; 0 then setStats(columns) else refreshStatsFromSource() end
	end)
	connectSignal(PlayerlistModule.OnStatChanged, function(name, value)
		updateStat(name, value)
		-- Keep the local replicated values as a fallback for this player's own stats.
		if not statColumns[name] then refreshStatsFromSource() end
	end)
end

Player.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "leaderstats" then watchLocalStatsFolder(child) end
end)
Player.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
	if child.Name == "leaderstats" then watchLocalStatsFolder(nil) end
end)
local initialLocalStats = Player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
if initialLocalStats then watchLocalStatsFolder(initialLocalStats) else refreshStatsFromSource() end

usernameButton.Activated:Connect(function()
	if nativePlayerListEnabled and PlayerlistModule and type(PlayerlistModule.ToggleVisibility) == "function" then
		local ok, err = pcall(PlayerlistModule.ToggleVisibility)
		if not ok then warnTopbar("Player list toggle failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end
end)

local hurtOverlay = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
hurtOverlay.Name = "HurtOverlay"
hurtOverlay.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
hurtOverlay.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0)
hurtOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
hurtOverlay.Image = HURT_OVERLAY_IMAGE
hurtOverlay.ImageTransparency = 1
hurtOverlay.Visible = false
hurtOverlay.ZIndex = 100
hurtOverlay.Parent = GuiRoot
local hurtTween
local function animateHurtOverlay()
	if VRService.VREnabled or HURT_OVERLAY_IMAGE == "" then return end
	if hurtTween then hurtTween:Cancel() end
	hurtOverlay.ImageTransparency = 0.1
	hurtOverlay.Visible = true
	hurtTween = TweenService:Create(hurtOverlay, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { ImageTransparency = 1 })
	hurtTween:Play()
	hurtTween.Completed:Once(function() hurtOverlay.Visible = false end)
end

local currentHumanoid
local healthConnection
local lastHealth
local characterChildAddedConnection
local characterChildRemovedConnection
local function healthColor(fraction)
	local samples = {
		{ position = 0.1, color = HEALTH_RED_COLOR },
		{ position = 0.5, color = HEALTH_YELLOW_COLOR },
		{ position = 0.8, color = HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR },
	}
	if fraction &lt; samples[1].position then return HEALTH_RED_COLOR end
	if fraction &gt; samples[3].position then return HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR end

	local numerator = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	local denominator = 0
	for _, sample in ipairs(samples) do
		local delta = fraction - sample.position
		if delta == 0 then return sample.color end
		local weight = 1 / (delta * delta)
		numerator += Vector3.new(sample.color.R, sample.color.G, sample.color.B) * weight
		denominator += weight
	end
	if denominator == 0 then return HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR end
	local result = numerator / denominator
	return Color3.new(result.X, result.Y, result.Z)
end

local function updateHealth()
	local humanoid = currentHumanoid
	if not humanoid or not humanoid.Parent then
		-- Keep the historical health strip present at 100 HP and during the
		-- short respawn gap before the new Humanoid is parented.
		healthFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		healthFill.BackgroundColor3 = HEALTH_GREEN_COLOR
		healthBack.Visible = topbarEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled
			and not VRService.VREnabled
		return
	end
	local maximum = humanoid.MaxHealth
	local fraction = maximum &gt; 0 and math.clamp(humanoid.Health / maximum, 0, 1) or 0
	healthFill.Size = UDim2.new(fraction, 0, 1, 0)
	healthFill.BackgroundColor3 = healthColor(fraction)
	healthBack.Visible = topbarEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled
		and not VRService.VREnabled
end
local function attachCharacter(character)
	disconnect(healthConnection)
	disconnect(characterChildAddedConnection)
	disconnect(characterChildRemovedConnection)
	currentHumanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	if currentHumanoid then
		lastHealth = currentHumanoid.Health
		healthConnection = currentHumanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(newHealth)
			if lastHealth and currentHumanoid.MaxHealth &gt; 0
				and lastHealth - newHealth &gt;= currentHumanoid.MaxHealth * HEALTH_PERCENTAGE_FOR_OVERLAY
				and newHealth &lt; currentHumanoid.MaxHealth then
				animateHurtOverlay()
			end
			lastHealth = newHealth
			updateHealth()
		end)
	end
	updateHealth()
	if character then
		characterChildAddedConnection = character.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if child:IsA("Humanoid") then attachCharacter(character) end
		end)
		characterChildRemovedConnection = character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			if child == currentHumanoid then attachCharacter(character) end
		end)
	end
end
Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(attachCharacter)
Player.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function() attachCharacter(nil) end)
if Player.Character then attachCharacter(Player.Character) end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- RECORDING BUTTON / RECORDSOMETHING REMOTEEVENT BRIDGE
-- The executor loader intercepts outbound RecordSomething:FireServer calls.
-- This UI sends commands only; RecordingStart/Stop/OnGoingRecording are outputs.
----------------------------------------------------------------

local RecordingBridge = { Active = false }
do
	local recordingButton, recordingIcon = makeButton(
		left, "StopRecording", "rbxasset://textures/ui/RecordDown.png", 6
	)
	recordingButton.Visible = false
	recordingButton.Parent = nil

	local recordingActive = false
	local recordingRequestPending = false
	local recordingEventConnections = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local recordSomethingEvent = nil
	local recordingStartAckSerial = 0
	local recordingStopAckSerial = 0
	local recordingContinueAckSerial = 0
	local recordingResumeAckSerial = 0
	local recordingPauseAckSerial = 0
	local ongoingRecordingEventAvailable = false
	local ongoingRecordingLastAt = nil
	local ongoingRecordingSeenThisSession = false
	local recordingHeartbeatGraceUntil = 0

	local RECORDING_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 2
	local RECORDING_ACK_TIMEOUT = 0.65
	local RECORDING_RETRY_PAUSE = 0.12
	local ONGOING_RECORDING_TIMEOUT = 0.55
	local ONGOING_RECORDING_START_GRACE = 0.60

	local function setRecording(active)
		active = active == true
		local wasActive = recordingActive
		recordingActive = active
		RecordingBridge.Active = active

		if active and not wasActive and ongoingRecordingEventAvailable
			and not ongoingRecordingSeenThisSession then
			recordingHeartbeatGraceUntil = os.clock() + ONGOING_RECORDING_START_GRACE
		end

		local showRecording = recordingActive and topbarEnabled
			and not allCoreGuiDisabled and not VRService.VREnabled
		recordingButton.Visible = showRecording
		recordingButton.Parent = showRecording and left or nil
	end

	local function isRecordingEvent(event)
		return event and (event:IsA("BindableEvent") or event:IsA("RemoteEvent"))
	end

	local function findRecordSomething()
		local event = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RecordSomething")
		if event and (event:IsA("RemoteEvent") or event:IsA("BindableEvent")) then
			recordSomethingEvent = event
			return event
		end
		if event then
			warnTopbar("ReplicatedStorage.RecordSomething must be a RemoteEvent or executor-local BindableEvent, got " .. event.ClassName)
		end
		recordSomethingEvent = nil
		return nil
	end

	local function fireRecordSomething(command)
		local event = findRecordSomething() or recordSomethingEvent
		if not event or not event.Parent then
			warnTopbar("ReplicatedStorage.RecordSomething bridge is missing; loader or executor bridge is unavailable.")
			return false
		end
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			if event:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
				event:FireServer(command)
			elseif event:IsA("BindableEvent") then
				event:Fire(command)
			else
				error("unsupported RecordSomething class: " .. event.ClassName)
			end
		end)
		if not ok then
			warnTopbar("RecordSomething(" .. tostring(command) .. ") failed: " .. tostring(err))
			return false
		end
		return true
	end

	local function getStatusSignal(event)
		if not isRecordingEvent(event) then
			return nil
		end
		return event:IsA("BindableEvent") and event.Event or event.OnClientEvent
	end

	local function bindStatus(event, kind)
		if not isRecordingEvent(event) or recordingEventConnections[event] then
			return
		end
		local signal = getStatusSignal(event)
		if not signal then
			return
		end
		recordingEventConnections[event] = signal:Connect(function(value)
			if kind == "start" then
				recordingStartAckSerial += 1
				ongoingRecordingLastAt = nil
				ongoingRecordingSeenThisSession = false
				setRecording(true)
			elseif kind == "stop" then
				recordingStopAckSerial += 1
				setRecording(false)
			elseif kind == "ongoing" then
				ongoingRecordingEventAvailable = true
				if value == false then
					setRecording(false)
					return
				end
				ongoingRecordingLastAt = os.clock()
				ongoingRecordingSeenThisSession = true
				setRecording(true)
			elseif kind == "continue" then
				recordingContinueAckSerial += 1
				setRecording(value ~= false)
			elseif kind == "resume" then
				recordingResumeAckSerial += 1
				setRecording(value ~= false)
			elseif kind == "pause" then
				recordingPauseAckSerial += 1
				setRecording(false)
			elseif kind == "changed" and type(value) == "boolean" then
				setRecording(value)
			end
		end)
		if kind == "ongoing" then
			ongoingRecordingEventAvailable = true
		end
	end

	local function bindRecordSomething(event)
		if event and event.Name == "RecordSomething"
			and (event:IsA("RemoteEvent") or event:IsA("BindableEvent")) then
			recordSomethingEvent = event
		elseif event and event.Name == "RecordSomething" then
			warnTopbar("RecordSomething must be a RemoteEvent or executor-local BindableEvent, not " .. event.ClassName)
		end
	end

	local function bindStatusByName(event)
		if not event or not event.Parent then
			return
		end
		local name = string.lower(event.Name)
		if name == "recordingstart" then
			bindStatus(event, "start")
		elseif name == "recordingstop" or name == "recordingend" then
			bindStatus(event, "stop")
		elseif name == "ongoingrecording" then
			bindStatus(event, "ongoing")
		elseif name == "recordingcontinue" then
			bindStatus(event, "continue")
		elseif name == "recordingresume" then
			bindStatus(event, "resume")
		elseif name == "recordingpause" then
			bindStatus(event, "pause")
		elseif name == "recordingchanged" then
			bindStatus(event, "changed")
		end
	end

	local function discoverRecording()
		bindRecordSomething(ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RecordSomething"))
		for _, event in ipairs(ReplicatedStorage:GetChildren()) do
			bindStatusByName(event)
		end
	end
	RecordingBridge.Discover = discoverRecording

	discoverRecording()
	ReplicatedStorage.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
		if child.Name == "RecordSomething" then
			bindRecordSomething(child)
		else
			bindStatusByName(child)
		end
	end)

	local function sendRecordingRequest(targetActive, forceCommand)
		if recordingRequestPending then
			return false
		end
		if targetActive == recordingActive and not forceCommand then
			return true
		end
		recordingRequestPending = true
		local command = targetActive and "start" or "stop"
		local starting = targetActive
		if starting then
			ongoingRecordingLastAt = nil
			ongoingRecordingSeenThisSession = false
			recordingHeartbeatGraceUntil = os.clock() + ONGOING_RECORDING_START_GRACE
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			local confirmed = false
			for attempt = 1, RECORDING_MAX_ATTEMPTS do
				local before = starting and recordingStartAckSerial or recordingStopAckSerial
				local sent = fireRecordSomething(command)
				if sent then
					local deadline = os.clock() + RECORDING_ACK_TIMEOUT
					while os.clock() &lt; deadline do
						local after = starting and recordingStartAckSerial or recordingStopAckSerial
						if after &gt; before then
							confirmed = true
							break
						end
						if (starting and not recordingActive) or (not starting and recordingActive) then
							-- A transient status may have arrived on the next scheduler tick.
						end
						task.wait(0.04)
					end
				end
				if confirmed then
					break
				end
				warnTopbar("Recording " .. command .. " was not acknowledged (attempt "
					.. tostring(attempt) .. "/" .. tostring(RECORDING_MAX_ATTEMPTS) .. ").")
				if attempt &lt; RECORDING_MAX_ATTEMPTS then
					task.wait(RECORDING_RETRY_PAUSE)
				end
			end
			recordingRequestPending = false
			if not confirmed then
				warnTopbar("Recording " .. command .. " was not acknowledged; continuing to listen for status events.")
			end
		end)
		return true
	end

	recordingButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		-- If this stop button is visible, the user explicitly requested STOP.
		-- Do not suppress the command because a status update raced local state.
		if not recordingRequestPending then
			sendRecordingRequest(false, true)
		end
	end)

	-- The loader pulses OnGoingRecording every 0.12s. This watchdog detects a
	-- genuinely missing heartbeat, not a missing RemoteEvent.OnClientEvent.
	task.spawn(function()
		while topbar and topbar.Parent do
			task.wait(0.10)
			if recordingActive and ongoingRecordingEventAvailable then
				local now = os.clock()
				local timedOut
				if ongoingRecordingLastAt then
					timedOut = now - ongoingRecordingLastAt &gt; ONGOING_RECORDING_TIMEOUT
				else
					timedOut = now &gt; recordingHeartbeatGraceUntil
				end
				if timedOut then
					setRecording(false)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	RecordingBridge.Refresh = function()
		setRecording(RecordingBridge.Active)
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- PASSIVE NATIVE COREGUI STATE
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function readCoreGui(coreGuiType, current)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(coreGuiType) end)
	if ok and typeof(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return current
end

local function updateCoreGuiState()
	if syncYekuChatHotkey then syncYekuChatHotkey() end
	nativeChatEnabled = readCoreGui(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, nativeChatEnabled)
	nativeBackpackEnabled = readCoreGui(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, nativeBackpackEnabled)
	nativePlayerListEnabled = readCoreGui(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, nativePlayerListEnabled)
	nativeHealthEnabled = true -- Historical 2015/2016 health bar remains visible.

	allCoreGuiDisabled = coreGuiAllState == false
	-- Keep per-category CoreGui states intact. All is a separate gate below.
	pcall(function()
		GuiRoot:SetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled", nativeChatEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled)
		GuiRoot:SetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled", not allCoreGuiDisabled)
	end)

	local menuOnly = not topbarEnabled or allCoreGuiDisabled

	-- Remove optional buttons from LeftFrame instead of hiding LeftFrame itself.
	-- Settings is deliberately not touched, so its hamburger remains accessible.
	setButtonShown(chatButton, not menuOnly and customChatEnabled() and nativeChatEnabled
        and ChatModule ~= nil and not chatDisabled())
    if mobileChatButton then
        setButtonShown(mobileChatButton, not menuOnly and customChatEnabled() and nativeChatEnabled
            and ChatModule ~= nil and not chatDisabled())
    end
	updateChatIcon()
	setButtonShown(backpackButton, not menuOnly and nativeBackpackEnabled and BackpackModule ~= nil)
	leaderstats.Visible = not menuOnly and nativePlayerListEnabled and PlayerlistModule ~= nil
	usernameButton.Visible = not menuOnly and (nativeHealthEnabled or nativePlayerListEnabled)
		and not VRService.VREnabled
	if nativeHealthEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled then
		username.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 22)
		username.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Bottom
	else
		username.Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 1, 0)
		username.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	end
	updateHealth()
	RecordingBridge.Refresh()
	updateTransparency()
	updateInset()

	if not customChatEnabled() and chatVisible() then chatSetVisible(false) end
end

local function readInitialCoreGuiState(coreGuiType, current)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(coreGuiType) end)
	if ok and typeof(value) == "boolean" then return value end
	return current
end
nativeChatEnabled = readInitialCoreGuiState(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, nativeChatEnabled)
nativeBackpackEnabled = readInitialCoreGuiState(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, nativeBackpackEnabled)
nativePlayerListEnabled = readInitialCoreGuiState(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, nativePlayerListEnabled)
nativeHealthEnabled = true -- Historical 2015/2016 health bar remains visible.

-- Initial state must not treat one disabled CoreGui category as All=false.
-- If the aggregate query is false, verify every individual category before
-- inferring an already-disabled All state; live All events remain authoritative.
local aggregateReadOk, aggregateInitial = pcall(function()
	return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.All)
end)
if aggregateReadOk and aggregateInitial == true then
	coreGuiAllState = true
elseif aggregateReadOk and aggregateInitial == false then
	local everyCategoryOff = true
	local foundCategory = false
	for _, coreGuiType in ipairs(Enum.CoreGuiType:GetEnumItems()) do
		if coreGuiType ~= Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
			local categoryOk, categoryEnabled = pcall(function()
				return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(coreGuiType)
			end)
			if not categoryOk or categoryEnabled ~= false then
				everyCategoryOff = false
				break
			end
			foundCategory = true
		end
	end
	if everyCategoryOff and foundCategory then coreGuiAllState = false end
end

connectSignal(StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal, function(coreGuiType, enabled)
	if typeof(coreGuiType) == "EnumItem" then
		if coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All then
			coreGuiAllState = enabled == true
		elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then
			-- Respect the game's Chat permission even though Yeku draws the UI.
			nativeChatEnabled = enabled == true
		elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack then
			nativeBackpackEnabled = enabled == true
		elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList then
			nativePlayerListEnabled = enabled == true
		elseif coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Health then
			-- Do not hide the 2015/2016 health bar at 100 HP.
			nativeHealthEnabled = true
		end
	end
	updateCoreGuiState()
end)

GuiRoot:GetAttributeChangedSignal("YekuCustomChatEnabled"):Connect(function()
    updateCoreGuiState()
    if syncYekuChatHotkey then syncYekuChatHotkey() end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local ok, permitted = pcall(function() return ChatService:CanUserChatAsync(Player.UserId) end)
	if ok then
		canChat = RunService:IsStudio() or permitted == true
		updateCoreGuiState()
	else
		warnTopbar("Chat permission query failed: " .. tostring(permitted))
	end
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SLASH HOTKEY: TOPBAR OWNS THE KEY; CAPTURE THE LIVE YEKU TEXTBOX
----------------------------------------------------------------

-- The chat button only changes visibility. The slash action is the only Topbar
-- keyboard route: if Yeku's real ChatBar is not already visible and focused, open
-- it, wait for the GUI/input frame, then call TextBox:CaptureFocus() on the exact
-- TextBox exposed by Modules.Chat:GetChatBarTextBox().
local SLASH_ACTION_NAME = "YekuLegacyChatOpenAndFocus"
local slashConsumeUntilEnd = false
local lastSlashRouteTime = -math.huge
local lastSlashFallbackTime = -math.huge
local function ensureChatModuleForSlash()
	if ChatModule then return not chatDisabled() end
	local deadline = os.clock() + 15
	while not ChatModule and os.clock() &lt; deadline and topbar.Parent do
		local loaded = safeRequire("Chat", 0.5, true)
		if type(loaded) == "table" then
			ChatModule = loaded
			chatModuleSignalsConnected = false
			connectChatModuleSignals()
			updateCoreGuiState()
			if syncYekuChatHotkey then syncYekuChatHotkey() end
		else
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
		end
	end
	return ChatModule ~= nil and not chatDisabled()
end

local function routeSlashOpenAndFocus(source)
    if not canRouteSlash() then return false, "chat is disabled or unavailable" end
    if not ensureChatModuleForSlash() then return false, "Chat module did not become available" end

    local focusedBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
    local target = findYekuChatTextBox()
    if focusedBox and focusedBox ~= target then
        local visibleInput = isInputActuallyVisible(focusedBox)
        local competingLegacy = isLegacyChatTextBox(focusedBox)
        if visibleInput and not competingLegacy then
            return false, "another visible text field owns keyboard focus"
        end
        pcall(function() focusedBox:ReleaseFocus(false) end)
    end

    log("Slash route (" .. tostring(source) .. "): calling Chat:FocusChatBar() to open and focus the live ChatBar.")

    -- This is the Chat module's atomic route: it opens the window, restores the
    -- real ChatBar, applies its focused mode/background/height, and captures it.
    local combinedOK, combinedResult = chatCall("FocusChatBar")
    if not combinedOK or combinedResult ~= true then
        local setOK, setResult = chatCall("SetVisible", true)
        if not setOK or setResult == false then
            warnTopbar("Chat:SetVisible(true) fallback returned " .. tostring(setResult))
        end
    end

    for attempt = 1, 14 do
        -- Re-apply through the live module API; never accept internal focus alone.
        local visualOK = invokeChatbarFocusVisual()
        local live = findYekuChatTextBox()
        if visualOK and live and isChatBarVisuallyFocused(live) then
            updateChatIcon()
            updateUnread()
            return true, "live ChatBar opened and visually focused"
        end
        if attempt == 4 or attempt == 9 then
            chatCall("FocusChatBar")
        end
        if attempt &lt; 14 then RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() end
    end

    -- Final recovery is the same topbar route used by the chat icon click.
    local fallbackOK, fallbackReason = chatFocus()
    for attempt = 1, 8 do
        local visualOK = invokeChatbarFocusVisual()
        local live = findYekuChatTextBox()
        if visualOK and live and isChatBarVisuallyFocused(live) then
            updateChatIcon()
            updateUnread()
            return true, "recovered by Chat:FocusChatBar()"
        end
        if attempt &lt; 8 then RunService.RenderStepped:Wait() end
    end

    local live = findYekuChatTextBox()
    return false, "ChatBar did not become visually focused (box=" .. tostring(live)
        .. ", focused=" .. tostring(live and isChatBarActuallyFocused(live))
        .. ", visual=" .. tostring(live and isChatBarVisuallyFocused(live))
        .. ", chatVisible=" .. tostring(chatVisible())
        .. ", FocusChatBar=" .. tostring(combinedOK) .. ":" .. tostring(combinedResult)
        .. ", fallback=" .. tostring(fallbackOK) .. ":" .. tostring(fallbackReason) .. ")"
end

local function slashActionHandler(_actionName, inputState, inputObject)
	if not inputObject or inputObject.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Slash then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end
	if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then slashConsumeUntilEnd = false end
	if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.End or inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
		if slashConsumeUntilEnd then slashConsumeUntilEnd = false; return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink end
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end
	if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		return slashConsumeUntilEnd and Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink or Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end
	if not canRouteSlash() then return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass end

	local target = findYekuChatTextBox()
	local focusedBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
	if target and isChatBarActuallyFocused(target) then
        -- Do not pass '/' until the visible focus style has been restored. This
        -- prevents the bug where Roblox reports focus internally but the ChatBar
        -- remains in its idle/placeholder appearance.
        local visualOK = invokeChatbarFocusVisual()
        if visualOK and isChatBarVisuallyFocused(target) then
            return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
        end
        slashConsumeUntilEnd = true
        lastSlashRouteTime = os.clock()
        task.spawn(function()
            local fixed, reason = routeSlashOpenAndFocus("focused-visual-repair")
            if not fixed then warnTopbar("Slash visual-focus repair failed: " .. tostring(reason)) end
            updateChatIcon()
            updateUnread()
        end)
        return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
    end
	if focusedBox and focusedBox ~= target and isInputActuallyVisible(focusedBox)
		and not isLegacyChatTextBox(focusedBox) then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end
	slashConsumeUntilEnd = true
	lastSlashRouteTime = os.clock()
	task.spawn(function()
		local focused, reason = routeSlashOpenAndFocus("ContextActionService")
		if not focused then warnTopbar("Slash route failed: " .. tostring(reason)) end
		updateChatIcon()
		updateUnread()
	end)
	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

syncYekuChatHotkey = function()
	for _, oldActionName in ipairs({SLASH_ACTION_NAME, "YekuLegacyChatMainSlashFocus", "YekuLegacyChatSlashOpenFocus"}) do
		pcall(function() ContextActionService:UnbindAction(oldActionName) end)
	end
	if not canRouteSlash() then return end
	local priority = Enum.ContextActionPriority.High.Value + 1000000
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindActionAtPriority(SLASH_ACTION_NAME, slashActionHandler, false, priority, Enum.KeyCode.Slash)
	end)
	if not ok then warnTopbar("Could not bind slash open/focus action: " .. tostring(err))
	else log("Bound '/' open/focus action at priority " .. tostring(priority) .. ".") end
end

-- Opening slash is handled only by the high-priority ContextActionService action.
-- UserInputService.InputBegan cannot sink the physical key and would type the opening "/".
syncYekuChatHotkey()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TOPBAR ENABLE / VR / LAYOUT
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function updateLayout()
	if smallTouchScreen() then
		left.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
		right.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
		right.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
		leaderstats.Visible = false
	else
		left.Size = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 1, 0)
		right.Position = UDim2.new(0.6, 0, 0, 0)
		right.Size = UDim2.new(0.4, 0, 1, 0)
		leaderstats.Visible = topbarEnabled and not allCoreGuiDisabled
			and nativePlayerListEnabled and PlayerlistModule ~= nil
	end
end
local viewportConnection
local function connectViewport()
	disconnect(viewportConnection)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if camera then viewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateLayout) end
end
workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function() connectViewport(); updateLayout() end)
connectViewport()
updateLayout()

local function topbarStateChanged()
	updateTransparency()
	updateInset()
	topbarEnabledChangedEvent:Fire(topbarEnabled)
	if ChatModule and type(ChatModule.TopbarEnabledChanged) == "function" then
		local ok, err = pcall(ChatModule.TopbarEnabledChanged, ChatModule, topbarEnabled)
		if not ok then warnTopbar("Chat TopbarEnabledChanged failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end

	updateCoreGuiState()
end

-- PlayerGui exposes the original signal used by the old topbar. Keep that
-- subscription and also poll as a fallback for clients lacking the signal.
task.spawn(function()
	local playerGui = Player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or Player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
	if playerGui then
		local ok, signal = pcall(function() return playerGui.TopbarTransparencyChangedSignal end)
		if ok and signal then
			connectSignal(signal, updateTransparency)
		end
	end
	while topbar.Parent do
		if not allCoreGuiDisabled and topbarEnabled and not VRService.VREnabled then
			local expected = settingsActive and TOPBAR_OPAQUE or computeTransparency()
			if math.abs(topbar.BackgroundTransparency - expected) &gt; 0.001 then
				updateTransparency()
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.25)
	end
end)

VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(topbarStateChanged)
if SetTopbarEnabledEvent then
	SetTopbarEnabledEvent.Event:Connect(function(enabled)
		if type(enabled) == "boolean" then topbarEnabled = enabled; topbarStateChanged() end
	end)
end
if GetTopbarEnabledFunction then GetTopbarEnabledFunction.OnInvoke = function() return topbarEnabled end end

updateSettingsIcon()
updateTransparency()
updateInset()
updateCoreGuiState()

task.delay(2, function() if RecordingBridge.Discover then RecordingBridge.Discover() end end)

local YekuTopbarReadyModules = {}
local YekuTopbarReadyCount = 0
local YekuTopbarExpectedCount = 3
local function markTopbarModuleReady(moduleName)
	if YekuTopbarReadyModules[moduleName] then return end
	YekuTopbarReadyModules[moduleName] = true
	-- The roster remains independently lazy. Waiting for PlayerlistModule
	-- used to hold Settings/Backpack/Chat behind its dropdown and block-list work.
	if moduleName == "Chat" or moduleName == "ChatMain"
		or moduleName == "BackpackScript" or moduleName == "SettingsHub" then
		YekuTopbarReadyCount += 1
	end
	if YekuTopbarReadyCount &gt;= YekuTopbarExpectedCount then
		pcall(function() topbar:SetAttribute("YekuTopbarModulesReady", true) end)
		local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		if root then pcall(function() root:SetAttribute("YekuTopbarModulesReady", true) end) end
		log("Topbar readiness reached (" .. tostring(YekuTopbarReadyCount) .. "/" .. tostring(YekuTopbarExpectedCount) .. ").")
	end
end
pcall(function() topbar:SetAttribute("YekuTopbarModulesReady", false) end)

-- Some Yeku loaders insert Modules.Chat after Topbar.lua has started.
-- Do not permanently disable slash/button control because the module was not
-- parented yet during the first lookup.
if not ChatModule then
	task.spawn(function()
		local deadline = os.clock() + 10
		while not ChatModule and os.clock() &lt; deadline and topbar.Parent do
			task.wait(0.35)
			local loaded = safeRequire("Chat", 1, true)
			if type(loaded) == "table" then
				ChatModule = loaded
				if type(ChatModule.PrintWelcome) == "function" then
					local welcomeOK, welcomeError = pcall(function() ChatModule:PrintWelcome() end)
					if not welcomeOK then
						warnTopbar("Could not print legacy chat welcome messages: " .. tostring(welcomeError))
					end
				end
				connectChatModuleSignals()
				updateCoreGuiState()
				updateChatIcon()
				if syncYekuChatHotkey then syncYekuChatHotkey() end
				log("Modules.Chat loaded after Topbar startup; chat controls enabled.")
			end
		end
		if not ChatModule then
			warnTopbar("Modules.Chat did not become available; check the module require error above.")
		end
		markTopbarModuleReady("Chat")
	end)
end




-- Deferred module initialization: none of these modules may delay topbar paint.
local function loadTopbarModuleAsync(modulePath, label, onReady)
	task.spawn(function()
		-- A module may still be initializing from StarterScript when Topbar
		-- first asks for it. Keep the visible topbar alive and retry this
		-- optional control independently instead of losing it permanently.
		local deadline = os.clock() + (label == "PlayerlistModule" and 90 or 30)
		local attempts = 0
		local lastError = "module did not return a table"
		while topbar and topbar.Parent and os.clock() &lt; deadline do
			attempts += 1
			local ok, loaded = pcall(function()
				return safeRequire(modulePath, 1, true)
			end)
			if ok and type(loaded) == "table" then
				local readyOK, readyError = pcall(onReady, loaded)
				if readyOK then
					log(label .. " initialized asynchronously after topbar paint (attempt " .. tostring(attempts) .. ").")
					markTopbarModuleReady(label)
					return
				end
				lastError = "attachment failed: " .. tostring(readyError)
				warnTopbar(label .. " attachment attempt " .. tostring(attempts) .. " failed; retrying: " .. tostring(readyError))
			else
				lastError = ok and ("require returned " .. typeof(loaded)) or tostring(loaded)
			end
			lastError = ok and ("require returned " .. typeof(loaded)) or tostring(loaded)
			task.wait(0.35)
		end
		if topbar and topbar.Parent then
			warnTopbar(label .. " did not become ready after " .. tostring(attempts) .. " attempts: " .. tostring(lastError))
		end
		markTopbarModuleReady(label)
	end)
end

loadTopbarModuleAsync("PlayerlistModule", "PlayerlistModule", function(loaded)
	if PlayerlistModuleInitialized then return end
	PlayerlistModule = loaded
	if type(PlayerlistModule.GetStats) == "function" then refreshStatsFromSource() end
	connectSignal(PlayerlistModule.OnLeaderstatsChanged, function(columns)
		if type(columns) == "table" and #columns &gt; 0 then setStats(columns) else refreshStatsFromSource() end
	end)
	connectSignal(PlayerlistModule.OnStatChanged, function(name, value)
		updateStat(name, value)
		if not statColumns[name] then refreshStatsFromSource() end
	end)
	updateLayout()
	updateCoreGuiState()
	PlayerlistModuleInitialized = true
end)

loadTopbarModuleAsync("BackpackScript", "BackpackScript", function(loaded)
	if BackpackModule then return end
	BackpackModule = loaded
	connectSignal(BackpackModule.StateChanged, function(open)
		backpackIcon.Image = open and "rbxasset://textures/ui/Backpack/Backpack_Down.png" or "rbxasset://textures/ui/Backpack/Backpack.png"
	end)
	connectSignal(topbarEnabledChangedEvent.Event, function(enabled)
		if type(BackpackModule.TopbarEnabledChanged) == "function" then
			pcall(BackpackModule.TopbarEnabledChanged, BackpackModule, enabled)
		end
	end)
	updateCoreGuiState()
end)

loadTopbarModuleAsync("Settings/SettingsHub", "SettingsHub", function(loaded)
	if SettingsModule then return end
	SettingsModule = loaded
	connectSignal(SettingsModule.SettingsShowSignal, function(active)
		settingsActive = active == true
		updateSettingsIcon()
		updateTransparency()
	end)
	updateSettingsIcon()
	updateTransparency()
	updateCoreGuiState()
end)

log("Historical topbar initialized; focus/style fix revision=YekuTopbar_2016Style_RecordingRemoteEvent_v341; Modules.Chat=" .. tostring(ChatModule ~= nil))

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    DialogScript.lua
    Yeku / legacy CoreGui compatibility patch

    Preserves the legacy Dialog UI while supporting the custom CoreGui root:
        CoreGui.NewRobloxGui

    Compatibility changes:
      * resolves NewRobloxGui first, with a legacy RobloxGui fallback
      * optional/non-blocking loads for old support modules
      * graceful fallbacks for GameTranslator and small-touch detection
      * guards missing SetDialogInUse / Google Analytics legacy APIs
      * supports both newer and older CollectionService APIs
      * handles missing legacy BottomLeftControl and dialog-purpose assets
]]

	},
}

local function setUsingGamepad(input)
	local inputType = input and input.UserInputType
	usingGamepad = inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3
		or inputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(setUsingGamepad)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(setUsingGamepad)

pcall(function()
	FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2 = settings():GetFFlag("CoreScriptTranslateGameText2")
end)

local function waitForProperty(instance, name)
	while instance and not instance[name] do
		instance.Changed:Wait()
	end
end

-- Find the custom legacy root first. This script is usually loaded from a
-- descendant of CoreGui.NewRobloxGui by the Yeku module loader.
local function findRobloxGuiRoot()
	if script then
		local ancestor = script:FindFirstAncestor("NewRobloxGui")
		if ancestor and ancestor:IsA("ScreenGui") then
			return ancestor
		end
	end

	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	root = CoreGui:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end

	return nil
end

local RobloxGui = findRobloxGuiRoot()
if not RobloxGui then
	warn("[Yeku DialogScript] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui was not found; legacy dialog UI was not started.")
	return
end

local function safeRequire(parent, childName)
	if not parent then
		return nil
	end

	local module = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if not module then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
	if ok then
		return result
	end

	warn("[Yeku DialogScript] Could not load " .. childName .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

local modulesFolder = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local settingsFolder = modulesFolder and modulesFolder:FindFirstChild("Settings")
local TenFootInterface = safeRequire(modulesFolder, "TenFootInterface")
local utility = safeRequire(settingsFolder, "Utility")
local GameTranslator = safeRequire(modulesFolder, "GameTranslator")

local isTenFootInterface = false
if TenFootInterface and type(TenFootInterface.IsEnabled) == "function" then
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return TenFootInterface:IsEnabled()
	end)
	isTenFootInterface = ok and result == true
end

local function fallbackIsSmallTouchScreen()
	if not UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		return false
	end

	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera then
		return true
	end

	local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
	return math.min(viewport.X, viewport.Y) &lt;= 600
end

local isSmallTouchScreen = fallbackIsSmallTouchScreen()
if utility and type(utility.IsSmallTouchScreen) == "function" then
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return utility:IsSmallTouchScreen()
	end)
	if ok then
		isSmallTouchScreen = result == true
	end
end

local function translateChoice(label, source, text)
	text = text or ""

	if GameTranslator then
		if FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2
			and type(GameTranslator.TranslateAndRegister) == "function" then
			local ok = pcall(function()
				GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(label, source, text)
			end)
			if ok then
				return
			end
		end

		if type(GameTranslator.TranslateGameText) == "function" then
			local ok, translated = pcall(function()
				return GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(source, text)
			end)
			if ok and translated ~= nil then
				label.Text = translated
				return
			end
		end
	end

	label.Text = text
end

local playerGui = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("ControlFrame")
local gui = playerGui or RobloxGui
local touchEnabled = UserInputService.TouchEnabled

-- The server event is not present in every place/runtime. Dialogs still render
-- without it; state replication simply becomes unavailable.
local setDialogInUseEvent
if RobloxReplicatedStorage then
	setDialogInUseEvent = RobloxReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("SetDialogInUse")
	if not setDialogInUseEvent then
		local ok, event = pcall(function()
			return RobloxReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("SetDialogInUse", 5)
		end)
		if ok then
			setDialogInUseEvent = event
		end
	end
end

local function setDialogInUse(dialog, inUse, delaySeconds)
	if setDialogInUseEvent and setDialogInUseEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		pcall(function()
			setDialogInUseEvent:FireServer(dialog, inUse, delaySeconds or 0)
		end)
	end
end

local function reportAnalytics(...)
	local args = table.pack(...)
	pcall(function()
		game:ReportInGoogleAnalytics(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
	end)
end

local function isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog)
	local success, value = pcall(function()
		return dialog.BehaviorType == Enum.DialogBehaviorType.MultiplePlayers
	end)
	return success and value or false
end

function currentTone()
	if currentConversationDialog then
		return currentConversationDialog.Tone
	end
	return Enum.DialogTone.Neutral
end

function createChatNotificationGui()
	chatNotificationGui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
	chatNotificationGui.Name = "ChatNotificationGui"
	chatNotificationGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
	chatNotificationGui.Size = UDim2.new(PROMPT_SIZE.X / 31.5, 0, PROMPT_SIZE.Y / 31.5, 0)
	chatNotificationGui.SizeOffset = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	chatNotificationGui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.7, 0)
	chatNotificationGui.Enabled = true
	pcall(function() chatNotificationGui.RobloxLocked = true end)
	chatNotificationGui.Active = true

	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = "Background"
	button.Active = false
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	button.Image = ""
	button.Parent = chatNotificationGui

	local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	icon.Name = "Icon"
	icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	icon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	icon.Image = ""
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.Parent = button

	local activationButton = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	activationButton.Name = "ActivationButton"
	activationButton.Position = UDim2.new(-0.3, 0, -0.4, 0)
	activationButton.Size = UDim2.new(.8, 0, .8 * (PROMPT_SIZE.X / PROMPT_SIZE.Y), 0)
	activationButton.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/XButtonDark.png"
	activationButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	activationButton.Visible = false
	activationButton.Parent = button
end

function getChatColor(tone)
	if tone == Enum.DialogTone.Neutral then
		return Enum.ChatColor.Blue
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Friendly then
		return Enum.ChatColor.Green
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Enemy then
		return Enum.ChatColor.Red
	end
	return Enum.ChatColor.Blue
end

function styleChoices()
	for _, obj in pairs(choices) do
		obj.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end
	if lastChoice then
		lastChoice.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end
end

function styleMainFrame(tone)
	if tone == Enum.DialogTone.Neutral then
		mainFrame.Style = Enum.FrameStyle.ChatBlue
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Friendly then
		mainFrame.Style = Enum.FrameStyle.ChatGreen
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Enemy then
		mainFrame.Style = Enum.FrameStyle.ChatRed
	end
	styleChoices()
end

function setChatNotificationTone(notificationGui, purpose, tone)
	if tone == Enum.DialogTone.Neutral then
		notificationGui.Background.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/chatBubble_blue_notify_bkg.png"
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Friendly then
		notificationGui.Background.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/chatBubble_green_notify_bkg.png"
	elseif tone == Enum.DialogTone.Enemy then
		notificationGui.Background.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/chatBubble_red_notify_bkg.png"
	end

	local purposeData = PURPOSE_DATA[purpose]
	if not purposeData then
		-- Unknown/new DialogPurpose: leave a usable notification instead of erroring.
		notificationGui.Background.Icon.Image = ""
		notificationGui.Background.Icon.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		notificationGui.Background.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.25, 0)
		return
	end

	local newIcon, size = unpack(purposeData)
	local relativeSize = size / PROMPT_SIZE
	notificationGui.Background.Icon.Size = UDim2.new(relativeSize.X, 0, relativeSize.Y, 0)
	notificationGui.Background.Icon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 - (relativeSize.X / 2), 0, 0.4 - (relativeSize.Y / 2), 0)
	notificationGui.Background.Icon.Image = newIcon
end

function createMessageDialog()
	messageDialog = Instance.new("Frame")
	messageDialog.Name = "DialogScriptMessage"
	messageDialog.Style = Enum.FrameStyle.Custom
	messageDialog.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	messageDialog.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(31 / 255, 31 / 255, 31 / 255)
	messageDialog.Visible = false
	pcall(function() messageDialog.RobloxLocked = true end)

	local text = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	text.Name = "Text"
	text.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -1)
	text.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	text.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size14
	text.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	text.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	text.Parent = messageDialog
end

function showMessage(msg, size)
	if not messageDialog or not messageDialog.Parent then
		return
	end
	messageDialog.Text.Text = msg
	messageDialog.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, 40)
	messageDialog.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -size / 2, 0.5, -40)
	messageDialog.Visible = true
	task.wait(2)
	if messageDialog and messageDialog.Parent then
		messageDialog.Visible = false
	end
end

function variableDelay(str)
	local length = math.min(string.len(str or ""), 100)
	task.wait(0.75 + ((length / 75) * 1.5))
end

function resetColor(frame)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
end

function wanderDialog()
	if mainFrame then
		mainFrame.Visible = false
	end
	endDialog()
	showMessage(characterWanderedOffMessage, characterWanderedOffSize)
end

function timeoutDialog()
	if mainFrame then
		mainFrame.Visible = false
	end
	endDialog()
	showMessage(conversationTimedOut, conversationTimedOutSize)
end

function normalEndDialog()
	endDialog()
end

function endDialog()
	if currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine then
		coroutineMap[currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine] = false
		currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine = nil
	end

	local dialog = currentConversationDialog
	currentConversationDialog = nil
	if dialog and dialog.InUse then
		-- Wait five seconds before making the dialog available again.
		setDialogInUse(dialog, false, 5)
		task.delay(5, function()
			pcall(function() dialog.InUse = false end)
		end)
	end

	for trackedDialog, notificationGui in pairs(dialogMap) do
		if trackedDialog and notificationGui and notificationGui.Parent then
			notificationGui.Enabled = not trackedDialog.InUse
		end
	end

	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("Nothing")
	currentConversationPartner = nil

	if touchControlGui and touchControlGui.Parent then
		touchControlGui.Visible = true
	end
end

function sanitizeMessage(msg)
	if not msg or string.len(msg) == 0 then
		return "..."
	end
	return msg
end

local function chatFunc(dialog, ...)
	local chat = game:GetService("Chat")
	local args = table.pack(...)
	local chatLocal
	pcall(function()
		chatLocal = chat.ChatLocal
	end)

	if isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog) and type(chatLocal) == "function" then
		local ok = pcall(function()
			chatLocal(chat, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
		end)
		if not ok then
			chat:Chat(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
		end
	else
		chat:Chat(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
	end
end

function selectChoice(choice)
	renewKillswitch(currentConversationDialog)

	mainFrame.Visible = false
	if choice == lastChoice then
		chatFunc(currentConversationDialog, localPlayer.Character, lastChoice.UserPrompt.Text, getChatColor(currentTone()))
		normalEndDialog()
	else
		local dialogChoice = choiceMap[choice]
		if not dialogChoice then
			normalEndDialog()
			return
		end

		chatFunc(currentConversationDialog, localPlayer.Character, sanitizeMessage(dialogChoice.UserDialog), getChatColor(currentTone()))
		task.wait(1)
		currentConversationDialog:SignalDialogChoiceSelected(localPlayer, dialogChoice)
		chatFunc(currentConversationDialog, currentConversationPartner, sanitizeMessage(dialogChoice.ResponseDialog), getChatColor(currentTone()))
		variableDelay(dialogChoice.ResponseDialog)
		presentDialogChoices(currentConversationPartner, dialogChoice:GetChildren(), dialogChoice)
	end
end

function newChoice()
	local dummyFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	dummyFrame.Visible = false

	local frame = Instance.new("TextButton")
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(227 / 255, 227 / 255, 227 / 255)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.AutoButtonColor = false
	frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	frame.Text = ""
	frame.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	end)
	frame.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	end)
	frame.SelectionImageObject = dummyFrame
	frame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		selectChoice(frame)
	end)
	pcall(function() frame.RobloxLocked = true end)

	local prompt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	prompt.Name = "UserPrompt"
	prompt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	prompt.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	prompt.FontSize = FONT_SIZE
	prompt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 0)
	prompt.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32 - 40, 1, 0)
	prompt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	prompt.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	prompt.TextWrap = true
	prompt.Parent = frame

	local selectionButton = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	selectionButton.Name = "RBXchatDialogSelectionButton"
	selectionButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -33 / 2)
	selectionButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 33, 0, 33)
	selectionButton.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/AButtonLightSmall.png"
	selectionButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectionButton.Visible = false
	selectionButton.Parent = frame

	return frame
end

function initialize(parent)
	choices[1] = newChoice()
	choices[2] = newChoice()
	choices[3] = newChoice()
	choices[4] = newChoice()

	lastChoice = newChoice()
	lastChoice.UserPrompt.Text = "Goodbye!"
	lastChoice.Size = UDim2.new(1, WIDTH_BONUS, 0, TEXT_HEIGHT + CHOICE_PADDING)

	mainFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	mainFrame.Name = "UserDialogArea"
	mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, FRAME_WIDTH, 0, 200)
	mainFrame.Style = Enum.FrameStyle.ChatBlue
	mainFrame.Visible = false

	for _, obj in pairs(choices) do
		pcall(function() obj.RobloxLocked = true end)
		obj.Parent = mainFrame
	end

	pcall(function() lastChoice.RobloxLocked = true end)
	lastChoice.Parent = mainFrame

	pcall(function() mainFrame.RobloxLocked = true end)
	mainFrame.Parent = parent
end

function presentDialogChoices(talkingPart, dialogChoices, parentDialog)
	if not currentConversationDialog then
		return
	end

	currentConversationPartner = talkingPart
	local sortedDialogChoices = {}
	for _, obj in pairs(dialogChoices) do
		if obj:IsA("DialogChoice") then
			table.insert(sortedDialogChoices, obj)
		end
	end
	table.sort(sortedDialogChoices, function(a, b)
		return a.Name &lt; b.Name
	end)

	if #sortedDialogChoices == 0 then
		normalEndDialog()
		return
	end

	local pos = 1
	local yPosition = 0
	choiceMap = {}
	for _, obj in pairs(choices) do
		obj.Visible = false
	end

	for _, obj in pairs(sortedDialogChoices) do
		if pos &lt;= #choices then
			choices[pos].Size = UDim2.new(1, WIDTH_BONUS, 0, TEXT_HEIGHT * 3)
			translateChoice(choices[pos].UserPrompt, obj, obj.UserDialog)

			local height = (math.ceil(choices[pos].UserPrompt.TextBounds.Y / TEXT_HEIGHT) * TEXT_HEIGHT) + CHOICE_PADDING
			choices[pos].Position = UDim2.new(0, XPOS_OFFSET, 0, YPOS_OFFSET + yPosition)
			choices[pos].Size = UDim2.new(1, WIDTH_BONUS, 0, height)
			choices[pos].Visible = true
			choiceMap[choices[pos]] = obj
			yPosition = yPosition + height + 1
			pos = pos + 1
		end
	end

	lastChoice.Size = UDim2.new(1, WIDTH_BONUS, 0, TEXT_HEIGHT * 3)
	lastChoice.UserPrompt.Text = parentDialog.GoodbyeDialog == "" and "Goodbye!" or parentDialog.GoodbyeDialog
	local height = (math.ceil(lastChoice.UserPrompt.TextBounds.Y / TEXT_HEIGHT) * TEXT_HEIGHT) + CHOICE_PADDING
	lastChoice.Size = UDim2.new(1, WIDTH_BONUS, 0, height)
	lastChoice.Position = UDim2.new(0, XPOS_OFFSET, 0, YPOS_OFFSET + yPosition)
	lastChoice.Visible = true

	if goodbyeChoiceActiveFlag and not parentDialog.GoodbyeChoiceActive then
		lastChoice.Visible = false
		mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, FRAME_WIDTH, 0, yPosition + (STYLE_PADDING * 2) + (YPOS_OFFSET * 2))
	else
		mainFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, FRAME_WIDTH, 0, yPosition + lastChoice.AbsoluteSize.Y + (STYLE_PADDING * 2) + (YPOS_OFFSET * 2))
	end

	mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 1, -mainFrame.Size.Y.Offset - 20)
	if isSmallTouchScreen then
		local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
		local touchScreenGui = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("TouchGui")
		if touchScreenGui then
			touchControlGui = touchScreenGui:FindFirstChild("TouchControlFrame")
			if touchControlGui then
				touchControlGui.Visible = false
			end
		end
		mainFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -mainFrame.Size.Y.Offset)
	end

	styleMainFrame(currentTone())
	mainFrame.Visible = true

	if usingGamepad then
		pcall(function() GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = choices[1] end)
	end
end

function doDialog(dialog)
	if dialog.InitialPrompt == "" then
		warn("Can't start a dialog with an empty InitialPrompt")
		return
	end

	local isMultiplePlayers = isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog)
	if dialog.InUse and not isMultiplePlayers then
		return
	else
		currentConversationDialog = dialog
		dialog.InUse = true
		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction("Nothing", function() end, false,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4)
		setDialogInUse(dialog, true, 0)
	end

	chatFunc(dialog, dialog.Parent, dialog.InitialPrompt, getChatColor(dialog.Tone))
	variableDelay(dialog.InitialPrompt)
	presentDialogChoices(dialog.Parent, dialog:GetChildren(), dialog)
end

function renewKillswitch(dialog)
	if currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine then
		coroutineMap[currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine] = false
		currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine = nil
	end

	currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine = coroutine.create(function(thisCoroutine)
		task.wait(15)
		if thisCoroutine ~= nil then
			if coroutineMap[thisCoroutine] == nil then
				setDialogInUse(dialog, false, 0)
				pcall(function() dialog.InUse = false end)
			end
			coroutineMap[thisCoroutine] = nil
		end
	end)
	coroutine.resume(currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine, currentDialogTimeoutCoroutine)
end

function checkForLeaveArea()
	while currentConversationDialog do
		local dialog = currentConversationDialog
		if dialog.Parent and dialog.Parent:IsA("BasePart")
			and localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(dialog.Parent.Position) &gt;= dialog.ConversationDistance then
			wanderDialog()
		end
		task.wait(1)
	end
end

function startDialog(dialog)
	if dialog.Parent and dialog.Parent:IsA("BasePart") then
		reportAnalytics("Dialogue", "Old Dialogue", "Conversation Initiated")

		if localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(dialog.Parent.Position) &gt;= dialog.ConversationDistance then
			showMessage(tooFarAwayMessage, tooFarAwaySize)
			return
		end

		for _, notificationGui in pairs(dialogMap) do
			if notificationGui then
				notificationGui.Enabled = false
			end
		end

		renewKillswitch(dialog)
		task.delay(1, checkForLeaveArea)
		doDialog(dialog)
	end
end

function removeDialog(dialog)
	if dialogMap[dialog] then
		dialogMap[dialog]:Destroy()
		dialogMap[dialog] = nil
	end
	if dialogConnections[dialog] then
		dialogConnections[dialog]:Disconnect()
		dialogConnections[dialog] = nil
	end
end

function addDialog(dialog)
	if not dialog.Parent then
		return
	end

	if dialog.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and dialog:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
		FlagHasReportedPlace = true
		reportAnalytics("Dialogue", "Old Dialogue", "Used In Place", nil, game.PlaceId)

		local chatGui = chatNotificationGui:Clone()
		chatGui.Adornee = dialog.Parent
		pcall(function() chatGui.RobloxLocked = true end)
		chatGui.Enabled = not dialog.InUse or isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog)
		chatGui.Parent = CoreGui

		chatGui.Background.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			startDialog(dialog)
		end)
		setChatNotificationTone(chatGui, dialog.Purpose, dialog.Tone)
		dialogMap[dialog] = chatGui

		dialogConnections[dialog] = dialog.Changed:Connect(function(prop)
			if prop == "Parent" and dialog.Parent then
				removeDialog(dialog)
				addDialog(dialog)
			elseif prop == "InUse" then
				if not isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog) then
					chatGui.Enabled = (currentConversationDialog == nil) and not dialog.InUse
				else
					chatGui.Enabled = currentConversationDialog ~= dialog
				end

				if not dialog.InUse and not isDialogMultiplePlayers(dialog)
					and dialog == currentConversationDialog then
					timeoutDialog()
				end
			elseif prop == "Tone" or prop == "Purpose" then
				setChatNotificationTone(chatGui, dialog.Purpose, dialog.Tone)
			end
		end)
	else
		-- Keep watching for a dialog moved under a BasePart later.
		dialogConnections[dialog] = dialog.Changed:Connect(function(prop)
			if prop == "Parent" and dialog.Parent then
				removeDialog(dialog)
				addDialog(dialog)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function connectDialogCollectionSignals()
	local addedSignal
	local removedSignal

	pcall(function() addedSignal = CollectionService:GetInstanceAddedSignal("Dialog") end)
	pcall(function() removedSignal = CollectionService:GetInstanceRemovedSignal("Dialog") end)

	if addedSignal then
		addedSignal:Connect(function(obj)
			if obj:IsA("Dialog") then addDialog(obj) end
		end)
	else
		pcall(function()
			CollectionService.ItemAdded:Connect(function(obj)
				if obj:IsA("Dialog") then addDialog(obj) end
			end)
		end)
	end

	if removedSignal then
		removedSignal:Connect(function(obj)
			if obj:IsA("Dialog") then removeDialog(obj) end
		end)
	else
		pcall(function()
			CollectionService.ItemRemoved:Connect(function(obj)
				if obj:IsA("Dialog") then removeDialog(obj) end
			end)
		end)
	end

	local taggedDialogs
	local ok = pcall(function()
		taggedDialogs = CollectionService:GetTagged("Dialog")
	end)
	if not ok then
		pcall(function()
			taggedDialogs = CollectionService:GetCollection("Dialog")
		end)
	end

	for _, obj in pairs(taggedDialogs or {}) do
		if obj:IsA("Dialog") then addDialog(obj) end
	end
end

function onLoad()
	waitForProperty(localPlayer, "Character")

	createChatNotificationGui()
	createMessageDialog()
	pcall(function() messageDialog.RobloxLocked = true end)
	messageDialog.Parent = gui

	local bottomLeftControl = gui:FindFirstChild("BottomLeftControl")
	if not bottomLeftControl then
		bottomLeftControl = gui:WaitForChild("BottomLeftControl", 5)
	end

	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = "DialogFrame"
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	pcall(function() frame.RobloxLocked = true end)
	pcall(function() GuiService:AddSelectionParent("RBXDialogGroup", frame) end)

	if touchEnabled and not isSmallTouchScreen then
		frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 0)
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.1, 0)
		frame.Parent = gui
	elseif isSmallTouchScreen then
		frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.9, -10)
		frame.Size = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0.1, 0)
		frame.Parent = gui
	elseif bottomLeftControl then
		frame.Parent = bottomLeftControl
	else
		warn("[Yeku DialogScript] BottomLeftControl is missing; putting the dialog selection frame directly under the legacy GUI.")
		frame.Parent = gui
	end

	initialize(frame)
	connectDialogCollectionSignals()
end

local function getLocalHumanoidRootPart()
	if localPlayer.Character then
		return localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end
	return nil
end

function dialogIsValid(dialog)
	return dialog ~= nil and dialog.Parent ~= nil and dialog.Parent:IsA("BasePart")
end

local lastClosestDialog = nil
local getClosestDialogToPosition
pcall(function()
	getClosestDialogToPosition = GuiService.GetClosestDialogToPosition
end)

local function findClosestTrackedDialog(characterPosition)
	local closestDialog = nil
	local closestDistance = math.huge

	for dialog in pairs(dialogMap) do
		if dialogIsValid(dialog) then
			local ok, distance = pcall(function()
				return (dialog.Parent.Position - characterPosition).Magnitude
			end)
			if ok and distance &lt; closestDistance then
				closestDistance = distance
				closestDialog = dialog
			end
		end
	end

	return closestDialog
end

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	local humanoidRootPart = getLocalHumanoidRootPart()
	local closestDialog = nil

	if humanoidRootPart then
		local characterPosition = humanoidRootPart.Position
		if type(getClosestDialogToPosition) == "function" then
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return getClosestDialogToPosition(GuiService, characterPosition)
			end)
			if ok then
				closestDialog = result
			end
		end
		if not closestDialog then
			closestDialog = findClosestTrackedDialog(characterPosition)
		end
	end

	if humanoidRootPart and dialogIsValid(closestDialog) and currentConversationDialog == nil then
		local dialogTriggerDistance = closestDialog.TriggerDistance
		local dialogTriggerOffset = closestDialog.TriggerOffset
		local distanceFromCharacterWithOffset = localPlayer:DistanceFromCharacter(
			closestDialog.Parent.Position + dialogTriggerOffset
		)

		if dialogTriggerDistance ~= 0
			and distanceFromCharacterWithOffset &lt; closestDialog.ConversationDistance
			and distanceFromCharacterWithOffset &lt; dialogTriggerDistance then
			startDialog(closestDialog)
		end
	end

	if usingGamepad == true and closestDialog ~= lastClosestDialog then
		if dialogMap[lastClosestDialog] then
			dialogMap[lastClosestDialog].Background.ActivationButton.Visible = false
		end
		lastClosestDialog = closestDialog
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("StartDialogAction")

		if closestDialog ~= nil then
			ContextActionService:BindCoreAction("StartDialogAction", function(_, userInputState)
				if userInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and closestDialog and closestDialog.Parent then
					startDialog(closestDialog)
				end
			end, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX)

			if dialogMap[closestDialog] then
				dialogMap[closestDialog].Background.ActivationButton.Visible = true
			end
		end
	end
end)

local lastSelectedChoice = nil
GuiService.Changed:Connect(function(property)
	if property == "SelectedCoreObject" then
		if lastSelectedChoice and lastSelectedChoice:FindFirstChild("RBXchatDialogSelectionButton") then
			lastSelectedChoice:FindFirstChild("RBXchatDialogSelectionButton").Visible = false
			lastSelectedChoice.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end

		lastSelectedChoice = nil
		pcall(function() lastSelectedChoice = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject end)
		if lastSelectedChoice and lastSelectedChoice:FindFirstChild("RBXchatDialogSelectionButton") then
			lastSelectedChoice:FindFirstChild("RBXchatDialogSelectionButton").Visible = true
			lastSelectedChoice.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		end
	end
end)

                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: NotificationScript2.lua
    Version 1.1 - Yeku NewRobloxGui compatibility patch
    Based on the original legacy NotificationScript2.lua.

    Compatibility changes:
      * Prefer CoreGui.NewRobloxGui instead of requiring CoreGui.RobloxGui.
      * Put notification UI under the ScreenGui, not the copied CoreScripts folder.
      * Treat optional historical modules/services/FFlags as optional.
      * Avoid infinite waits for legacy GamepadMenu modules.
      * Guard legacy-only event/API access so one unsupported event does not stop
        the rest of the notification system.
      * Preserve the original notification queue and event behaviors.
]]

local function getService(name)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return game:GetService(name)
	end)
	if ok then
		return result
	end
	return nil
end

local function getFFlag(name, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return settings():GetFFlag(name)
	end)
	if ok and type(value) == "boolean" then
		return value
	end
	return defaultValue == true
end

local function getFVariable(name, defaultValue)
	local ok, value = pcall(function()
		return tonumber(settings():GetFVariable(name))
	end)
	if ok and value ~= nil then
		return value
	end
	return defaultValue
end

local function findModule(parent, name)
	if not parent then
		return nil
	end
	local direct = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if direct and (direct:IsA("ModuleScript") or direct:IsA("Script")) then
		return direct
	end
	return nil
end

local function safeRequire(moduleScript)
	if not moduleScript then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return require(moduleScript)
	end)
	if ok then
		return result
	end
	warn("[NotificationScript2] Could not load optional module " .. moduleScript:GetFullName() .. ": " .. tostring(result))
	return nil
end

local function LocalizedGetString(key, fallbackValue)
    key = tostring(key or "")
    if fallbackValue ~= nil then return tostring(fallbackValue) end
    if LocalizationService then
        local okTables, tables = pcall(function()
            return LocalizationService:GetCorescriptLocalizations()
        end)
        if okTables and type(tables) == "table" then
            for _, localizationTable in pairs(tables) do
                local okEntries, entries = pcall(function() return localizationTable:GetEntries() end)
                if okEntries and type(entries) == "table" then
                    for _, entry in ipairs(entries) do
                        if type(entry) == "table" and entry.Key == key
                            and type(entry.Source) == "string" and entry.Source ~= "" then
                            return entry.Source
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return key
end

local function reportAnalytics(methodName, ...)
	if not AnalyticsService then
		return
	end
	-- Lua/Luau does not allow forwarding `...` directly from a nested closure.
	local args = { ... }
	local unpackArgs = table.unpack or unpack
	pcall(function()
		local method = AnalyticsService[methodName]
		if type(method) == "function" then
			method(AnalyticsService, unpackArgs(args))
		end
	end)
end

local function createFrame(name, size, position, bgt)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Name = name
	frame.Size = size
	frame.Position = position
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = bgt
	return frame
end

	local button = Instance.new("TextButton")
	button.Name = name
	button.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -2, 0.5, 0)
	button.Position = position
	button.BackgroundTransparency = BG_TRANSPARENCY
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
	button.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	button.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size18
	button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	button.Text = text
	return button
end

	button.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	return button
end
