local insertNotification
local removeNotification

local function getFriendImage(playerId)
	-- Preserve SocialUtil's old thumbnail behavior when its module exists.
	if SocialUtil and type(SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage) == "function" then
		local ok, image = pcall(function()
			return SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage(playerId, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, MAX_GET_FRIEND_IMAGE_YIELD_TIME)
		end)
		if ok and image then
			return image
		end
	end

	local ok, image = pcall(function()
		local content = Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(playerId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size48x48)
		return content
	end)
	if ok and image then
		return image
	end
	return FRIEND_IMAGE .. tostring(playerId)
end

local function createNotification(title, text, image)
	local notificationFrame = DefaultNotification:Clone()
	notificationFrame.Position = UDim2.new(1, 4, 1, -NOTIFICATION_Y_OFFSET - 4)

	local notificationTitle = NotificationTitle:Clone()
	notificationTitle.Text = tostring(title or "")
	notificationTitle.Parent = notificationFrame

	local notificationText = NotificationText:Clone()
	notificationText.Text = tostring(text or "")
	notificationText.Parent = notificationFrame

	if image == nil or image == "" then
		notificationFrame.Parent = NotificationFrame
		if not notificationText.TextFits then
			local textSize = TextService:GetTextSize(notificationText.Text, notificationText.TextSize, notificationText.Font, Vector2.new(notificationText.AbsoluteSize.X, 1000))
			local addHeight = math.min(textSize.Y - notificationText.Size.Y.Offset, NOTIFICATION_TEXT_HEIGHT_MAX - notificationText.Size.Y.Offset)
			notificationTitle.Position = notificationTitle.Position - UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight / 2)
			notificationText.Position = notificationText.Position - UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight / 2)
			notificationFrame.Size = notificationFrame.Size + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight)
			notificationText.Size = notificationText.Size + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight)
		end
		notificationFrame.Parent = nil
	end

	if image and image ~= "" then
		local notificationImage = NotificationImage:Clone()
		notificationImage.Image = image
		notificationImage.Parent = notificationFrame

		notificationTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, (4.0 / 3.0) * IMAGE_SIZE, 0.5, -NOTIFICATION_TITLE_Y_OFFSET)
		notificationTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

		notificationFrame.Parent = NotificationFrame
		notificationText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -IMAGE_SIZE - 16, 0, NOTIFICATION_TEXT_HEIGHT)
		notificationText.Position = UDim2.new(0, (4.0 / 3.0) * IMAGE_SIZE, 0.5, NOTIFICATION_TEXT_Y_OFFSET)
		notificationText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		if not notificationText.TextFits then
			local extraText = nil
			local originalText = notificationText.Text
			for i = string.len(originalText) - 1, 2, -1 do
				if string.sub(originalText, i, i) == " " then
					notificationText.Text = string.sub(originalText, 1, i - 1)
					if notificationText.TextFits then
						extraText = string.sub(originalText, i + 1)
						break
					end
				end
			end
			if extraText then
				local notificationText2 = NotificationText:Clone()
				notificationText2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				notificationText2.Text = extraText
				notificationText2.Name = "ExtraText"
				notificationText2.Parent = notificationFrame

				local textSize = TextService:GetTextSize(extraText, notificationText2.TextSize, notificationText2.Font, Vector2.new(notificationText2.AbsoluteSize.X, 1000))
				local addHeight = math.min(textSize.Y, NOTIFICATION_TEXT_HEIGHT_MAX - notificationText.Size.Y.Offset)
				notificationTitle.Position = notificationTitle.Position - UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight / 2)
				notificationText.Position = notificationText.Position - UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight / 2)
				notificationFrame.Size = notificationFrame.Size + UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, addHeight)

				notificationText2.Size = UDim2.new(notificationText2.Size.X.Scale, notificationText2.Size.X.Offset, 0, addHeight)
				notificationText2.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
				notificationText2.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, notificationText.Position.Y.Scale, notificationText.Position.Y.Offset + notificationText.AbsoluteSize.Y)
			else
				notificationText.Text = originalText
			end
		end
		notificationFrame.Parent = nil
	end

	if GuiService and HttpService then
		pcall(function()
			GuiService:AddSelectionParent(HttpService:GenerateGUID(false), notificationFrame)
		end)
	end
	return notificationFrame
end

local function findNotification(notification)
	for i = 1, #NotificationQueue do
		if NotificationQueue[i] == notification then
			return i
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function updateNotifications()
	local pos = 1
	local yOffset = 0
	for i = #NotificationQueue, 1, -1 do
		local currentNotification = NotificationQueue[i]
		if currentNotification then
			local frame = currentNotification.Frame
			if frame and frame.Parent then
				local thisOffset = currentNotification.IsFriend and frame.Size.Y.Offset + ((NOTIFICATION_Y_OFFSET + 2) * 0.5) or frame.Size.Y.Offset
				yOffset = yOffset + thisOffset
				frame:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -yOffset - (pos * 4)), EASE_DIR, EASE_STYLE, TWEEN_TIME, true, function()
					if currentNotification.TweenOutCallback then
						currentNotification.TweenOutCallback()
					end
				end)
				pos = pos + 1
			end
		end
	end
end

local lastTimeInserted = 0
insertNotification = function(notification)
	spawn(function()
		while isPaused do wait() end
		notification.IsActive = true
		local size = #NotificationQueue
		if size &gt;= MAX_NOTIFICATIONS then
			if NotificationQueue[1] then
				NotificationQueue[1].Duration = 0
			end
			OverflowQueue[#OverflowQueue + 1] = notification
			return
		end

		NotificationQueue[size + 1] = notification
		notification.Frame.Parent = NotificationFrame

		spawn(function()
			wait(TWEEN_TIME * 2)
			while notification.Duration &gt; 0 do
				wait(0.2)
				notification.Duration = notification.Duration - 0.2
			end
			removeNotification(notification)
		end)

		while tick() - lastTimeInserted &lt; TWEEN_TIME do
			wait()
		end
		lastTimeInserted = tick()
		updateNotifications()
	end)
end

removeNotification = function(notification)
	if not notification then return end
	local index = findNotification(notification)
	if index then
		table.remove(NotificationQueue, index)
	end
	local frame = notification.Frame
	if frame and frame.Parent then
		notification.IsActive = false
		spawn(function()
			while isPaused do wait() end
			local function doTweenOut()
				return frame:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, frame.Position.Y.Offset), EASE_DIR, EASE_STYLE, TWEEN_TIME, false, function()
					if frame and frame.Parent then
						frame:Destroy()
					end
				end)
			end
			if not doTweenOut() then
				notification.TweenOutCallback = doTweenOut
			end
		end)
	end
	if #OverflowQueue &gt; 0 then
		local nextNotification = table.remove(OverflowQueue, 1)
		insertNotification(nextNotification)
		if #OverflowQueue &gt; 0 and NotificationQueue[1] then
			NotificationQueue[1].Duration = 0
		end
	else
		updateNotifications()
	end
end

local function sendNotificationInfo(notificationInfo)
	if type(notificationInfo) ~= "table" then return end
	notificationInfo.Duration = notificationInfo.Duration or DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION
	BindableEvent_SendNotificationInfo:Fire(notificationInfo)
end

local function onSendNotificationInfo(notificationInfo)
	if VRService and VRService.VREnabled then
		-- VR notifications are handled by Modules.VR.NotificationHub.
		return
	end
	local callback = notificationInfo.Callback
	local button1Text = notificationInfo.Button1Text
	local button2Text = notificationInfo.Button2Text
	local localizedButton1Text = notificationInfo.Button1TextLocalized
	local localizedButton2Text = notificationInfo.Button2TextLocalized
	local notification = {}
	local notificationFrame

	if FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2 then
		notificationFrame = createNotification(
			GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(CoreGui, notificationInfo.Title),
			GameTranslator:TranslateGameText(CoreGui, notificationInfo.Text),
			notificationInfo.Image)
	else
		notificationFrame = createNotification(notificationInfo.Title, notificationInfo.Text, notificationInfo.Image)
	end

	local button1
	if button1Text and button1Text ~= "" then
		notification.IsFriend = true
		if FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2 then
			button1 = createTextButton("Button1", UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 2))
			if notificationInfo.AutoLocalize then
				GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(button1, CoreGui, button1Text)
			else
				button1.Text = button1Text
			end
		else
			button1 = createTextButtonOld("Button1", localizedButton1Text or button1Text, UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 2))
		end
		button1.Parent = notificationFrame
		local button1ClickedConnection
		button1ClickedConnection = button1.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if button1ClickedConnection then
				button1ClickedConnection:Disconnect()
				button1ClickedConnection = nil
				removeNotification(notification)
				if callback and typeof(callback) == "Instance" and callback:IsA("BindableFunction") then
					pcall(function() callback:Invoke(button1Text) end)
				elseif type(callback) == "function" then
					pcall(callback, button1Text)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local button2
	if button2Text and button2Text ~= "" then
		notification.IsFriend = true
		if FFlagCoreScriptTranslateGameText2 then
			button2 = createTextButton("Button2", UDim2.new(0.5, 2, 1, 2))
			if notificationInfo.AutoLocalize then
				GameTranslator:TranslateAndRegister(button2, CoreGui, button2Text)
			else
				button2.Text = button2Text
			end
		else
			button2 = createTextButtonOld("Button2", localizedButton2Text or button2Text, UDim2.new(0.5, 2, 1, 2))
		end
		button2.Parent = notificationFrame
		local button2ClickedConnection
		button2ClickedConnection = button2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if button2ClickedConnection then
				button2ClickedConnection:Disconnect()
				button2ClickedConnection = nil
				removeNotification(notification)
				if callback and typeof(callback) == "Instance" and callback:IsA("BindableFunction") then
					pcall(function() callback:Invoke(button2Text) end)
				elseif type(callback) == "function" then
					pcall(callback, button2Text)
				end
			end
		end)
	elseif button1 then
		button1.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 0.5, 0)
	end

	notification.Frame = notificationFrame
	notification.Duration = tonumber(notificationInfo.Duration) or DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION
	insertNotification(notification)
end

BindableEvent_SendNotificationInfo.Event:Connect(onSendNotificationInfo)

-- New follower notification.
task.spawn(function()
	if isTenFootInterface or not RobloxReplicatedStorage then
		return
	end
	local RemoteEvent_NewFollower = RobloxReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("NewFollower", 86400)
	if not RemoteEvent_NewFollower or not RemoteEvent_NewFollower:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		return
	end
	RemoteEvent_NewFollower.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(followerRbxPlayer)
		local message = ("%s is now following you"):format(followerRbxPlayer.Name)
		if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
			message = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.NewFollower", { RBX_NAME = followerRbxPlayer.Name })
		elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			message = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.NewFollower", message), "{RBX_NAME}", followerRbxPlayer.Name)
		end
		sendNotificationInfo({
			GroupName = "Friends",
			Title = "New Follower",
			Text = message,
			DetailText = message,
			Image = getFriendImage(followerRbxPlayer.UserId),
			Duration = 5,
		})
	end)
end)

local checkFriendRequestIsThrottled
do
	local friendRequestThrottlingMap = {}
	checkFriendRequestIsThrottled = function(fromPlayer)
		local throttleFinishedTime = friendRequestThrottlingMap[fromPlayer]
		if throttleFinishedTime and tick() &lt; throttleFinishedTime then
			return true
		end
		friendRequestThrottlingMap[fromPlayer] = tick() + FRIEND_REQUEST_NOTIFICATION_THROTTLE
		return false
	end
end

local function sendFriendNotification(fromPlayer)
	if checkFriendRequestIsThrottled(fromPlayer) then return end
	local acceptText = "Accept"
	local declineText = "Decline"
	sendNotificationInfo({
		GroupName = "Friends",
		Title = fromPlayer.Name,
		Text = "Sent you a friend request!",
		DetailText = fromPlayer.Name,
		Image = getFriendImage(fromPlayer.UserId),
		Duration = 8,
		Callback = function(buttonChosen)
			if buttonChosen == acceptText then
				reportAnalytics("ReportCounter", "NotificationScript-RequestFriendship")
				reportAnalytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "RequestFriendship", "NotificationScript")
				pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestFriendship(fromPlayer) end)
			else
				reportAnalytics("ReportCounter", "NotificationScript-RevokeFriendship")
				reportAnalytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "RevokeFriendship", "NotificationScript")
				pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RevokeFriendship(fromPlayer) end)
				FriendRequestBlacklist[fromPlayer] = true
			end
		end,
		Button1Text = acceptText,
		Button2Text = declineText,
	})
end

local function onFriendRequestEvent(fromPlayer, toPlayer, event)
	if fromPlayer ~= LocalPlayer and toPlayer ~= LocalPlayer then return end
	if fromPlayer == LocalPlayer then
		if event == Enum.FriendRequestEvent.Accept then
			local detailText = "You are now friends with " .. toPlayer.Name .. "!"
			if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
				detailText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.FriendRequestEvent.Accept", { RBX_NAME = toPlayer.Name })
			elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				detailText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.FriendRequestEvent.Accept", detailText), "{RBX_NAME}", toPlayer.Name)
			end
			sendNotificationInfo({ GroupName = "Friends", Title = "New Friend", Text = toPlayer.Name, DetailText = detailText, Image = getFriendImage(toPlayer.UserId), Duration = DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION })
		end
	elseif toPlayer == LocalPlayer then
		if event == Enum.FriendRequestEvent.Issue then
			if not FriendRequestBlacklist[fromPlayer] then
				sendFriendNotification(fromPlayer)
			end
		elseif event == Enum.FriendRequestEvent.Accept then
			local detailText = "You are now friends with " .. fromPlayer.Name .. "!"
			if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
				detailText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.FriendRequestEvent.Accept", { RBX_NAME = fromPlayer.Name })
			elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				detailText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.FriendRequestEvent.Accept", detailText), "{RBX_NAME}", fromPlayer.Name)
			end
			sendNotificationInfo({ GroupName = "Friends", Title = "New Friend", Text = fromPlayer.Name, DetailText = detailText, Image = getFriendImage(fromPlayer.UserId), Duration = DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION })
		end
	end
end

local function onPointsAwarded(userId, pointsAwarded, userBalanceInGame, userTotalBalance)
	if not pointsNotificationsActive or userId ~= LocalPlayer.UserId then return end
	local title, text
	if pointsAwarded == 1 then
		title = "Point Awarded"
		text = "You received 1 point!"
		if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
			text = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.single", { RBX_NUMBER = tostring(pointsAwarded) })
		elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			text = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.single", text), "{RBX_NUMBER}", tostring(pointsAwarded))
		end
	elseif pointsAwarded &gt; 0 then
		title = "Points Awarded"
		text = ("You received %d points!"):format(pointsAwarded)
		if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
			text = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.multiple", { RBX_NUMBER = tostring(pointsAwarded) })
		elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			text = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.multiple", text), "{RBX_NUMBER}", tostring(pointsAwarded))
		end
	elseif pointsAwarded &lt; 0 then
		title = "Points Lost"
		text = ("You lost %d points!"):format(math.abs(pointsAwarded))
		if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
			text = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.negative", { RBX_NUMBER = tostring(pointsAwarded) })
		elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			text = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("NotificationScript2.onPointsAwarded.negative", text), "{RBX_NUMBER}", tostring(math.abs(pointsAwarded)))
		end
	else
		return
	end
	sendNotificationInfo({ GroupName = "PlayerPoints", Title = title, Text = text, DetailText = text, Image = PLAYER_POINTS_IMG, Duration = DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION })
end

local function onBadgeAwarded(message, userId, badgeId)
	if not BadgeBlacklist[badgeId] and badgesNotificationsActive and userId == LocalPlayer.UserId then
		BadgeBlacklist[badgeId] = true
		sendNotificationInfo({ GroupName = "BadgeAwards", Title = "Badge Awarded", Text = message, DetailText = message, Image = BADGE_IMG, Duration = DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION })
	end
end

local function onGameSettingsChanged(property, amount)
	if property ~= "SavedQualityLevel" or not GameSettings then return end
	local savedQuality
	local ok = pcall(function() savedQuality = GameSettings.SavedQualityLevel.Value end)
	if not ok or type(savedQuality) ~= "number" then return end
	local level = savedQuality + amount
	if level &gt; 10 then level = 10 elseif level &lt; 1 then level = 1 end
	if level &gt; 0 and level ~= CurrentGraphicsQualityLevel and savedQuality ~= Enum.SavedQualitySetting.Automatic then
		local action = (level &gt; CurrentGraphicsQualityLevel) and "Increased" or "Decreased"
		local message = ("%s to (%d)"):format(action, level)
		if FFlagNotificationScript2UseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
			local key = (level &gt; CurrentGraphicsQualityLevel) and "NotificationScrip2.onCurrentGraphicsQualityLevelChanged.Increased" or "NotificationScrip2.onCurrentGraphicsQualityLevelChanged.Decreased"
			message = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey(key, { RBX_NUMBER = tostring(level) })
		elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
			local key = (level &gt; CurrentGraphicsQualityLevel) and "NotificationScrip2.onCurrentGraphicsQualityLevelChanged.Increased" or "NotificationScrip2.onCurrentGraphicsQualityLevelChanged.Decreased"
			message = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString(key, message), "{RBX_NUMBER}", tostring(level))
		end
		sendNotificationInfo({ GroupName = "Graphics", Title = "Graphics Quality", Text = message, DetailText = message, Image = "", Duration = 2 })
		CurrentGraphicsQualityLevel = level
	end
end

if BadgeService then
	pcall(function()
		BadgeService.BadgeAwarded:Connect(onBadgeAwarded)
	end)
end
if not isTenFootInterface then
	if Players then pcall(function() Players.FriendRequestEvent:Connect(onFriendRequestEvent) end) end
	if PointsService then pcall(function() PointsService.PointsAwarded:Connect(onPointsAwarded) end) end
	pcall(function()
		game.GraphicsQualityChangeRequest:Connect(function(graphicsIncrease)
			onGameSettingsChanged("SavedQualityLevel", graphicsIncrease == true and 1 or -1)
		end)
	end)
end

			Title = "Screenshot Taken",
			Text = "Check out your screenshots folder to see it.",
			Duration = 3.0,
			Button1Text = "Open Folder",
			Callback = function(text)
				if text == "Open Folder" then pcall(function() game:OpenScreenshotsFolder() end) end
			end,
		})
	end)
end)

pcall(function()
	local gameSettingsService = settings():GetService("GameSettings")
	gameSettingsService.VideoRecordingChangeRequest:Connect(function(value)
		if not value then
			sendNotificationInfo({
				Title = "Video Recorded",
				Text = "Check out your videos folder to see it.",
				Duration = 3.0,
				Button1Text = "Open Folder",
				Callback = function(text)
					if text == "Open Folder" then pcall(function() game:OpenVideosFolder() end) end
				end,
			})
		end
	end)
end)

if GuiService then
	pcall(function()
		GuiService.SendCoreUiNotification = function(title, text)
			local notification = createNotification(title, text, "")
			notification.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
			notification.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.1, 0)
			notification.Position = UDim2.new(0.25, 0, -0.1, 0)
			notification.NotificationTitle.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size36
			notification.NotificationText.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size24
			notification.Parent = RbxGui
			notification:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0.25, 0, 0, 0), EASE_DIR, EASE_STYLE, TWEEN_TIME, true)
			wait(5)
			if notification and notification.Parent then notification:Destroy() end
		end
	end)
end

-- Legacy center-dialog API used by CreatePlaceInPlayerInventoryAsync.
local function onClientLuaDialogRequested(msg, accept, decline)
	PopupText.Text = tostring(msg or "")
	local acceptCn, declineCn
	local function disconnectCns()
		if acceptCn then acceptCn:Disconnect() end
		if declineCn then declineCn:Disconnect() end
		if GuiService then pcall(function() GuiService:RemoveCenterDialog(PopupFrame) end) end
		PopupFrame.Visible = false
	end
	acceptCn = PopupAcceptButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		disconnectCns()
		pcall(function() MarketplaceService:SignalServerLuaDialogClosed(true) end)
	end)
	declineCn = PopupDeclineButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		disconnectCns()
		pcall(function() MarketplaceService:SignalServerLuaDialogClosed(false) end)
	end)
	local centerDialogSuccess = pcall(function()
		GuiService:AddCenterDialog(PopupFrame, Enum.CenterDialogType.QuitDialog,
			function()
				PopupOKButton.Visible = false
				PopupAcceptButton.Visible = true
				PopupDeclineButton.Visible = true
				PopupAcceptButton.Text = accept
				PopupDeclineButton.Text = decline
				PopupFrame.Visible = true
			end,
			function() PopupFrame.Visible = false end)
	end)
	if not centerDialogSuccess then
		PopupFrame.Visible = true
		PopupAcceptButton.Text = accept
		PopupDeclineButton.Text = decline
	end
	return true
end
if MarketplaceService then
	pcall(function() MarketplaceService.ClientLuaDialogRequested:Connect(onClientLuaDialogRequested) end)
end

local function createDeveloperNotification(notificationTable)
	if type(notificationTable) ~= "table" then return end
	if type(notificationTable.Title) ~= "string" or type(notificationTable.Text) ~= "string" then return end
	local iconImage = type(notificationTable.Icon) == "string" and notificationTable.Icon or ""
	local duration = type(notificationTable.Duration) == "number" and notificationTable.Duration or DEFAULT_NOTIFICATION_DURATION
	local bindable = typeof(notificationTable.Callback) == "Instance" and notificationTable.Callback:IsA("BindableFunction") and notificationTable.Callback or nil
	local button1Text = type(notificationTable.Button1) == "string" and notificationTable.Button1 or ""
	local button2Text = type(notificationTable.Button2) == "string" and notificationTable.Button2 or ""
	local autoLocalize = notificationTable.AutoLocalize == nil or notificationTable.AutoLocalize == true
	sendNotificationInfo({
		GroupName = "Developer",
		Title = notificationTable.Title,
		Text = notificationTable.Text,
		Image = iconImage,
		Duration = duration,
		Callback = bindable,
		Button1Text = button1Text,
		Button2Text = button2Text,
		AutoLocalize = autoLocalize,
	})
end

if StarterGui then
	pcall(function() StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("PointsNotificationsActive", function(value) if type(value) == "boolean" then pointsNotificationsActive = value end end) end)
	pcall(function() StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("BadgesNotificationsActive", function(value) if type(value) == "boolean" then badgesNotificationsActive = value end end) end)
	pcall(function() StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("PointsNotificationsActive", function() return pointsNotificationsActive end) end)
	pcall(function() StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("BadgesNotificationsActive", function() return badgesNotificationsActive end) end)
	pcall(function() StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("SendNotification", createDeveloperNotification) end)
end

if not isTenFootInterface then
	local coreScripts = RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("CoreScripts")
	local gamepadMenu = (coreScripts and coreScripts:FindFirstChild("GamepadMenu")) or RobloxGui:FindFirstChild("GamepadMenu")
	local gamepadNotifications = gamepadMenu and gamepadMenu:FindFirstChild("GamepadNotifications")

	if gamepadNotifications and gamepadNotifications:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		local leaveNotificationFunc = function(name, state, inputObject)
			if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return end
			local selected = GuiService and GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
			if selected and selected:IsDescendantOf(NotificationFrame) then
				GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
			end
			ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction("LeaveNotificationSelection")
		end

		gamepadNotifications.Event:Connect(function(isSelected)
			if not isSelected then return end
			isPaused = true
			local notifications = NotificationFrame:GetChildren()
			for i = 1, #notifications do
				local noteComponents = notifications[i]:GetChildren()
				for j = 1, #noteComponents do
					if noteComponents[j]:IsA("GuiButton") and noteComponents[j].Visible then
						GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = noteComponents[j]
						break
					end
				end
			end

			if GuiService and GuiService.SelectedCoreObject then
				ContextActionService:BindCoreAction("LeaveNotificationSelection", leaveNotificationFunc, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB)
			else
				isPaused = false
				local settingsModules = Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("Settings")
				local utilityModule = settingsModules and findModule(settingsModules, "Utility")
				local utility = safeRequire(utilityModule)
				if utility and type(utility.ShowAlert) == "function" then
					pcall(function() utility:ShowAlert("You have no notifications", "Ok", nil, function() end, true) end)
				end
			end
		end)

		if GuiService then
			GuiService.Changed:Connect(function(prop)
				if prop == "SelectedCoreObject" then
					local selected = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
					if not selected or not selected:IsDescendantOf(NotificationFrame) then
						isPaused = false
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	else
		warn("[NotificationScript2] GamepadNotifications is missing; gamepad notification focus handling is disabled.")
	end
end

local Platform = UserInputService and UserInputService:GetPlatform() or nil
local CSMModule = Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("ControllerStateManager")
if Modules and not CSMModule then
	local ShellModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("Shell")
	if ShellModules then CSMModule = ShellModules:FindFirstChild("ControllerStateManager") end
end

if Platform == Enum.Platform.XBoxOne and CSMModule then
	local PlatformService = getService("PlatformService")
	if PlatformService then
		local controllerStateManager = safeRequire(CSMModule)
		if controllerStateManager then
			pcall(function()
				controllerStateManager:Initialize()
				if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
				controllerStateManager:CheckUserConnected()
			end)
		end
	end
end

    Filename: PerformanceStatsManagerScript.lua
    Written by: dbanks
    Yeku NewRobloxGui compatibility patch.

    Uses the existing NewRobloxGui root and original Stats modules from the
    RBXM package. No FastFlag lookup is required for visibility handling.

--[[ Services ]]--
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local Settings = UserSettings()
local GameSettings = Settings and Settings.GameSettings
local CoreGuiService = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local CoreGuiRoot = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
if not CoreGuiRoot then
	CoreGuiRoot = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end
if not CoreGuiRoot then
	CoreGuiRoot = CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 15)
end
if not CoreGuiRoot or not CoreGuiRoot:IsA("ScreenGui") then 
	warn("[PerformanceStatsManagerScript] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing; performance stats were not started.")
	return
end

local function safeRequire(moduleScript, name)
	if not moduleScript or not moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		warn("[PerformanceStatsManagerScript] Missing module: " .. tostring(name))
		return nil
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not ok then
		warn("[PerformanceStatsManagerScript] Failed to require " .. tostring(name) .. ": " .. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end

	return result
end

local Modules = CoreGuiRoot:FindFirstChild("Modules")
local StatsFolder = Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("Stats")

-- Analytics is telemetry only; performance stats should still work without it.
local GoogleAnalyticsUtils = safeRequire(
	Modules and Modules:FindFirstChild("GoogleAnalyticsUtils"),
	"Modules.GoogleAnalyticsUtils"
) or { CA_CATEGORY_GAME = "Game" }

-- These modules implement the actual stats UI. Do not fetch substitute scripts.
local StatsAggregatorManagerClass = safeRequire(
	StatsFolder and StatsFolder:FindFirstChild("StatsAggregatorManager"),
	"Modules.Stats.StatsAggregatorManager"
)
local StatsButtonClass = safeRequire(
	StatsFolder and StatsFolder:FindFirstChild("StatsButton"),
	"Modules.Stats.StatsButton"
)
local StatsUtils = safeRequire(
	StatsFolder and StatsFolder:FindFirstChild("StatsUtils"),
	"Modules.Stats.StatsUtils"
)
local StatsViewerClass = safeRequire(
	StatsFolder and StatsFolder:FindFirstChild("StatsViewer"),
	"Modules.Stats.StatsViewer"
)

if not (StatsAggregatorManagerClass and StatsButtonClass and StatsUtils and StatsViewerClass) then
	warn("[PerformanceStatsManagerScript] One or more required Stats modules are missing. Check the original RBXM hierarchy under NewRobloxGui.Modules.Stats.")
	return
end

local function reportAnalytics(actionName, statType)
	pcall(function()
		game:ReportInGoogleAnalytics(
			GoogleAnalyticsUtils.CA_CATEGORY_GAME or "Game",
			actionName,
			statType or "",
			0
		)
	end)
end

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
local masterFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
masterFrame.Name = "PerformanceStats"

local statsAggregatorManager = StatsAggregatorManagerClass.getSingleton()
local statsViewer = StatsViewerClass.new()
local statsButtonsByType = {}
local currentStatType = nil
local OnButtonToggled

for _, statType in ipairs(StatsUtils.AllStatTypes) do
	statsButtonsByType[statType] = StatsButtonClass.new(statType)
end

local function ShowMasterFrame()
	masterFrame.Visible = true
	masterFrame.Parent = CoreGuiRoot
end

local function HideMasterFrame()
	masterFrame.Visible = false
	masterFrame.Parent = nil
end

--[[ Functions ]]--
local function ConfigureMasterFrame()
	masterFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	masterFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	masterFrame.Selectable = false
	masterFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	masterFrame.Active = false
	masterFrame.ZIndex = 0

	-- Start hidden. The visibility event below decides when to parent/show it.
	HideMasterFrame()
end

local function AddButton(statType, index)
	local button = statsButtonsByType[statType]
	if not button then
		return
	end

	button:SetParent(masterFrame)
	button:SetStatsAggregator(statsAggregatorManager:GetAggregator(statType))

	local buttonCount = tonumber(StatsUtils.NumButtonTypes) or #StatsUtils.AllStatTypes
	if buttonCount &lt;= 0 then
		warn("[PerformanceStatsManagerScript] StatsUtils.NumButtonTypes is not positive; cannot lay out stat buttons.")
		return
	end

	local fraction = 1 / buttonCount
	local size = UDim2.new(fraction, 0, 0, StatsUtils.ButtonHeight)
	local position = UDim2.new(fraction * (index - 1), 0, 0, 0)
	button:SetSizeAndPosition(size, position)
	button:SetToggleCallbackFunction(OnButtonToggled)
end

local function UpdateButtonSelectedStates()
	for _, buttonType in ipairs(StatsUtils.AllStatTypes) do
		local button = statsButtonsByType[buttonType]
		if button then
			button:SetIsSelected(buttonType == currentStatType)
		end
	end
end

local function UpdateViewerVisibility()
	if currentStatType == nil then
		if statsViewer:GetIsVisible() then
			statsViewer:SetVisible(false)
			statsViewer:SetStatsAggregator(nil)
		end
		return
	end

	local somethingChanged = false
	if not statsViewer:GetIsVisible() then
		somethingChanged = true
		statsViewer:SetVisible(true)
	end

	if currentStatType ~= statsViewer:GetStatType() then
		statsViewer:SetStatType(currentStatType)
		statsViewer:SetStatsAggregator(statsAggregatorManager:GetAggregator(currentStatType))
		somethingChanged = true
	end

	if somethingChanged then
		reportAnalytics("Enlarge PerfStat", currentStatType)
	end
end

OnButtonToggled = function(toggledStatType)
	local toggledButton = statsButtonsByType[toggledStatType]
	if not toggledButton then
		return
	end

	if not toggledButton._isSelected then
		currentStatType = toggledStatType
	else
		currentStatType = nil
	end

	UpdateButtonSelectedStates()
	UpdateViewerVisibility()
end

local function ConfigureStatButtonsInMasterFrame()
	for index, statType in ipairs(StatsUtils.AllStatTypes) do
		AddButton(statType, index)
	end
end

local function ConfigureStatViewerInMasterFrame()
	statsViewer:SetParent(masterFrame)
	local size = UDim2.new(0, StatsUtils.ViewerWidth, 0, StatsUtils.ViewerHeight)
	local position = UDim2.new(1, -StatsUtils.ViewerWidth, 0, StatsUtils.ButtonHeight + StatsUtils.ViewerTopMargin)
	statsViewer:SetSizeAndPosition(size, position)
end

local function UpdatePerformanceStatsVisibility()
	local shouldBeVisible = StatsUtils.PerformanceStatsShouldBeVisible()

	if shouldBeVisible then
		ShowMasterFrame()
	else
		HideMasterFrame()
	end

	statsViewer:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
	for _, buttonType in ipairs(StatsUtils.AllStatTypes) do
		local button = statsButtonsByType[buttonType]
		if button then
			button:OnPerformanceStatsShouldBeVisibleChanged()
		end
	end

	reportAnalytics(shouldBeVisible and "Show PerfStats" or "Hide PerfStats", "")
end

--[[ Top Level Code ]]--
ConfigureMasterFrame()
ConfigureStatButtonsInMasterFrame()
ConfigureStatViewerInMasterFrame()

-- Some runtimes do not expose this event. If unavailable, use the current state.
if GameSettings then
	pcall(function()
		GameSettings.PerformanceStatsVisibleChanged:Connect(UpdatePerformanceStatsVisibility)
	end)
end

UpdateButtonSelectedStates()
UpdateViewerVisibility()
UpdatePerformanceStatsVisibility()

task.spawn(function()
	local localPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
	while not localPlayer do
		PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Wait()
		localPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
	end
	UpdatePerformanceStatsVisibility()
end)

    Filename: BlockPlayerPrompt.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch for CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.
    Original behavior retained; dependencies are resolved from the copied legacy tree.
]]

local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGuiService = game:GetService("CoreGui")

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then
		return root
	end
	root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then
		return root
	end
	root = CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 20)
		or CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 10)
	assert(root, "BlockPlayerPrompt: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")
	return root
end

local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()
local LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
while LocalPlayer == nil do
	PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Wait()
	LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
end

local CoreGuiModules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(CoreGuiModules, "BlockPlayerPrompt: legacy Modules folder is missing under " .. RobloxGui:GetFullName())

local function requireLegacyModule(name)
	local module = CoreGuiModules:FindFirstChild(name)
	assert(module and module:IsA("ModuleScript"), "BlockPlayerPrompt: missing ModuleScript Modules." .. name)
	local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
	assert(ok, "BlockPlayerPrompt: require Modules." .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value))
	return value
end

local PromptCreator = requireLegacyModule("PromptCreator")
local SocialUtil = requireLegacyModule("SocialUtil")
local PlayerDropDownModule = requireLegacyModule("PlayerDropDown")
local BlockingUtility = PlayerDropDownModule:CreateBlockingUtility()

local THUMBNAIL_URL = "https://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Avatar.ashx?x=200&amp;y=200&amp;format=png&amp;userId="
local BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL = "https://www.roblox.com/bust-thumbnail/image?width=420&amp;height=420&amp;format=png&amp;userId="
local REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
local CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size352x352
local REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot
local CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail

pcall(function()
	PlayerDropDownModule:InitBlockListAsync()
end)

local function createFetchImageFunction(...)
	local args = {...}
	return function(imageLabel)
		task.spawn(function()
			local imageUrl
			local ok = pcall(function()
				imageUrl = SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage(unpack(args))
			end)
			if not ok or not imageUrl then
				local userId = args[1]
				local thumbOk, thumb = pcall(function()
					return PlayersService:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
						userId,
						args[3] or Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
						args[2] or Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
					)
				end)
				if thumbOk then imageUrl = thumb end
			end
			if imageLabel and imageLabel.Parent and imageUrl then
				pcall(function()
					if imageLabel:IsA("ImageLabel") or imageLabel:IsA("ImageButton") then
						imageLabel.Image = imageUrl
					elseif imageLabel:FindFirstChild("Icon") then
						imageLabel.Icon.Image = imageUrl
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function waitForPromptSlot()
	local deadline = os.clock() + 15
	while PromptCreator:IsCurrentlyPrompting() and os.clock() &lt; deadline do
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end

local function DoPromptBlockPlayer(playerToBlock)
	if BlockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(playerToBlock.UserId) then
		return
	end

	local function promptCompletedCallback(clickedConfirm)
		if not clickedConfirm then return end
		local callOk, successfullyBlocked = pcall(function()
			return BlockingUtility:BlockPlayerAsync(playerToBlock)
		end)
		if not callOk or not successfullyBlocked then
			waitForPromptSlot()
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Error Blocking Player",
				MainText = string.format("An error occurred while blocking %s. Please try again later.", playerToBlock.Name),
				ConfirmationText = "Okay",
				CancelActive = false,
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToBlock.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToBlock.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToBlock.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToBlock.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
		end
	end

	PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
		WindowTitle = "Confirm Block",
		MainText = string.format("Are you sure you want to block %s?", playerToBlock.Name),
		ConfirmationText = "Block",
		CancelText = "Cancel",
		CancelActive = true,
		Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToBlock.UserId,
		ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToBlock.UserId,
		FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToBlock.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToBlock.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		PromptCompletedCallback = promptCompletedCallback,
	})
end

local function PromptBlockPlayer(player)
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptBlockPlayer can not be called for guests!") end
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then
		error("Invalid argument to PromptBlockPlayer")
	end
	if player.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptBlockPlayer can not be called on guests!") end
	if player == LocalPlayer then error("PromptBlockPlayer: A user can not block themselves!") end
	DoPromptBlockPlayer(player)
end

local function DoPromptUnblockPlayer(playerToUnblock)
	if not BlockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(playerToUnblock.UserId) then
		return
	end

	local function promptCompletedCallback(clickedConfirm)
		if not clickedConfirm then return end
		local callOk, successfullyUnblocked = pcall(function()
			return BlockingUtility:UnblockPlayerAsync(playerToUnblock)
		end)
		if not callOk or not successfullyUnblocked then
			waitForPromptSlot()
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Error Unblocking Player",
				MainText = string.format("An error occurred while unblocking %s. Please try again later.", playerToUnblock.Name),
				ConfirmationText = "Okay",
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnblock.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnblock.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnblock.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnblock.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
		end
	end

	PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
		WindowTitle = "Confirm Unblock",
		MainText = string.format("Would you like to unblock %s?", playerToUnblock.Name),
		ConfirmationText = "Unblock",
		CancelText = "Cancel",
		CancelActive = true,
		Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnblock.UserId,
		ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnblock.UserId,
		FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnblock.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnblock.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		PromptCompletedCallback = promptCompletedCallback,
	})
end

local function PromptUnblockPlayer(player)
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptUnblockPlayer can not be called for guests!") end
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then
		error("Invalid argument to PromptUnblockPlayer")
	end
	if player.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptUnblockPlayer can not be called on guests!") end
	if player == LocalPlayer then error("PromptUnblockPlayer: A user can not block themselves!") end
	DoPromptUnblockPlayer(player)
end

local function GetBlockedUserIds()
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 then error("GetBlockedUserIds can not be called for guests!") end
	return BlockingUtility:GetBlockedUserIdsAsync()
end

StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("PromptBlockPlayer", PromptBlockPlayer)
StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("PromptUnblockPlayer", PromptUnblockPlayer)
StarterGui:RegisterGetCore("GetBlockedUserIds", GetBlockedUserIds)

local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGuiService = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local AnalyticsService = game:GetService("AnalyticsService")

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 20)
		or CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 10)
	assert(root, "FriendPlayerPrompt: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")

	PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Wait()
	LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer

local CoreGuiModules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(CoreGuiModules, "FriendPlayerPrompt: legacy Modules folder is missing.")

local function requireLegacyModule(name, optional)
	local module = CoreGuiModules:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		if optional then return nil end
		error("FriendPlayerPrompt: missing ModuleScript Modules." .. name, 2)
	end
	local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
	if not ok then
		if optional then
			warn("FriendPlayerPrompt: optional module " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value))
			return nil
		end
		error("FriendPlayerPrompt: require Modules." .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value), 2)
	end
	return value
end

local PromptCreator = requireLegacyModule("PromptCreator")
local SocialUtil = requireLegacyModule("SocialUtil", true)
local PlayerDropDownModule = requireLegacyModule("PlayerDropDown")

local function getFlag(name)
	local ok, value = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag(name) end)
	return ok and value == true
end

local FFlagCoreScriptsUseLocalizationModule = false -- Legacy 2015-2017 UI is English-only.
local FFlagFriendPlayerPromptUseFormatByKey = false
local RobloxTranslator = nil
if FFlagCoreScriptsUseLocalizationModule then
	RobloxTranslator = requireLegacyModule("RobloxTranslator", true)
end

local THUMBNAIL_URL = "https://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Avatar.ashx?x=200&amp;y=200&amp;format=png&amp;userId="
local BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL = "https://www.roblox.com/bust-thumbnail/image?width=420&amp;height=420&amp;format=png&amp;userId="
local REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size150x150
local CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE = Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size352x352
local REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot
local CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE = Enum.ThumbnailType.AvatarThumbnail

local success, result = pcall(function() return settings():GetFFlag("UseNotificationsLocalization") end)
local FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization = false

local function LocalizedGetString(key, fallback)
	local resultText = fallback
	pcall(function()
		if FFlagCoreScriptsUseLocalizationModule and RobloxTranslator then
			resultText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey(key)
		else
			local localizationService = game:GetService("LocalizationService")
			local localizations = localizationService:GetCorescriptLocalizations()
			local localization = localizations and localizations[1]
			if localization then
				resultText = localization:GetString("en-us", key)
			end
		end
	end)
	return resultText or fallback
end

local function createFetchImageFunction(...)
	local args = {...}
	return function(imageLabel)
		task.spawn(function()
			local imageUrl
			if SocialUtil and type(SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage) == "function" then
				pcall(function() imageUrl = SocialUtil.GetPlayerImage(unpack(args)) end)
			end
			if not imageUrl then
				local ok, image = pcall(function()
					return PlayersService:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
						args[1], args[3] or REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE,
						args[2] or REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE
					)
				end)
				if ok then imageUrl = image end
			end
			if imageLabel and imageLabel.Parent and imageUrl then
				pcall(function()
					if imageLabel:IsA("ImageLabel") or imageLabel:IsA("ImageButton") then
						imageLabel.Image = imageUrl
					elseif imageLabel:FindFirstChild("Icon") then
						imageLabel.Icon.Image = imageUrl
					end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local function reportAnalytics(methodName, ...)
	local args = {...}
	pcall(function()
		local method = AnalyticsService[methodName]
		if type(method) == "function" then method(AnalyticsService, unpack(args)) end
	end)
end

local function SendFriendRequest(playerToFriend)
	reportAnalytics("ReportCounter", "FriendPlayerPrompt-RequestFriendship")
	reportAnalytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "RequestFriendship", "FriendPlayerPrompt")
	local ok, resultValue = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:RequestFriendship(playerToFriend) end)
	return ok and resultValue ~= false
end

local function AtFriendLimit(player)
	local ok, friendCount = pcall(function() return PlayerDropDownModule:GetFriendCountAsync(player) end)
	if not ok or friendCount == nil then return false end
	local maxOk, maxCount = pcall(function() return PlayerDropDownModule:MaxFriendCount() end)
	return maxOk and friendCount &gt;= maxCount
end

local function waitForPromptSlot()
	local deadline = os.clock() + 15
	while PromptCreator:IsCurrentlyPrompting() and os.clock() &lt; deadline do
		task.wait(0.1)
	end
end

local function DoPromptRequestFriendPlayer(playerToFriend)
	local isFriendsOk, alreadyFriends = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(playerToFriend.UserId) end)
	if isFriendsOk and alreadyFriends then return end

	local function promptCompletedCallback(clickedConfirm)
		if not clickedConfirm then return end
		if AtFriendLimit(LocalPlayer) then
			waitForPromptSlot()
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Friend Limit Reached",
				MainText = "You can not send a friend request because you are at the max friend limit.",
				ConfirmationText = "Okay", CancelActive = false,
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
			return
		end

		if AtFriendLimit(playerToFriend) then
			local mainText = string.format("You can not send a friend request to %s because they are at the max friend limit.", playerToFriend.Name)
			if FFlagFriendPlayerPromptUseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
				pcall(function() mainText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("FriendPlayerPrompt.promptCompletedCallback.AtFriendLimit", {RBX_NAME = playerToFriend.Name}) end)
			elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				mainText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("FriendPlayerPrompt.promptCompletedCallback.AtFriendLimit", mainText), "{RBX_NAME}", playerToFriend.Name)
			end
			waitForPromptSlot()
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Error Sending Friend Request", MainText = mainText,
				ConfirmationText = "Okay", CancelActive = false,
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
			return
		end

		if not SendFriendRequest(playerToFriend) then
			waitForPromptSlot()
			local mainText = string.format("An error occurred while sending %s a friend request. Please try again later.", playerToFriend.Name)
			if FFlagFriendPlayerPromptUseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
				pcall(function() mainText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("FriendPlayerPrompt.promptCompletedCallback.UnknownError", {RBX_NAME = playerToFriend.Name}) end)
			elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				mainText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("FriendPlayerPrompt.promptCompletedCallback.UnknownError", mainText), "{RBX_NAME}", playerToFriend.Name)
			end
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Error Sending Friend Request", MainText = mainText,
				ConfirmationText = "Okay", CancelActive = false,
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
		end
	end

	local mainText = string.format("Would you like to send %s a Friend Request?", playerToFriend.Name)
	if FFlagFriendPlayerPromptUseFormatByKey and RobloxTranslator then
		pcall(function() mainText = RobloxTranslator:FormatByKey("FriendPlayerPrompt.DoPromptRequestFriendPlayer", {RBX_NAME = playerToFriend.Name}) end)
	elseif FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		mainText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("FriendPlayerPrompt.DoPromptRequestFriendPlayer", mainText), "{RBX_NAME}", playerToFriend.Name)
	end

	PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
		WindowTitle = "Send Friend Request?", MainText = mainText,
		ConfirmationText = "Send Request", CancelText = "Cancel", CancelActive = true,
		Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
		ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToFriend.UserId,
		FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToFriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		PromptCompletedCallback = promptCompletedCallback,
	})
end

local function PromptRequestFriendPlayer(player)
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptSendFriendRequest can not be called for guests!") end
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then error("Invalid argument to PromptSendFriendRequest") end
	if player.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptSendFriendRequest can not be called on guests!") end
	if player == LocalPlayer then error("PromptSendFriendRequest: A user can not friend themselves!") end
	DoPromptRequestFriendPlayer(player)
end

local function UnFriendPlayer(playerToUnfriend)
	local ok, resultValue = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:RevokeFriendship(playerToUnfriend) end)
	return ok and resultValue ~= false
end

local function DoPromptUnfriendPlayer(playerToUnfriend)
	local isFriendsOk, areFriends = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer:IsFriendsWith(playerToUnfriend.UserId) end)
	if not isFriendsOk or not areFriends then return end

	local function promptCompletedCallback(clickedConfirm)
		if not clickedConfirm then return end
		if not UnFriendPlayer(playerToUnfriend) then
			waitForPromptSlot()
			local mainText = string.format("An error occurred while unfriending %s. Please try again later.", playerToUnfriend.Name)
			if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
				mainText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("FriendPlayerPrompt.promptCompletedCallback.UnknownError", mainText), "{RBX_NAME}", playerToUnfriend.Name)
			end
			PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
				WindowTitle = "Error Unfriending Player", MainText = mainText,
				ConfirmationText = "Okay", CancelActive = false,
				Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnfriend.UserId,
				ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnfriend.UserId,
				FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnfriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnfriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
				StripeColor = Color3.fromRGB(183, 34, 54),
			})
		end
	end

	local mainText = string.format("Would you like to remove %s from your friends list?", playerToUnfriend.Name)
	if FFlagUseNotificationsLocalization then
		mainText = string.gsub(LocalizedGetString("FriendPlayerPrompt.DoPromptUnfriendPlayer", mainText), "{RBX_NAME}", playerToUnfriend.Name)
	end
	PromptCreator:CreatePrompt({
		WindowTitle = "Unfriend Player?", MainText = mainText,
		ConfirmationText = "Unfriend", CancelText = "Cancel", CancelActive = true,
		Image = BUST_THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnfriend.UserId,
		ImageConsoleVR = THUMBNAIL_URL .. playerToUnfriend.UserId,
		FetchImageFunction = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnfriend.UserId, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, REGULAR_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		FetchImageFunctionConsoleVR = createFetchImageFunction(playerToUnfriend.UserId, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_SIZE, CONSOLE_THUMBNAIL_IMAGE_TYPE),
		PromptCompletedCallback = promptCompletedCallback,
	})
end

local function PromptUnfriendPlayer(player)
	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptUnfriend can not be called for guests!") end
	if typeof(player) ~= "Instance" or not player:IsA("Player") then error("Invalid argument to PromptUnfriend") end
	if player.UserId &lt; 0 then error("PromptUnfriend can not be called on guests!") end
	if player == LocalPlayer then error("PromptUnfriend: A user can not unfriend themselves!") end
	DoPromptUnfriendPlayer(player)
end

StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("PromptSendFriendRequest", PromptRequestFriendPlayer)
StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("PromptUnfriend", PromptUnfriendPlayer)

                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: AvatarContextMenu.lua
    Yeku compatibility patch for CoreGui.NewRobloxGui.
    Original avatar context menu interaction retained.
]]

-- OPTIONS
local DEBUG_MODE = game:GetService("RunService"):IsStudio()
local isAvatarContextMenuEnabled = false

-- CONSTANTS
local MAX_CONTEXT_MENU_DISTANCE = 100
local OPEN_MENU_TIME = 0.2
local OPEN_MENU_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(OPEN_MENU_TIME, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
local CLOSE_MENU_TIME = 0.2
local CLOSE_MENU_TWEEN = TweenInfo.new(CLOSE_MENU_TIME, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
local LEAVE_MENU_ACTION_NAME = "EscapeAvatarContextMenu"
local MAX_MOVEMENT_THRESHOLD = 20

-- SERVICES
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local PlayersService = game:GetService("Players")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local CoreGuiService = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local AnalyticsService = game:GetService("AnalyticsService")

-- Register these before requiring modules so first-frame SetCore calls are queued.
local TempSetCoreQueue = {}
local function QueueSetCoreMethod(methodName, args)
	if TempSetCoreQueue[methodName] == nil then TempSetCoreQueue[methodName] = {} end
	table.insert(TempSetCoreQueue[methodName], args)
end

StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("AddAvatarContextMenuOption", function(...) QueueSetCoreMethod("AddAvatarContextMenuOption", {...}) end)
StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("RemoveAvatarContextMenuOption", function(...) QueueSetCoreMethod("RemoveAvatarContextMenuOption", {...}) end)

local hasTrackedAvatarContextMenu = false
StarterGui:RegisterSetCore("SetAvatarContextMenuEnabled", function(enabled)
	isAvatarContextMenuEnabled = not not enabled
	if isAvatarContextMenuEnabled and not hasTrackedAvatarContextMenu then
		hasTrackedAvatarContextMenu = true
		pcall(function()
			AnalyticsService:TrackEvent("Game", "AvatarContextMenuEnabled", "placeId: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId))
		end)
	end
end)

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 20)
		or CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 10)
	assert(root, "AvatarContextMenu: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")
	return root
end

local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()
local CoreGuiModules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules", 15)
assert(CoreGuiModules, "AvatarContextMenu: legacy Modules folder is missing.")
local AvatarMenuModules = CoreGuiModules:WaitForChild("AvatarContextMenu", 15)
assert(AvatarMenuModules, "AvatarContextMenu: Modules.AvatarContextMenu is missing.")

local function requireLegacyModule(parent, name, optional)
	local module = parent and parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		if optional then return nil end
		error("AvatarContextMenu: missing ModuleScript " .. tostring(parent and parent:GetFullName() or "&lt;nil&gt;") .. "." .. name, 2)
	end
	local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
	if not ok then
		if optional then
			warn("AvatarContextMenu: optional module " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value))
			return nil
		end
		error("AvatarContextMenu: require " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value), 2)
	end
	return value
end

local ContextMenuGui = requireLegacyModule(AvatarMenuModules, "ContextMenuGui")
local ContextMenuItemsModule = requireLegacyModule(AvatarMenuModules, "ContextMenuItems")
local ContextMenuUtil = requireLegacyModule(AvatarMenuModules, "ContextMenuUtil")
local SelectedCharacterIndicator = requireLegacyModule(AvatarMenuModules, "SelectedCharacterIndicator")

local LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
while not LocalPlayer do
	PlayersService.PlayerAdded:Wait()
	LocalPlayer = PlayersService.LocalPlayer
end

if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 and not DEBUG_MODE then return end

local ContextMenuItems = nil
local ContextMenuFrame = nil
local ContextMenuOpening = false
local ContextMenuOpen = false
local SelectedPlayer = nil
local lastInputObject = nil
local initialScreenPoint = nil
local hasTouchSwipeInput = nil
local contextMenuPlayerChangedConn = nil

ContextMenuFrame = ContextMenuGui:CreateMenuFrame()
ContextMenuItems = ContextMenuItemsModule.new(ContextMenuFrame.Content.ContextActionList)

for setCoreMethod, queue in pairs(TempSetCoreQueue) do
	for i = 1, #queue do
		pcall(function() StarterGui:SetCore(setCoreMethod, unpack(queue[i])) end)
	end
end

local function SetSelectedPlayer(player, dontTween)
	if SelectedPlayer == player then return end
	SelectedPlayer = player
	SelectedCharacterIndicator:ChangeSelectedPlayer(SelectedPlayer)
	ContextMenuItems:BuildContextMenuItems(SelectedPlayer)
	ContextMenuGui:SwitchToPlayerEntry(SelectedPlayer, dontTween)
end

local CloseContextMenu

local function OpenMenu()
	ContextMenuOpening = true
	ContextMenuFrame.Visible = true
	ContextMenuFrame.Content.ContextActionList.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	ContextMenuFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, ContextMenuFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y)

	contextMenuPlayerChangedConn = ContextMenuGui.SelectedPlayerChanged:Connect(function()
		SetSelectedPlayer(ContextMenuGui:GetSelectedPlayer())
	end)

	local positionTween = TweenService:Create(ContextMenuFrame, OPEN_MENU_TWEEN, {
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1 - ContextMenuGui:GetBottomScreenPaddingConstant(), 0),
	})
	positionTween:Play()
	positionTween.Completed:Wait()
	ContextMenuOpening = false
end

local function BindMenuActions()
	local closeMenuFunc = function(actionName, inputState, input)
		if inputState ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return end
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(LEAVE_MENU_ACTION_NAME)
		CloseContextMenu()
	end

	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(LEAVE_MENU_ACTION_NAME, closeMenuFunc, false, Enum.KeyCode.Escape)
	local menuOpenedConnection
	menuOpenedConnection = GuiService.MenuOpened:Connect(function()
		if menuOpenedConnection then menuOpenedConnection:Disconnect() end
		closeMenuFunc(nil, Enum.UserInputState.Begin, nil)
	end)
end

local function BuildPlayerCarousel(selectedPlayer, worldPoint)
	local playersByProximity = {}
	local players = PlayersService:GetPlayers()
	for i = 1, #players do
		if players[i].UserId &gt; 0 and players[i] ~= LocalPlayer then
			local playerPosition = ContextMenuUtil:GetPlayerPosition(players[i])
			if playerPosition then
				local distanceFromClicked = (worldPoint - playerPosition).Magnitude
				table.insert(playersByProximity, {players[i], distanceFromClicked})
			end
		end
	end
	table.sort(playersByProximity, function(playerA, playerB)
		return playerA[2] &gt; playerB[2]
	end)
	ContextMenuGui:BuildPlayerCarousel(playersByProximity)
end

local function OpenContextMenu(player, worldPoint)
	if ContextMenuOpening or ContextMenuOpen or not isAvatarContextMenuEnabled then return end
	ContextMenuOpen = true
	BuildPlayerCarousel(player, worldPoint)
	ContextMenuUtil:DisablePlayerMovement()
	BindMenuActions()
	SetSelectedPlayer(player, true)
	OpenMenu()
end

CloseContextMenu = function()
	GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = nil
	ContextMenuUtil:EnablePlayerMovement()
	if contextMenuPlayerChangedConn then
		contextMenuPlayerChangedConn:Disconnect()
		contextMenuPlayerChangedConn = nil
	end
	local positionTween = TweenService:Create(ContextMenuFrame, CLOSE_MENU_TWEEN, {
		Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, ContextMenuFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y),
	})
	positionTween:Play()
	positionTween.Completed:Wait()
	ContextMenuFrame.Visible = false
	SetSelectedPlayer(nil)
	ContextMenuOpen = false
end

ContextMenuGui:SetCloseMenuFunc(CloseContextMenu)
ContextMenuItems:SetCloseMenuFunc(CloseContextMenu)

local function isPointInside(point, topLeft, bottomRight)
	return point.X &gt;= topLeft.X and point.X &lt;= bottomRight.X
		and point.Y &gt;= topLeft.Y and point.Y &lt;= bottomRight.Y
end

local function PointInSwipeArea(screenPoint)
	local topLeft = ContextMenuFrame.AbsolutePosition
	local nameTag = ContextMenuFrame.Content:FindFirstChild("NameTag")
	if not nameTag then return false end
	local bottomRight = Vector2.new(
		topLeft.X + ContextMenuFrame.AbsoluteSize.X,
		nameTag.AbsolutePosition.Y + nameTag.AbsoluteSize.Y
	)
	return isPointInside(screenPoint, topLeft, bottomRight)
end

local function LocalPlayerHasToolEquipped()
	if not LocalPlayer.Character then return false end
	for _, child in ipairs(LocalPlayer.Character:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("BackpackItem") or child:IsA("Tool") then return true end
	end
	return false
end

local function clickedOnPoint(screenPoint)
	local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
	if not camera or LocalPlayerHasToolEquipped() then return end
	local ray = camera:ScreenPointToRay(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y)
	ray = Ray.new(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * MAX_CONTEXT_MENU_DISTANCE)
	local hitPart, hitPoint = workspace:FindPartOnRay(ray, nil, false, true)
	local player = ContextMenuUtil:FindPlayerFromPart(hitPart)
	if player and player ~= LocalPlayer and (DEBUG_MODE or player.UserId &gt; 0) then
		if ContextMenuOpen then SetSelectedPlayer(player) else OpenContextMenu(player, hitPoint) end
	elseif not player and ContextMenuOpen then
		CloseContextMenu()
	end
end

local function OnUserInput(screenPoint, inputObject)
	if inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin and lastInputObject == nil then
		lastInputObject = inputObject
		initialScreenPoint = screenPoint
	elseif lastInputObject == inputObject and inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Change then
		if (screenPoint - initialScreenPoint).Magnitude &gt; 5 then
			lastInputObject, initialScreenPoint = nil, nil
		end
	elseif inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End and lastInputObject == inputObject then
		lastInputObject, initialScreenPoint = nil, nil
		clickedOnPoint(screenPoint)
	end
end

local function OnMouseMoved(screenPoint)
	if not ContextMenuOpen and lastInputObject and (screenPoint - initialScreenPoint).Magnitude &gt; MAX_MOVEMENT_THRESHOLD then
		lastInputObject, initialScreenPoint = nil, nil
	end
end

local function trackTouchSwipeInput(inputObject)
	if inputObject.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
	if not hasTouchSwipeInput and inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then
		if PointInSwipeArea(inputObject.Position) then hasTouchSwipeInput = inputObject end
	elseif hasTouchSwipeInput == inputObject and inputObject.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
		task.spawn(function() hasTouchSwipeInput = nil end)
	end
end

local function functionProcessInput(inputObject, gameProcessedEvent)
	trackTouchSwipeInput(inputObject)
	if gameProcessedEvent then return end
	if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
		OnUserInput(Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.X, inputObject.Position.Y), inputObject)
	elseif inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		OnMouseMoved(Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.X, inputObject.Position.Y))
	end
end

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(functionProcessInput)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(functionProcessInput)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(functionProcessInput)

pcall(function()
	UserInputService.TouchSwipe:Connect(function(swipeDir, numOfTouches, gameProcessedEvent)
		if not gameProcessedEvent or not ContextMenuOpen or not hasTouchSwipeInput then return end
		local offset = 0
		if swipeDir == Enum.SwipeDirection.Left then offset = 1
		elseif swipeDir == Enum.SwipeDirection.Right then offset = -1 end
		if offset ~= 0 then
			ContextMenuGui:OffsetPlayerEntry(offset)
			SetSelectedPlayer(ContextMenuGui:GetSelectedPlayer())
		end
	end)
end)

pcall(function()
	LocalPlayer.FriendStatusChanged:Connect(function(player, friendStatus)
		if player and player == SelectedPlayer then ContextMenuItems:UpdateFriendButton(friendStatus) end
	end)
end)

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

	Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()

local function resolveRobloxGui()
	local root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root and root:IsA("ScreenGui") then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
	if root then return root end
	root = CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("NewRobloxGui", 20)
		or CoreGuiService:WaitForChild("RobloxGui", 10)
	assert(root, "VehicleHud: CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing.")
	return root
end

local RobloxGui = resolveRobloxGui()

local VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn = nil
local VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn = nil

	local ok, result = pcall(require, tenFootModule)
	if ok and result and type(result.IsEnabled) == "function" then
		local enabledOk, enabled = pcall(function() return result:IsEnabled() end)
		isTenFootInterface = enabledOk and enabled == true
	else
		warn("VehicleHud: TenFootInterface unavailable; using desktop dimensions.")
	end
else
	warn("VehicleHud: missing TenFootInterface; using desktop dimensions.")
end

local function createImageLabel(name, size, position, image, parent)
	local imageLabel = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	imageLabel.Name = name
	imageLabel.Size = size
	imageLabel.Position = position
	imageLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	imageLabel.Image = image
	imageLabel.Parent = parent
	return imageLabel
end

local function createTextLabel(name, alignment, text, parent)
	local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	textLabel.Name = name
	textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, isTenFootInterface and 50 or 20)
	textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, isTenFootInterface and -50 or -20)
	textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	textLabel.TextXAlignment = alignment
	textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	textLabel.FontSize = isTenFootInterface and Enum.FontSize.Size48 or Enum.FontSize.Size18
	textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(49/255, 49/255, 49/255)
	textLabel.Text = text
	textLabel.Parent = parent
	return textLabel
end

local VehicleHudFrame = Instance.new("Frame")

local function getHumanoid()
	local character = LocalPlayer and LocalPlayer.Character
	if character then
		return character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
	end
end

local function onHeartbeat()
	local seat = CurrentVehicleSeat
	if not seat or not seat.Parent then return end
	local speed = 0
	pcall(function()
		speed = seat.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude
	end)
	if speed == 0 then
		pcall(function() speed = seat.Velocity.Magnitude end)
	end
	SpeedText.Text = tostring(math.min(math.floor(speed), 9999))
	local maxSpeed = 0
	pcall(function() maxSpeed = seat.MaxSpeed end)
	local drawSize = 0
	if maxSpeed &gt; 0 then drawSize = math.floor((speed / maxSpeed) * SpeedBG.Size.X.Offset) end
	drawSize = math.clamp(drawSize, 0, SpeedBG.Size.X.Offset)
	speedBarClippingFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, drawSize, 0, isTenFootInterface and 24 or 4)
end

local function onVehicleSeatChanged(property)
	if property == "HeadsUpDisplay" and CurrentVehicleSeat then
		VehicleHudFrame.Visible = CurrentVehicleSeat.HeadsUpDisplay == true
	end
end

local function onSeated(active, currentSeatPart)
	if active and currentSeatPart and currentSeatPart:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
		if CurrentVehicleSeat == currentSeatPart then return end
		if VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn then VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn:Disconnect(); VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn = nil end
		if VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn then VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn:Disconnect(); VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn = nil end
		CurrentVehicleSeat = currentSeatPart
		VehicleHudFrame.Visible = currentSeatPart.HeadsUpDisplay
		VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(onHeartbeat)
		VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn = currentSeatPart.Changed:Connect(onVehicleSeatChanged)
	elseif not active then
		VehicleHudFrame.Visible = false
		CurrentVehicleSeat = nil
		if VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn then VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn:Disconnect(); VehicleSeatHeartbeatCn = nil end
		if VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn then VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn:Disconnect(); VehicleSeatHUDChangedCn = nil end
	end
end

local seatedConnection
local function connectSeated()
	local humanoid = getHumanoid()
	if not humanoid then
		local character = LocalPlayer.Character
		if character then humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10) end
	end
	if humanoid then
		if seatedConnection then seatedConnection:Disconnect() end
		seatedConnection = humanoid.Seated:Connect(onSeated)
	end
end

if LocalPlayer.Character then connectSeated() end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
	onSeated(false)
	connectSeated()
end)

local GuiRoot = resolveRobloxGui()
local function requireLegacyModule(pathRoot, name, optional)
	local module = pathRoot and pathRoot:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not module or not module:IsA("ModuleScript") then
		if optional then return nil end
		error("GamepadMenu: required ModuleScript " .. tostring(pathRoot and pathRoot:GetFullName() or "&lt;nil&gt;") .. "." .. name .. " is missing", 2)
	end
	local ok, value = pcall(require, module)
	if not ok then
		if optional then
			warn("GamepadMenu: optional module " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value))
			return nil
		end
		error("GamepadMenu: requiring " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(value), 2)
	end
	return value
end

	Notifications = {Range = {Begin = 112.5, End = 157.5}}, LeaveGame = {Range = {Begin = 157.5, End = 202.5}},
