local freezeControllerActionName = "doNothingAction"
local radialSelectActionName = "RadialSelectAction"
local thumbstick2RadialActionName = "Thumbstick2RadialAction"
local radialCancelActionName = "RadialSelectCancel"
local radialAcceptActionName = "RadialSelectAccept"
local toggleMenuActionName = "RBXToggleMenuAction"
local noOpFunc = function() end
local doGamepadMenuButton = nil
local toggleCoreGuiRadial = nil

local function safeCreate(className, props)
	local ok, result = pcall(function() return utility:Create(className)(props) end)
	if ok then return result end
	error("GamepadMenu: utility:Create failed for " .. className .. ": " .. tostring(result), 2)
end

local function getSelectedObjectFromAngle(angle)
	local closest = nil
	local closestDistance = 30
	local currentLayout = VRService.VREnabled and vrButtonLayout or radialButtonLayout
	for radialKey, buttonLayout in pairs(currentLayout) do
		local button = gamepadSettingsFrame[radialKey]
		if button and radialButtons[button] and radialButtons[button].Disabled == false then
			if buttonLayout.Range.Begin &lt; buttonLayout.Range.End then
				if angle &gt; buttonLayout.Range.Begin and angle &lt;= buttonLayout.Range.End then return button end
			else
				if angle &gt; buttonLayout.Range.Begin or angle &lt;= buttonLayout.Range.End then return button end
			end
			local distanceBegin = math.min(math.abs((buttonLayout.Range.Begin + 360) - angle), math.abs(buttonLayout.Range.Begin - angle))
			local distanceEnd = math.min(math.abs((buttonLayout.Range.End + 360) - angle), math.abs(buttonLayout.Range.End - angle))
			local distance = math.min(distanceBegin, distanceEnd)
			if distance &lt; closestDistance then closestDistance = distance; closest = button end
		end
	end
	return closest
end

local function setSelectedRadialButton(selectedObject)
	for button in pairs(radialButtons) do
		local selected = button == selectedObject
		local selectedImage = button:FindFirstChild("Selected")
		local label = button:FindFirstChild("RadialLabel")
		if selectedImage then selectedImage.Visible = selected end
		if label then label.Visible = selected end
		if VRService.VREnabled then button.ImageTransparency = selected and 1 or 0 end
	end
end

local function activateSelectedRadialButton()
	for button, buttonTable in pairs(radialButtons) do
		local selected = button:FindFirstChild("Selected")
		if selected and selected.Visible then buttonTable.Function(); return true end
	end
	return false
end

local function radialSelectAccept(name, state, input)
	if gamepadSettingsFrame and gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible and state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then activateSelectedRadialButton() end
end
local function radialSelectCancel(name, state, input)
	if gamepadSettingsFrame and gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible and state == Enum.UserInputState.Begin then toggleCoreGuiRadial() end
end

local D_PAD_VR_DIRS = {
	[Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp] = Vector2.new(0, 1), [Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown] = Vector2.new(0, -1),
	[Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight] = Vector2.new(1, 0), [Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft] = Vector2.new(-1, 0),
}

local function radialSelect(name, state, input)
	local inputVector = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1 then
		inputVector = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)
	elseif D_PAD_VR_DIRS[input.KeyCode] then
		local D_PAD_BUTTONS = {[Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp] = false, [Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown] = false, [Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft] = false, [Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight] = false}
		local ok, gamepadState = pcall(function() return InputService:GetGamepadState(input.UserInputType) end)
		if ok then
			for _, value in ipairs(gamepadState) do
				if D_PAD_BUTTONS[value.KeyCode] ~= nil then D_PAD_BUTTONS[value.KeyCode] = value.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.Begin end
			end
		end
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			for keyCode, value in pairs(D_PAD_BUTTONS) do if value then inputVector = inputVector + D_PAD_VR_DIRS[keyCode] end end
		elseif D_PAD_BUTTONS[Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp] or D_PAD_BUTTONS[Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown] then
			inputVector = D_PAD_BUTTONS[Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp] and Vector2.new(0, 1) or Vector2.new(0, -1)
			if D_PAD_BUTTONS[Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft] then inputVector = Vector2.new(-1, inputVector.Y)
			elseif D_PAD_BUTTONS[Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight] then inputVector = Vector2.new(1, inputVector.Y) end
		end
		if inputVector.Magnitude &gt; 0 then inputVector = inputVector.Unit end
	end
	if inputVector.Magnitude &gt; 0.8 then
		local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(inputVector.X, inputVector.Y))
		if angle &lt; 0 then angle = angle + 360 end
		setSelectedRadialButton(getSelectedObjectFromAngle(angle))
	end
end

local function unbindAllRadialActions()
	GuiService.CoreGuiNavigationEnabled = true
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(radialSelectActionName)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(radialCancelActionName)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(radialAcceptActionName)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(freezeControllerActionName)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(thumbstick2RadialActionName)
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(radialAcceptActionName .. "VR")
	ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(toggleMenuActionName)
end

local function bindAllRadialActions()
	GuiService.CoreGuiNavigationEnabled = false
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(freezeControllerActionName, noOpFunc, false, Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(radialAcceptActionName, radialSelectAccept, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(radialCancelActionName, radialSelectCancel, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(radialSelectActionName, radialSelect, false, Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick1, Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp, Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown, Enum.KeyCode.DPadLeft, Enum.KeyCode.DPadRight)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(thumbstick2RadialActionName, noOpFunc, false, Enum.KeyCode.Thumbstick2)
	ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(toggleMenuActionName, doGamepadMenuButton, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart)
	if VRService.VREnabled then ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(radialAcceptActionName .. "VR", radialSelectAccept, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2) end
end

local function setOverrideMouseIconBehavior(override)
	pcall(function()
		if override then
			InputService.OverrideMouseIconBehavior = InputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior.ForceHide or Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior.ForceShow
		else
			InputService.OverrideMouseIconBehavior = Enum.OverrideMouseIconBehavior.None
		end
	end)
end

local function updateGuiVisibility()
	if VRService.VREnabled and vrPanel and isVisible then pcall(function() vrPanel:SetVisible(true, true) end) end
	if not gamepadSettingsFrame then return end
	for _, child in ipairs(gamepadSettingsFrame:GetChildren()) do
		local icon = child:FindFirstChild("RadialIcon")
		local label = child:FindFirstChild("RadialLabel")
		if icon then icon.Visible = isVisible end
		if label and not isVisible then label.Visible = false end
	end
end

local function getVRKidSafeHint()
	local text = ""
	if businessLogic and type(businessLogic.GetVisibleAgeForPlayer) == "function" then
		pcall(function() text = businessLogic.GetVisibleAgeForPlayer(Players.LocalPlayer) end)
	end
	if text == "" then text = "Account" end
	local textSize = TextService:GetTextSize(text, 24, Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, Vector2.new(800, 800))
	local bubble = safeCreate("ImageLabel", {
		Name = "AccountTypeBubble", Size = UDim2.new(0, textSize.X + 20, 0, 50),
		Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/TopBar/Round.png", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice,
		SliceCenter = Rect.new(10, 10, 10, 10), ImageTransparency = 0.3, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
	})
	bubble.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -bubble.Size.X.Offset/2, 1, 10)
	safeCreate("TextLabel", {
		Name = "AccountTypeText", Text = text, Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, -20),
		Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10), Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold,
		FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size24, BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1),
		TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Parent = bubble,
	})
	return bubble
end

local setButtonEnabled

local function toggleVR(vrEnabled)
	if vrEnabled then
		gamepadSettingsFrame.Size = VR_FRAME_SIZE
		if not Panel3D then
			warn("GamepadMenu: Panel3D unavailable; VR radial panel is disabled.")
			return
		end
		vrPanel = Panel3D.Get("GamepadMenu")
		vrPanel:SetEnabled(true); vrPanel:SetVisible(false); vrPanel:SetCanFade(false)
		vrPanel:ResizeStuds(PANEL_SIZE_STUDS, PANEL_SIZE_STUDS, PANEL_RESOLUTION)
		vrPanel:SetType(Panel3D.Type.Standard, {CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 0.5)})
		gamepadSettingsFrame.Parent = vrPanel:GetGUI()
		function vrPanel:OnUpdate(dt)
			if not vrPanel:IsVisible() then return end
			local lookAtPixel = vrPanel.lookAtPixel
			local lookAtScale = lookAtPixel / vrPanel.gui.AbsoluteSize
			local inputVector = (lookAtScale - Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)) * 2
			if inputVector.Magnitude &gt; 0.4 and inputVector.Magnitude &lt; 0.8 then
				local angle = math.deg(math.atan2(inputVector.X, -inputVector.Y))
				if angle &lt; 0 then angle = angle + 360 end
				local button = getSelectedObjectFromAngle(angle)
				if button then setSelectedRadialButton(button) end
			end
		end
		for button, info in pairs(radialButtons) do
			if info.VRSlot then
				local slotImages = vrSlotImages[info.VRSlot]
				if slotImages then
					button.Parent = gamepadSettingsFrame
					button.Image = info.Disabled and slotImages.disabled or slotImages.background
					button.Rotation = slotImages.rotation
					button.RadialIcon.Image = slotImages.icon
					button.RadialIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.09, 0)
					button.RadialIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					button.RadialIcon.Size = slotImages.iconSize
					button.RadialIcon.Rotation = -slotImages.rotation
					button.RadialLabel.Rotation = -slotImages.rotation
					button.RadialLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
					button.RadialLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
					local selectedImage = button:FindFirstChild("Selected")
					if selectedImage then selectedImage.Image = slotImages.active end
					local mouseFrame = button:FindFirstChild("MouseFrame")
					if mouseFrame then mouseFrame.Visible = false end
				end
			end
		end
		safeCreate("Frame", {Parent = gamepadSettingsFrame, Position = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 192, 0, 32), BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		getVRKidSafeHint().Parent = gamepadSettingsFrame
		local chatButton = radialButtonsByName.Chat
		if chatButton then
			-- Keep legacy behavior: chat is unavailable in this VR radial menu.
			if radialButtons[chatButton] then radialButtons[chatButton].Disabled = true end
		end
	else
		gamepadSettingsFrame.Size = NON_VR_FRAME_SIZE
		if vrPanel then pcall(function() vrPanel:SetEnabled(false) end) end
		vrPanel = nil
		for button, info in pairs(radialButtons) do
			if info.Slot then
				local backgroundImage, activeImage, iconImage, iconPosition, iconSize = getImagesForSlot(info.Slot)
				if info.Disabled then backgroundImage = string.gsub(backgroundImage, "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/Empty") end
				button.Image = backgroundImage; button.Rotation = 0
				button.RadialIcon.Position = iconPosition; button.RadialIcon.Size = iconSize
				button.RadialIcon.Image = iconImage; button.RadialIcon.Rotation = 0; button.RadialIcon.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				local mouseFrame = button:FindFirstChild("MouseFrame")
				if mouseFrame then mouseFrame.Visible = true end
			else
				button.Parent = nil
			end
		end
	end
	if gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible then toggleCoreGuiRadial() end
end

local emptySelectedImageObject = safeCreate("ImageLabel", {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), Image = ""})

local function setSelectedForButton(button)
	if button and button:FindFirstChild("Selected") then setSelectedRadialButton(button) end
end

local function createRadialButton(name, text, slot, vrSlot, disabled, coreGuiType, activateFunc)
	local slotImage, selectedSlotImage, slotIcon, slotIconPosition, slotIconSize, mouseFrameSize, mouseFramePos = getImagesForSlot(slot)
	local radialButton = safeCreate("ImageButton", {
		Name = name, Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0),
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = slotImage,
		ZIndex = 2, SelectionImageObject = emptySelectedImageObject, Selectable = false,
		Parent = gamepadSettingsFrame,
	})
	if disabled then radialButton.Image = string.gsub(radialButton.Image, "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/Empty") end
	safeCreate("ImageLabel", {Name = "Selected", Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = selectedSlotImage, ZIndex = 2, Visible = false, Parent = radialButton})
	safeCreate("ImageLabel", {Name = "RadialIcon", Position = slotIconPosition, Size = slotIconSize, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = slotIcon, ZIndex = 3, ImageTransparency = disabled and 1 or 0, Parent = radialButton})
	local nameLabel = safeCreate("TextLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.new(0,220,0,50), Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-110,0.5,-25), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Text = text, Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size14, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1),
		Name = "RadialLabel", Visible = false, ZIndex = 3, Parent = radialButton,
	})
	if not smallScreen then nameLabel.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size36; nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(nameLabel.Size.X.Scale, nameLabel.Size.X.Offset, nameLabel.Size.Y.Scale, nameLabel.Size.Y.Offset + 4) end
	safeCreate("ImageLabel", {Name = text .. "BackgroundImage", Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,2), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/RadialLabel@2x.png", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice, SliceCenter = Rect.new(24,4,130,42), ZIndex = 2, Parent = nameLabel})
	local mouseFrame = safeCreate("ImageButton", {Name = "MouseFrame", Position = mouseFramePos, Size = mouseFrameSize, ZIndex = 3, BackgroundTransparency = 1, SelectionImageObject = emptySelectedImageObject, Parent = radialButton})
	mouseFrame.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if radialButtons[radialButton] and not radialButtons[radialButton].Disabled then setSelectedForButton(radialButton) end end)
	mouseFrame.MouseLeave:Connect(function() setSelectedRadialButton(nil) end)
	mouseFrame.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() local selected = radialButton:FindFirstChild("Selected"); if selected and selected.Visible then activateFunc() end end)
	radialButtons[radialButton] = {Function = activateFunc, Disabled = disabled, CoreGuiType = coreGuiType, Slot = slot, VRSlot = vrSlot}
	radialButtonsByName[name] = radialButton
	return radialButton
end

setButtonEnabled = function(button, enabled)
	local info = radialButtons[button]
	if not info or info.Disabled == not enabled then return end
	local selected = button:FindFirstChild("Selected")
	if selected and selected.Visible then setSelectedRadialButton(nil) end
	if enabled then
		if VRService.VREnabled then button.Image = vrSlotBackgroundImage
		else button.Image = string.gsub(button.Image, "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/Empty", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/") end
		button.ImageTransparency = 0; button.RadialIcon.ImageTransparency = 0
	else
		if VRService.VREnabled then button.Image = vrSlotDisabledImage
		else button.Image = string.gsub(button.Image, "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/", "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Radial/Empty") end
		button.ImageTransparency = 0; button.RadialIcon.ImageTransparency = 1
	end
	info.Disabled = not enabled
end

local function setButtonVisible(button, visible)
	button.Visible = visible
	if not visible then setButtonEnabled(button, false) end
end

toggleCoreGuiRadial = function(goingToSettings)
	if not gamepadSettingsFrame then return false end
	isVisible = not gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible
	updateGuiVisibility()
	if isVisible then
		setOverrideMouseIconBehavior(true)
		if lastInputChangedCon then lastInputChangedCon:Disconnect() end
		lastInputChangedCon = InputService.LastInputTypeChanged:Connect(function() setOverrideMouseIconBehavior(true) end)
		gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible = true
		for _, child in ipairs(gamepadSettingsFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("GuiButton") then pcall(function() utility:TweenProperty(child, "ImageTransparency", 1, 0, 0.1, utility:GetEaseOutQuad(), nil) end) end
		end
		local desiredSize = VRService.VREnabled and VR_FRAME_SIZE or NON_VR_FRAME_SIZE
		gamepadSettingsFrame:TweenSizeAndPosition(desiredSize, UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, 0.18, true, function() updateGuiVisibility() end)
	else
		if lastInputChangedCon then lastInputChangedCon:Disconnect(); lastInputChangedCon = nil end
		for _, child in ipairs(gamepadSettingsFrame:GetChildren()) do
			if child:IsA("GuiButton") then pcall(function() utility:TweenProperty(child, "ImageTransparency", 0, 1, 0.1, utility:GetEaseOutQuad(), nil) end) end
		end
		local desiredSize = VRService.VREnabled and VR_FRAME_HIDDEN_SIZE or NON_VR_FRAME_HIDDEN_SIZE
		gamepadSettingsFrame:TweenSizeAndPosition(desiredSize, UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0), Enum.EasingDirection.Out, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, 0.1, true, function()
			if not VRService.VREnabled then setOverrideMouseIconBehavior(false) end
			if not goingToSettings and not isVisible then GuiService:SetMenuIsOpen(false) end
			gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible = false
			if vrPanel then pcall(function() vrPanel:SetVisible(false) end) end
		end)
	end
	if isVisible then setSelectedRadialButton(nil); GuiService:SetMenuIsOpen(true); bindAllRadialActions()
	else unbindAllRadialActions() end
	return gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible
end

local function isCoreGuiDisabled()
	for _, enumItem in pairs(Enum.CoreGuiType:GetEnumItems()) do
		local ok, enabled = pcall(function() return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(enumItem) end)
		if ok and enabled then return false end
	end
	return true
end

local function createGamepadMenuGui()
	if gamepadSettingsFrame then return end
	gamepadSettingsFrame = safeCreate("Frame", {Name = "GamepadSettingsFrame", Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = NON_VR_FRAME_SIZE, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), Visible = false, Parent = GuiRoot})

	local function settingsFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial(true)
		local settingsModule = SettingsModules:FindFirstChild("SettingsHub")
		if settingsModule then
			local menuModule = require(settingsModule)
			menuModule:SetVisibility(true, nil, menuModule.Instance.GameSettingsPage, true)
		end
	end
	createRadialButton("Settings", "Settings", 1, 1, false, nil, settingsFunc)

	local function playerListFunc()
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			toggleCoreGuiRadial(true)
			local settingsModule = SettingsModules:FindFirstChild("SettingsHub")
			if settingsModule then local menuModule = require(settingsModule); menuModule:SetVisibility(true, nil, menuModule.Instance.PlayersPage, true) end
		else
			local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("PlayerlistModule")
			if module then
				local playerListModule = require(module)
				if playerListModule and not playerListModule:IsOpen() then toggleCoreGuiRadial(true); playerListModule:ToggleVisibility()
				else toggleCoreGuiRadial() end
			else toggleCoreGuiRadial() end
		end
	end
	createRadialButton("PlayerList", "Playerlist", 2, 2, not StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList), Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList, playerListFunc)

	local gamepadNotifications = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	gamepadNotifications.Name = "GamepadNotifications"
	gamepadNotifications.Parent = script
	local function notificationsFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial()
		if VRService.VREnabled then
			local vrModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("VR")
			local notificationModule = vrModules and vrModules:FindFirstChild("NotificationHub")
			if notificationModule then local hub = require(notificationModule); hub:SetVisible(not hub:IsVisible()) end
		else gamepadNotifications:Fire(true) end
	end
	local notificationsRadial = createRadialButton("Notifications", "Notifications", 3, 4, false, nil, notificationsFunc)
	if isTenFootInterface then setButtonEnabled(notificationsRadial, false) end

	local function leaveGameFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial(true)
		local settingsModule = SettingsModules:FindFirstChild("SettingsHub")
		local leavePage = SettingsModules:FindFirstChild("Pages") and SettingsModules.Pages:FindFirstChild("LeaveGame")
		if settingsModule and leavePage then require(settingsModule):SetVisibility(true, false, require(leavePage), true) end
	end
	createRadialButton("LeaveGame", "Leave Game", 4, 5, false, nil, leaveGameFunc)

	local function backpackFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial(true)
		local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("BackpackScript")
		if module then local backpackModule = require(module); backpackModule:OpenClose() end
	end
	createRadialButton("Backpack", "Backpack", 5, 6, not StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack), Enum.CoreGuiType.Backpack, backpackFunc)

	local function chatFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial()
		local module = Modules:FindFirstChild("ChatSelector")
		if module then require(module):ToggleVisibility() end
	end
	local chatRadial = createRadialButton("Chat", "Chat", 6, 8, not StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat), Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat, chatFunc)
	if isTenFootInterface then setButtonEnabled(chatRadial, false) end

	local function recenterFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial()
		local vrModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("VR")
		local module = vrModules and vrModules:FindFirstChild("Recenter")
		if module then local recenter = require(module); recenter:SetVisible(not recenter:IsVisible()) end
	end
	createRadialButton("Recenter", "Recenter", nil, 3, false, nil, recenterFunc)

	local function toggleUIFunc()
		toggleCoreGuiRadial()
		local vrModules = Modules:FindFirstChild("VR")
		local module = vrModules and vrModules:FindFirstChild("UserGui")
		if module then local userGui = require(module); userGui:SetVisible(not userGui:IsVisible()) end
	end
	createRadialButton("ToggleUI", "Toggle UI", nil, 7, false, nil, toggleUIFunc)

	local closeHintFrame = safeCreate("Frame", {Name = "CloseHintFrame", Position = UDim2.new(1,10,1,10), Size = UDim2.new(0,103,0,60), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Parent = gamepadSettingsFrame})
	local closeHintImage = safeCreate("ImageLabel", {Name = "CloseHint", Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0,0.5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/BButtonDark.png", Parent = closeHintFrame})
	safeCreate("UIAspectRatioConstraint", {AspectRatio = 1, Parent = closeHintImage})
	if isTenFootInterface then closeHintImage.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Help/BButtonDark@2x.png"; closeHintFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0,133,0,90) end
	local closeHintText = safeCreate("TextLabel", {Name = "closeHintText", Position = UDim2.new(1,0,0.5,0), Size = UDim2.new(0,43,0,24), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,0.5), Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold, FontSize = isTenFootInterface and Enum.FontSize.Size36 or Enum.FontSize.Size24, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "Back", TextColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, Parent = closeHintFrame})

	GuiService:AddSelectionParent(HttpService:GenerateGUID(false), gamepadSettingsFrame)
	gamepadSettingsFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if not gamepadSettingsFrame.Visible then unbindAllRadialActions() end end)
	VRService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("VREnabled"):Connect(function() pcall(toggleVR, VRService.VREnabled) end)
	pcall(toggleVR, VRService.VREnabled)
end

local function doGamepadMenuButton(name, state, input)
	if state ~= Enum.UserInputState.Begin then return end
	if game:IsLoaded() then
		if not toggleCoreGuiRadial() then unbindAllRadialActions() end
	end
end
doGamepadMenuButton = doGamepadMenuButton

local function canChangeButtonVisibleState(buttonType)
	if isTenFootInterface and (buttonType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat or buttonType == Enum.CoreGuiType.PlayerList) then return false end
	if VRService.VREnabled and buttonType == Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat then return false end
	return true
end

if InputService:GetGamepadConnected(Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1) then
	createGamepadMenuGui()
else
	InputService.GamepadConnected:Connect(function(gamepadEnum)
		if gamepadEnum == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then createGamepadMenuGui() end
	end)
end

local isLoadingGuiRemoved = false
local isPlayerAdded = false
local function updateRadialMenuActionBinding()
	if isLoadingGuiRemoved and isPlayerAdded then
		createGamepadMenuGui()
		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(toggleMenuActionName, doGamepadMenuButton, false, Enum.KeyCode.ButtonStart)
	end
end
local loadedConnection
local function handlePlayerAdded()
	if loadedConnection then loadedConnection:Disconnect(); loadedConnection = nil end
	isPlayerAdded = true
	updateRadialMenuActionBinding()
end
loadedConnection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
	if Players.LocalPlayer and plr == Players.LocalPlayer then handlePlayerAdded() end
end)
if Players.LocalPlayer then handlePlayerAdded() end

local ReplicatedFirst = game:GetService("ReplicatedFirst")
local defaultLoadingGuiRemovedConnection
local function handleDefaultLoadingGuiRemoved()
	if defaultLoadingGuiRemovedConnection then defaultLoadingGuiRemovedConnection:Disconnect(); defaultLoadingGuiRemovedConnection = nil end
	isLoadingGuiRemoved = true
	updateRadialMenuActionBinding()
end
if ReplicatedFirst:IsDefaultLoadingGuiRemoved() then
	handleDefaultLoadingGuiRemoved()
else
	defaultLoadingGuiRemovedConnection = ReplicatedFirst.DefaultLoadingGuiRemoved:Connect(handleDefaultLoadingGuiRemoved)
end

pcall(function()
	StarterGui.CoreGuiChangedSignal:Connect(function(coreGuiType, enabled)
		for button, buttonTable in pairs(radialButtons) do
			local buttonType = buttonTable.CoreGuiType
			if buttonType and (coreGuiType == buttonType or coreGuiType == Enum.CoreGuiType.All) and canChangeButtonVisibleState(buttonType) then
				setButtonEnabled(button, enabled)
			end
		end
	end)
end)

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local ContextActionService = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local STOP_MOVEMENT_ACTION_NAME = "AvatarContextMenuStopInput"
local MAX_THUMBNAIL_RETRIES = 4

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local RobloxGui = findCoreRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "[ContextMenuUtil] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LocalPlayer then
	LocalPlayer = Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
end

local ContextMenuUtil = {}
ContextMenuUtil.__index = ContextMenuUtil

local function getSelectedObject()
	local selected
	pcall(function()
		selected = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
	end)
	if selected then
		return selected
	end
	pcall(function()
		selected = GuiService.SelectedObject
	end)
	return selected
end

local function setSelectedObject(object)
	local success = pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = object
	end)
	if not success then
		pcall(function()
			GuiService.SelectedObject = object
		end)
	end
end

function ContextMenuUtil:GetHeadshotForPlayer(player)
	if not player then
		return ""
	end

	local cached = self.HeadShotUrlCache[player]
	if cached and cached ~= "" then
		return cached
	end

	self.HeadShotUrlCache[player] = ""

	local thumbnail = ""
	for attempt = 0, MAX_THUMBNAIL_RETRIES do
		local ok, url, isFinal = pcall(function()
			return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(
				player.UserId,
				Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot,
				Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size180x180
			)
		end)

		if ok and type(url) == "string" then
			thumbnail = url
			if isFinal then
				break
			end
		else
			warn("[ContextMenuUtil] Headshot lookup failed: " .. tostring(url))
		end

		if attempt &lt; MAX_THUMBNAIL_RETRIES then
			task.wait(math.min(attempt + 1, 3))
		end
	end

	self.HeadShotUrlCache[player] = thumbnail
	return thumbnail
end

function ContextMenuUtil:HasOrGettingHeadShot(player)
	return self.HeadShotUrlCache[player] ~= nil
end

function ContextMenuUtil:FindPlayerFromPart(part)
	local current = part

	while current and not current:IsA("Model") do
		current = current.Parent
	end

	if current then
		return Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(current)
	end

	return nil
end

function ContextMenuUtil:GetPlayerPosition(player)
	local character = player and player.Character
	local rootPart = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	return rootPart and rootPart.Position or nil
end

local movementEnabled = true

local function movementAction(_, inputState)
	if inputState == Enum.UserInputState.End
		or inputState == Enum.UserInputState.Cancel then
		return Enum.ContextActionResult.Pass
	end
	return Enum.ContextActionResult.Sink
end

function ContextMenuUtil:DisablePlayerMovement()
	if not movementEnabled then
		return
	end
	movementEnabled = false

	local inputs = {
		Enum.PlayerActions.CharacterForward,
		Enum.PlayerActions.CharacterBackward,
		Enum.PlayerActions.CharacterLeft,
		Enum.PlayerActions.CharacterRight,
		Enum.PlayerActions.CharacterJump,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3,
		Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4,
	}

	local ok = pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:BindCoreAction(
			STOP_MOVEMENT_ACTION_NAME,
			movementAction,
			false,
			table.unpack(inputs)
		)
	end)

	if not ok then
		pcall(function()
			ContextActionService:BindAction(
				STOP_MOVEMENT_ACTION_NAME,
				movementAction,
				false,
				table.unpack(inputs)
			)
		end)
	end
end

function ContextMenuUtil:EnablePlayerMovement()
	if movementEnabled then
		return
	end
	movementEnabled = true

	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindCoreAction(STOP_MOVEMENT_ACTION_NAME)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		ContextActionService:UnbindAction(STOP_MOVEMENT_ACTION_NAME)
	end)
end

function ContextMenuUtil:GetFriendStatus(player)
	if not player then
		return Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return LocalPlayer:GetFriendStatus(player)
	end)

	return (ok and result) or Enum.FriendStatus.NotFriend
end

local SelectionOverrideObject = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
SelectionOverrideObject.Image = ""
SelectionOverrideObject.BackgroundTransparency = 1

local function isPointerInput(inputObject)
	return inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
		or inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function MakeDefaultButton(name, size, clickFunc)
	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = name .. "Button"
	button.Image = ""
	button.AutoButtonColor = false
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.Size = size
	button.ZIndex = 2
	button.SelectionImageObject = SelectionOverrideObject
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	button.SliceCenter = Rect.new(8, 6, 46, 44)

	local underline = Instance.new("Frame")
	underline.Name = "Underline"
	underline.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(137, 137, 137)
	underline.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
	underline.BorderSizePixel = 0
	underline.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
	underline.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0, 1)
	underline.Parent = button

	if type(clickFunc) == "function" then
		button.Activated:Connect(function()
			clickFunc(UserInputService:GetLastInputType())
		end)
	end

	button.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObject)
		if button.Selectable and isPointerInput(inputObject) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		end
	end)

	button.InputEnded:Connect(function(inputObject)
		if button.Selectable
			and getSelectedObject() ~= button
			and isPointerInput(inputObject) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end
	end)

	pcall(function()
		button.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
			button.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
		end)
	end)

	pcall(function()
		button.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
			button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		end)
	end)

	return button
end

local function getViewportSize()
	local deadline = os.clock() + 10

	repeat
		local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			local size = camera.ViewportSize
			if size.X &gt; 1 and size.Y &gt; 1 then
				return size
			end
		end
		task.wait(0.1)
	until os.clock() &gt;= deadline

	return Vector2.new(1280, 720)
end

local function isSmallTouchScreen()
	local size = getViewportSize()
	return UserInputService.TouchEnabled
		and (size.Y &lt; 500 or size.X &lt; 700)
end

function ContextMenuUtil:MakeStyledButton(name, text, size, clickFunc)
	local button = MakeDefaultButton(name, size, clickFunc)

	local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	textLabel.Name = name .. "TextLabel"
	textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	textLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
	textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -8)
	textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	textLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansLight
	textLabel.TextSize = 24
	textLabel.Text = text
	textLabel.TextScaled = true
	textLabel.TextWrapped = true
	textLabel.ZIndex = 2
	textLabel.Parent = button

	local constraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	constraint.MaxTextSize = isSmallTouchScreen() and 18 or 24
	if GuiService:IsTenFootInterface() then
		constraint.MaxTextSize = 36
	end
	constraint.Parent = textLabel

	return button, textLabel
end

function ContextMenuUtil.new()
	return setmetatable({
		HeadShotUrlCache = {},
	}, ContextMenuUtil)
end

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local AnalyticsService = game:GetService("AnalyticsService")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local RobloxGui = findCoreRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "[ContextMenuItems] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local Modules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules")
local SettingsModules = Modules:WaitForChild("Settings")
local AvatarMenuModules = Modules:WaitForChild("AvatarContextMenu")

local function requireChild(parent, name)
	local moduleScript = parent:WaitForChild(name)
	assert(moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[ContextMenuItems] Expected ModuleScript " .. moduleScript:GetFullName())
	return require(moduleScript)
end

local ContextMenuUtil = requireChild(AvatarMenuModules, "ContextMenuUtil")
local PlayerDropDownModule = requireChild(Modules, "PlayerDropDown")
local ReportAbuseMenu = requireChild(SettingsPages, "ReportAbuseMenu")
local PromptCreatorModule = Modules:FindFirstChild("PromptCreator")
if PromptCreatorModule and PromptCreatorModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
	local ok, err = pcall(require, PromptCreatorModule)
	if not ok then
		warn("[ContextMenuItems] PromptCreator failed: " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
if not LocalPlayer then
	LocalPlayer = Players.PlayerAdded:Wait()
end

local EnabledContextMenuItems = {
	[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Chat] = true,
	[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Friend] = true,
	[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Emote] = true,
}

local CustomContextMenuItems = {}
local BlockingUtility = PlayerDropDownModule:CreateBlockingUtility()

local ContextMenuItems = {}
ContextMenuItems.__index = ContextMenuItems

local function analytics(methodName, ...)
	local args = table.pack(...)
	pcall(function()
		local method = AnalyticsService[methodName]
		if type(method) == "function" then
			method(AnalyticsService, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
		end
	end)
end

local function registerLegacySetCore(name, callback)
	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		StarterGui:RegisterSetCore(name, callback)
	end)

	if not ok then
		warn("[ContextMenuItems] Could not register SetCore '" ..
			name .. "': " .. tostring(err))
	end
end

local function sendChatCommand(message)
	local general
	local channels = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
	if channels then
		general = channels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
		if not general then
			for _, child in ipairs(channels:GetChildren()) do
				if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
					general = child
					break
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if general and general:IsA("TextChannel") then
		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			general:SendAsync(message)
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[ContextMenuItems] SendAsync failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
		return ok
	end

	warn("[ContextMenuItems] Cannot send chat command: no TextChannel exists")
	return false
end

function ContextMenuItems:ClearMenuItems()
	if not self.MenuItemFrame then
		return
	end

	for _, child in ipairs(self.MenuItemFrame:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("GuiObject") then
			child:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

function ContextMenuItems:AddCustomAvatarMenuItem(menuOption, bindableEvent)
	CustomContextMenuItems[menuOption] = bindableEvent
end

function ContextMenuItems:RemoveCustomAvatarMenuItem(menuOption)
	CustomContextMenuItems[menuOption] = nil
end

function ContextMenuItems:IsContextAvatarEnumItem(enumItem)
	for _, item in ipairs(Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption:GetEnumItems()) do
		if item == enumItem then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

function ContextMenuItems:EnableDefaultMenuItem(menuOption)
	EnabledContextMenuItems[menuOption] = true
end

function ContextMenuItems:RemoveDefaultMenuItem(menuOption)
	EnabledContextMenuItems[menuOption] = false
end

function ContextMenuItems:RegisterCoreMethods()
	registerLegacySetCore("AddAvatarContextMenuOption", function(args)
		if type(args) == "table" then
			if type(args[1]) ~= "string" then
				error("AddAvatarContextMenuOption expects a string name in item 1")
			end
			if typeof(args[2]) ~= "Instance" or not args[2]:IsA("BindableEvent") then
				error("AddAvatarContextMenuOption expects a BindableEvent in item 2")
			end
			self:AddCustomAvatarMenuItem(args[1], args[2])
		elseif typeof(args) == "EnumItem" and self:IsContextAvatarEnumItem(args) then
			self:EnableDefaultMenuItem(args)
		else
			error("Invalid AddAvatarContextMenuOption argument")
		end
	end)

	registerLegacySetCore("RemoveAvatarContextMenuOption", function(menuOption)
		if type(menuOption) == "string" then
			self:RemoveCustomAvatarMenuItem(menuOption)
		elseif typeof(menuOption) == "EnumItem"
			and self:IsContextAvatarEnumItem(menuOption) then
			self:RemoveDefaultMenuItem(menuOption)
		else
			error("Invalid RemoveAvatarContextMenuOption argument")
		end
	end)
end

function ContextMenuItems:CreateCustomMenuItems()
	for buttonText, bindableEvent in pairs(CustomContextMenuItems) do
		if typeof(bindableEvent) == "Instance"
			and bindableEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then

			analytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "AvatarContextMenuCustomButton",
				"name: " .. tostring(buttonText))

			local function click()
				bindableEvent:Fire(self.SelectedPlayer)
			end

			local button = ContextMenuUtil:MakeStyledButton(
				"CustomButton",
				buttonText,
				UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0, 52),
				click
			)

			button.Name = "CustomButton"
			button.LayoutOrder = 20
			button.Parent = self.MenuItemFrame
		end
	end
end

local FRIEND_LAYOUT_ORDER = 1
local CHAT_LAYOUT_ORDER = 3
local WAVE_LAYOUT_ORDER = 4
local CUSTOM_LAYOUT_ORDER = 20

function ContextMenuItems:CreateFriendButton(status)
	local oldButton = self.MenuItemFrame:FindFirstChild("FriendStatus")
	if oldButton then
		oldButton:Destroy()
	end

	if self._friendStatusConnection then
		self._friendStatusConnection:Disconnect()
		self._friendStatusConnection = nil
	end

	local labelText
	local button

	local function addFriend()
		if not button or not button.Selectable or not self.SelectedPlayer then
			return
		end

		button.Selectable = false
		labelText.TextTransparency = 0.75
		labelText.Text = "Friend Request Pending"

		analytics("ReportCounter", "AvatarContextMenu-RequestFriendship")
		analytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "RequestFriendship", "AvatarContextMenu")

		local ok, err = pcall(function()
			LocalPlayer:RequestFriendship(self.SelectedPlayer)
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[ContextMenuItems] Friend request failed: " .. tostring(err))
		end
	end

	button, labelText = ContextMenuUtil:MakeStyledButton(
		"FriendStatus",
		"Add Friend",
		UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0, 52),
		addFriend
	)

	if status == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
		button.Selectable = false
		labelText.TextTransparency = 0.75
		labelText.Text = "Friends"
	else
		button.Selectable = true
		labelText.TextTransparency = 0
		if status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
			labelText.Text = "Accept Friend Request"
		elseif status == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestSent then
			labelText.Text = "Friend Request Pending"
			button.Selectable = false
		end
	end

	local ok, signal = pcall(function()
		return LocalPlayer.FriendStatusChanged
	end)

	if ok and signal then
		self._friendStatusConnection = signal:Connect(function(player, newStatus)
			if player ~= self.SelectedPlayer or not labelText.Parent then
				return
			end

			if newStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.Friend then
				button.Selectable = false
				labelText.TextTransparency = 0.75
				labelText.Text = "Friends"
			elseif newStatus == Enum.FriendStatus.FriendRequestReceived then
				labelText.Text = "Accept Friend Request"
			end
		end)
	end

	button.LayoutOrder = FRIEND_LAYOUT_ORDER
	button.Parent = self.MenuItemFrame
end

function ContextMenuItems:UpdateFriendButton(status)
	if self.MenuItemFrame:FindFirstChild("FriendStatus") then
		self:CreateFriendButton(status)
	end
end

function ContextMenuItems:CreateEmoteButton()
	if self.MenuItemFrame:FindFirstChild("Wave") then
		return
	end

	local function wave()
		if self.CloseMenuFunc then
			self.CloseMenuFunc()
		end

		analytics("ReportCounter", "AvatarContextMenu-Wave")
		analytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "AvatarContextMenuWave",
			"placeId: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId))

		sendChatCommand("/e wave")
	end

	local button = ContextMenuUtil:MakeStyledButton(
		"Wave",
		"Wave",
		UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0, 52),
		wave
	)

	button.LayoutOrder = WAVE_LAYOUT_ORDER
	button.Parent = self.MenuItemFrame
end

function ContextMenuItems:CreateChatButton()
	local function chatFunc()
		if self.CloseMenuFunc then
			self.CloseMenuFunc()
		end

		analytics("ReportCounter", "AvatarContextMenu-Chat")
		analytics("TrackEvent", "Game", "AvatarContextMenuChat",
			"placeId: " .. tostring(game.PlaceId))

		local target = self.SelectedPlayer
		if not target then
			return
		end

		local ChatSelector = Modules:FindFirstChild("ChatSelector")
		if ChatSelector and ChatSelector:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			local ok, chatModule = pcall(require, ChatSelector)
			if ok and chatModule then
				pcall(function()
					chatModule:SetVisible(true)
					chatModule:FocusChatBar()
				end)
			else
				warn("[ContextMenuItems] ChatSelector failed to load")
			end
		end

		-- Retain the old textbox prefill only when that UI is actually present.
		pcall(function()
			local playerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
			local chatBar = playerGui
				and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Chat")
				and playerGui.Chat:FindFirstChild("Frame")
				and playerGui.Chat.Frame:FindFirstChild("ChatBarParentFrame")
				and playerGui.Chat.Frame.ChatBarParentFrame:FindFirstChild("Frame")
				and playerGui.Chat.Frame.ChatBarParentFrame.Frame:FindFirstChild("BoxFrame")
				and playerGui.Chat.Frame.ChatBarParentFrame.Frame.BoxFrame:FindFirstChild("Frame")
				and playerGui.Chat.Frame.ChatBarParentFrame.Frame.BoxFrame.Frame:FindFirstChild("ChatBar")

			if chatBar and chatBar:IsA("TextBox") then
				chatBar.Text = "/w " .. target.Name .. " "
				chatBar.CursorPosition = #chatBar.Text + 1
			end
		end)
	end

	local button = self.MenuItemFrame:FindFirstChild("ChatStatus")
	if not button then
		button = ContextMenuUtil:MakeStyledButton(
			"ChatStatus",
			"Chat",
			UDim2.new(0.96, 0, 0, 52),
			chatFunc
		)
		button.LayoutOrder = CHAT_LAYOUT_ORDER
	end

	local canChat = true
	local success, allowed = pcall(function()
		return Chat:CanUserChatAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId)
	end)

	if success then
		canChat = RunService:IsStudio() or allowed == true
	end

	button.Parent = canChat and self.MenuItemFrame or nil
end

function ContextMenuItems:BuildContextMenuItems(player)
	if not player then
		return
	end

	local friendStatus = ContextMenuUtil:GetFriendStatus(player)
	local isBlocked = false
	local isMuted = false

	pcall(function()
		isBlocked = BlockingUtility:IsPlayerBlockedByUserId(player.UserId)
	end)
	pcall(function()
		isMuted = BlockingUtility:IsPlayerMutedByUserId(player.UserId)
	end)

	-- Leave these values accessible to the menu without letting an
	-- optional block/mute API failure prevent the menu from being built.
	self.IsSelectedPlayerBlocked = isBlocked
	self.IsSelectedPlayerMuted = isMuted

	self:ClearMenuItems()
	self:SetSelectedPlayer(player)

	if EnabledContextMenuItems[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Friend] then
		self:CreateFriendButton(friendStatus)
	end
	if EnabledContextMenuItems[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Chat] then
		self:CreateChatButton()
	end
	if EnabledContextMenuItems[Enum.AvatarContextMenuOption.Emote] then
		self:CreateEmoteButton()
	end

	self:CreateCustomMenuItems()
end

function ContextMenuItems:SetSelectedPlayer(player)
	self.SelectedPlayer = player
end

function ContextMenuItems:SetCloseMenuFunc(callback)
	self.CloseMenuFunc = callback
end

function ContextMenuItems.new(menuItemFrame)
	local self = setmetatable({}, ContextMenuItems)
	self.MenuItemFrame = menuItemFrame
	self.SelectedPlayer = nil
	self:RegisterCoreMethods()
	return self
end

local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local BG_TRANSPARENCY = 1
local BOTTOM_SCREEN_PADDING_PERCENT = 0.02

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local RobloxGui = findCoreRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "[ContextMenuGui] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

	local instance = AvatarMenuModules:WaitForChild(name)
	assert(instance:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[ContextMenuGui] Expected ModuleScript: " .. name)
	return require(instance)

local ContextMenuUtil = requireModule("ContextMenuUtil")

local ContextMenuGui = {}
ContextMenuGui.__index = ContextMenuGui

local PlayerCarousel
local PlayerChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local viewportConnections = {}

local function disconnectViewportConnections()
	for _, connection in ipairs(viewportConnections) do
		pcall(function()
			connection:Disconnect()
		end)
	end
	table.clear(viewportConnections)
end

local function listenToViewportChange(callback)
	if type(callback) ~= "function" then
		return
	end

	local viewportConnection

	local function updateCamera()
		if viewportConnection then
			viewportConnection:Disconnect()
			viewportConnection = nil
		end

		local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if camera then
			viewportConnection = camera:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize")
				:Connect(callback)
			table.insert(viewportConnections, viewportConnection)
		end

		pcall(callback)
	end

	table.insert(
		viewportConnections,
		Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(updateCamera)
	)

	updateCamera()
end

function ContextMenuGui:CreateContextMenuHolder()
	local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
	holder.Name = "AvatarContextMenu"
	holder.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	holder.Parent = RobloxGui

	return holder
end

function ContextMenuGui:CreateLeaveMenuButton(frame)
	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = "CloseMenuButton"
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	button.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
	button.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
	button.Size = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.1, 0)
	button.Image = "rbxasset://textures/loading/cancelButton.png"
	button.Selectable = false

	button.Activated:Connect(function()
		if type(self.CloseMenuFunc) == "function" then
			self.CloseMenuFunc()
		end
	end)

	local constraint = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	constraint.AspectType = Enum.AspectType.FitWithinMaxSize
	constraint.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height
	constraint.AspectRatio = 1
	constraint.Parent = button

	button.Parent = frame
	return button
end

function ContextMenuGui:CreateMenuFrame()
	if self.ContextMenuFrame and self.ContextMenuFrame.Parent then
		self.ContextMenuFrame:Destroy()
	end

	local holder = self:CreateContextMenuHolder()

	local menu = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	menu.Name = "Menu"
	menu.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0.9, 0)
	menu.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1 - BOTTOM_SCREEN_PADDING_PERCENT, 0)
	menu.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
	menu.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	menu.Selectable = false
	menu.Image = "rbxasset://textures/blackBkg_round.png"
	menu.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	menu.SliceCenter = Rect.new(12, 12, 12, 12)
	menu.Visible = false
	menu.Active = true
	menu.ClipsDescendants = true

	pcall(function()
		GuiService:AddSelectionParent("AvatarContextMenuGroup", menu)
	end)

	local aspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	aspect.AspectType = Enum.AspectType.ScaleWithParentSize
	aspect.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height
	aspect.AspectRatio = 1.15
	aspect.Parent = menu

	local function updateAspect()
		local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not camera then
			return
		end

		local size = camera.ViewportSize
		aspect.DominantAxis = size.X &lt; size.Y
			and Enum.DominantAxis.Width
			or Enum.DominantAxis.Height
	end

	listenToViewportChange(updateAspect)

	local sizeConstraint = Instance.new("UISizeConstraint")
	sizeConstraint.MaxSize = Vector2.new(300, 300)
	sizeConstraint.MinSize = Vector2.new(200, 200)
	sizeConstraint.Parent = menu

	local content = Instance.new("Frame")
	content.Name = "Content"
	content.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	content.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	content.Parent = menu

	local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	layout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
	layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	layout.Parent = content

	local actionList = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	actionList.Name = "ContextActionList"
	actionList.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
	actionList.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(79, 79, 79)
	actionList.BorderSizePixel = 0
	actionList.LayoutOrder = 2
	actionList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.54, 0)
	actionList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 208)
	actionList.ScrollBarThickness = 4
	actionList.Selectable = false
	actionList.Parent = content

	local actionLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	actionLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	actionLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	actionLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
	actionLayout.Parent = actionList

	actionLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
		actionList.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(
			0, 0, 0, actionLayout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
		)
	end)

	local nameTag = Instance.new("TextButton")
	nameTag.Name = "NameTag"
	nameTag.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1)
	nameTag.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(79, 79, 79)
	nameTag.AutoButtonColor = false
	nameTag.BorderSizePixel = 0
	nameTag.LayoutOrder = 1
	nameTag.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0.16, 0)
	nameTag.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	nameTag.Text = ""
	nameTag.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	nameTag.TextSize = 24
	nameTag.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	nameTag.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	nameTag.Selectable = false
	nameTag.Parent = content

	local underline = Instance.new("Frame")
	underline.Name = "Underline"
	underline.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	underline.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1)
	underline.BorderSizePixel = 0
	underline.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
	underline.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2)
	underline.Parent = nameTag

	self:CreateLeaveMenuButton(menu)
	menu.Parent = holder

	self.ContextMenuFrame = menu
	self.ContextMenuHolder = holder
	return menu
end

function ContextMenuGui:BuildPlayerCarousel(playersByProximity)
	if not self.ContextMenuFrame then
		self:CreateMenuFrame()
	end

	if not PlayerCarousel then
		PlayerCarousel = requireModule("PlayerCarousel")
		PlayerCarousel.rbxGui.Parent = self.ContextMenuFrame.Content
	end

	PlayerCarousel:ClearPlayerEntries()

	for _, entry in ipairs(playersByProximity or {}) do
		local player = entry[1]
		local distance = entry[2] or 0
		if player then
			PlayerCarousel:CreatePlayerEntry(player, distance)
		end
	end

	local first = playersByProximity and playersByProximity[1]
	self.ContextMenuFrame.Content.NameTag.Text =
		first and first[1] and first[1].Name or ""

	if self._playerChangedConnection then
		self._playerChangedConnection:Disconnect()
	end

	self._playerChangedConnection = PlayerCarousel.PlayerChanged:Connect(function(player)
		if self.ContextMenuFrame and self.ContextMenuFrame.Parent then
			self.ContextMenuFrame.Content.NameTag.Text =
				player and player.Name or ""
		end
		PlayerChangedEvent:Fire(player)
	end)
end

function ContextMenuGui:GetBottomScreenPaddingConstant()
	return BOTTOM_SCREEN_PADDING_PERCENT
end

function ContextMenuGui:SetCloseMenuFunc(callback)
	self.CloseMenuFunc = callback
end

function ContextMenuGui:SwitchToPlayerEntry(player, dontTween)
	if PlayerCarousel then
		PlayerCarousel:SwitchToPlayerEntry(player, dontTween)
	end
end

function ContextMenuGui:OffsetPlayerEntry(offset)
	if PlayerCarousel then
		PlayerCarousel:OffsetPlayerEntry(offset)
	end
end

function ContextMenuGui:GetSelectedPlayer()
	return PlayerCarousel and PlayerCarousel:GetSelectedPlayer() or nil
end

function ContextMenuGui:Destroy()
	disconnectViewportConnections()

	if self._playerChangedConnection then
		self._playerChangedConnection:Disconnect()
		self._playerChangedConnection = nil
	end

	if self.ContextMenuHolder then
		self.ContextMenuHolder:Destroy()
		self.ContextMenuHolder = nil
		self.ContextMenuFrame = nil
	end
end

function ContextMenuGui.new()
	return setmetatable({
		CloseMenuFunc = nil,
		ContextMenuFrame = nil,
		ContextMenuHolder = nil,
		LastSetPlayerIcon = nil,
		SelectedPlayerChanged = PlayerChangedEvent.Event,
		_playerChangedConnection = nil,
	}, ContextMenuGui)
end

local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local InsertService = game:GetService("InsertService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

local SelectedCharacterIndicator = {}
SelectedCharacterIndicator.__index = SelectedCharacterIndicator

local RENDER_ARROW_CONTEXT_ACTION = "ContextActionMenuRenderArrow"
local ARROW_ASSET = "rbxasset://models/AvatarContextMenu/AvatarContextArrow.rbxm"

local function getCamera()
	return Workspace.CurrentCamera
end

local function findBasePart(instance)
	if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
		return instance
	end

	if instance:IsA("Model") then
		if instance.PrimaryPart then
			return instance.PrimaryPart
		end

		return instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	end

	return instance:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
end

local function applyArrow(character)
	if not character then
		return nil
	end

	local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		or character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 15)

	local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		or character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 15)

	if not humanoid or not rootPart then
		return nil
	end

	local asset
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return InsertService:LoadLocalAsset(ARROW_ASSET)
	end)

	if not ok or not result then
		warn("[SelectedCharacterIndicator] Could not load arrow asset: "
			.. tostring(result))
		return nil
	end

	asset = result
	local arrowPart = findBasePart(asset)

	if not arrowPart then
		asset:Destroy()
		warn("[SelectedCharacterIndicator] Arrow asset has no BasePart")
		return nil
	end

	local camera = getCamera()
	if not camera then
		asset:Destroy()
		return nil
	end

	local model = Instance.new("Model")
	model.Name = "ContextMenuArrow"
	asset.Parent = model
	model.Parent = camera

	for _, descendant in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
		if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
			descendant.Anchored = true
			descendant.CanCollide = false
			descendant.CanTouch = false
			descendant.CanQuery = false
		end
	end

	local tween
	local tweenOk, tweenResult = pcall(function()
		return TweenService:Create(
			arrowPart,
			TweenInfo.new(
				4,
				Enum.EasingStyle.Linear,
				Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
				-1,
				false
			),
			{ Orientation = Vector3.new(0, 360, 180) }
		)
	end)

	if tweenOk then
		tween = tweenResult
		tween:Play()
	end

	local alive = true
	local function update()
		if not alive or not model.Parent or not rootPart.Parent then
			return
		end

		local currentCamera = getCamera()
		if currentCamera and model.Parent ~= currentCamera then
			model.Parent = currentCamera
		end

		arrowPart.Position = rootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
	end

	local function kill()
		if not alive then
			return
		end

		alive = false
		pcall(function()
			RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(RENDER_ARROW_CONTEXT_ACTION)
		end)

		if tween then
			pcall(function()
				tween:Cancel()
				tween:Destroy()
			end)
		end

		if model then
			model:Destroy()
		end
	end

	RunService:BindToRenderStep(
		RENDER_ARROW_CONTEXT_ACTION,
		Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1,
		update
	)

	humanoid.Died:Connect(kill)
	character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
		if parent == nil then
			kill()
		end
	end)

	update()
	return kill
end

function SelectedCharacterIndicator:ChangeSelectedPlayer(selectedPlayer)
	if self.SelectedPlayer == selectedPlayer then
		return
	end

	if self.CharacterAddedConn then
		self.CharacterAddedConn:Disconnect()
		self.CharacterAddedConn = nil
	end

	if self.KillOldRenderFunction then
		self.KillOldRenderFunction()
		self.KillOldRenderFunction = nil
	end

	self.SelectedPlayer = selectedPlayer

	if not selectedPlayer then
		return
	end

	self.CharacterAddedConn = selectedPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		if self.KillOldRenderFunction then
			self.KillOldRenderFunction()
			self.KillOldRenderFunction = nil
		end

		self.KillOldRenderFunction = applyArrow(character)
	end)

	if selectedPlayer.Character then
		self.KillOldRenderFunction = applyArrow(selectedPlayer.Character)
	end
end

function SelectedCharacterIndicator.new()
	return setmetatable({
		KillOldRenderFunction = nil,
		SelectedPlayer = nil,
		CharacterAddedConn = nil,
	}, SelectedCharacterIndicator)
end

return SelectedCharacterIndicator.new()</ProtectedString>
                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-461</int64>
                                    <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                </Properties>
                            </Item>

                                <Properties>
                                    <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                    <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>

local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local CoreGui = game:GetService("CoreGui")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

local BACKGROUND_SELECTED_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(0, 162, 255)
local BACKGROUND_DEFAULT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
local PAGE_LAYOUT_TWEEN_TIME = 0.25

local function findCoreRoot()
	return CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
		or CoreGui:FindFirstChild("RobloxGui")
end

local RobloxGui = findCoreRoot()
assert(RobloxGui, "[PlayerCarousel] CoreGui.NewRobloxGui is missing")

local CoreGuiModules = RobloxGui:WaitForChild("Modules")
local AvatarMenuModules = CoreGuiModules:WaitForChild("AvatarContextMenu")

local function requireModule(name)
	local instance = AvatarMenuModules:WaitForChild(name)
	assert(instance:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		"[PlayerCarousel] Expected ModuleScript: " .. name)
	return require(instance)
end

local ContextMenuUtil = requireModule("ContextMenuUtil")

local PlayerCarousel = {}
PlayerCarousel.__index = PlayerCarousel

local selectedPlayerFrame
local uiPageLayout
local playerChangedEvent
local buttonToPlayerMap = {}
local playerToButtonMap = {}
local inputConnection

local function getSelectedObject()
	local selected

	pcall(function()
		selected = GuiService.SelectedCoreObject
	end)

	if selected then
		return selected
	end

	pcall(function()
		selected = GuiService.SelectedObject
	end)

	return selected
end

local function setSelectedObject(object)
	local ok = pcall(function()
		GuiService.SelectedCoreObject = object
	end)

	if not ok then
		pcall(function()
			GuiService.SelectedObject = object
		end)
	end
end

local function createMenuCarousel()
	local selection = Instance.new("Frame")
	selection.Name = "PlayerCarousel"
	selection.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	selection.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selection.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	selection.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.28, 0)
	selection.ClipsDescendants = true

	local innerFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	innerFrame.Name = "InnerFrame"
	innerFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	innerFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	innerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	innerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 1, 0)
	innerFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
	innerFrame.Active = true
	innerFrame.Parent = selection

	selectedPlayerFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	selectedPlayerFrame.Name = "SelectedPlayer"
	selectedPlayerFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	selectedPlayerFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	selectedPlayerFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	selectedPlayerFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, -10)
	selectedPlayerFrame.Parent = innerFrame

	uiPageLayout = Instance.new("UIPageLayout")
	uiPageLayout.EasingDirection = Enum.EasingDirection.Out
	uiPageLayout.EasingStyle = Enum.EasingStyle.Quad
	uiPageLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
	uiPageLayout.TweenTime = PAGE_LAYOUT_TWEEN_TIME
	uiPageLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
	uiPageLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
	uiPageLayout.TouchInputEnabled = false
	uiPageLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

	local selectedAspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	selectedAspect.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Height
	selectedAspect.Parent = selectedPlayerFrame

	playerChangedEvent = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	playerChangedEvent.Name = "PlayerChanged"

	uiPageLayout:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentPage"):Connect(function()
		local currentPage = uiPageLayout.CurrentPage

		for button in pairs(buttonToPlayerMap) do
			button.BackgroundColor3 = button == currentPage
				and BACKGROUND_SELECTED_COLOR
				or BACKGROUND_DEFAULT_COLOR
		end

		setSelectedObject(currentPage)
		playerChangedEvent:Fire(
			currentPage and buttonToPlayerMap[currentPage] or nil
		)
	end)

	uiPageLayout.Parent = selectedPlayerFrame

	local nextButton = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	nextButton.Name = "NextButton"
	nextButton.Image = "rbxassetid://471630112"
	nextButton.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	nextButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
	nextButton.Position = UDim2.new(1, -5, 0.5, 0)
	nextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.3, 0, 0.3, 0)
	nextButton.Selectable = false
	nextButton.Parent = selection

	local nextAspect = Instance.new("UIAspectRatioConstraint")
	nextAspect.DominantAxis = Enum.DominantAxis.Width
	nextAspect.Parent = nextButton

	local prevButton = nextButton:Clone()
	prevButton.Name = "PrevButton"
	prevButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
	prevButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0.5, 0)
	prevButton.Rotation = 180
	prevButton.Parent = selection

	nextButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if uiPageLayout then
			uiPageLayout:Next()
		end
	end)

	prevButton.Activated:Connect(function()
		if uiPageLayout then
			uiPageLayout:Previous()
		end
	end)

	local function updateArrowVisibility()
		local touch = UserInputService:GetLastInputType()
			== Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		local count = 0

		for _ in pairs(buttonToPlayerMap) do
			count += 1
		end

		local visible = not touch and count &gt; 1
		nextButton.Visible = visible
		prevButton.Visible = visible
	end

	if inputConnection then
		inputConnection:Disconnect()
	end

	inputConnection = UserInputService.LastInputTypeChanged:Connect(
		updateArrowVisibility
	)

	PlayerCarousel._updateArrowVisibility = updateArrowVisibility
	updateArrowVisibility()

	return selection
end

function PlayerCarousel:ClearPlayerEntries()
	for button in pairs(buttonToPlayerMap) do
		pcall(function()
			button:Destroy()
		end)
	end

	table.clear(buttonToPlayerMap)
	table.clear(playerToButtonMap)

	setSelectedObject(nil)

	if self._updateArrowVisibility then
		self._updateArrowVisibility()
	end
end

function PlayerCarousel:CreatePlayerEntry(player, distanceToLocalPlayer)
	if not player or not selectedPlayerFrame or not uiPageLayout then
		return
	end

	local existing = playerToButtonMap[player]
	if existing and existing.Parent then
		existing.LayoutOrder = tonumber(distanceToLocalPlayer) or 0
		return
	end

	local button = Instance.new("ImageButton")
	button.Name = player.Name
	button.BorderSizePixel = 0
	button.LayoutOrder = tonumber(distanceToLocalPlayer) or 0
	button.BackgroundColor3 = BACKGROUND_DEFAULT_COLOR
	button.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	button.Image = ""
	button.Selectable = true

	buttonToPlayerMap[button] = player
	playerToButtonMap[player] = button

	button.SelectionLost:Connect(function()
		if button ~= uiPageLayout.CurrentPage then
			button.BackgroundColor3 = BACKGROUND_DEFAULT_COLOR
		end
	end)

	button.SelectionGained:Connect(function()
		button.BackgroundColor3 = BACKGROUND_SELECTED_COLOR
	end)

	local loadingTween = TweenService:Create(
		button,
		TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
		{
			BackgroundTransparency = 1,
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
		}
	)

	loadingTween:Play()

	button.Activated:Connect(function()
		if uiPageLayout and button.Parent then
			uiPageLayout:JumpTo(button)
		end
	end)

	button.Parent = selectedPlayerFrame

	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, thumbnail = pcall(function()
			return ContextMenuUtil:GetHeadshotForPlayer(player)
		end)

		if not button.Parent then
			loadingTween:Cancel()
			return
		end

		loadingTween:Cancel()

		if ok and type(thumbnail) == "string" then
			button.Image = thumbnail
		else
			warn("[PlayerCarousel] Headshot failed for "
				.. player.Name .. ": " .. tostring(thumbnail))
		end

		button.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		button.BackgroundColor3 = button == uiPageLayout.CurrentPage
			and BACKGROUND_SELECTED_COLOR
			or BACKGROUND_DEFAULT_COLOR

		if self._updateArrowVisibility then
			self._updateArrowVisibility()
		end
	end)

	if self._updateArrowVisibility then
		self._updateArrowVisibility()
	end
end

function PlayerCarousel:SwitchToPlayerEntry(player, dontTween)
	if not player or not uiPageLayout then
		return
	end

	local button = playerToButtonMap[player]
	if not button then
		self:CreatePlayerEntry(player, 0)
		button = playerToButtonMap[player]
	end

	if not button or not button.Parent then
		return
	end

	local oldTweenTime = uiPageLayout.TweenTime
	if dontTween then
		uiPageLayout.TweenTime = 0
	end

	uiPageLayout:JumpTo(button)

	if dontTween then
		task.defer(function()
			if uiPageLayout then
				uiPageLayout.TweenTime = oldTweenTime
			end
		end)
	end
end

function PlayerCarousel:OffsetPlayerEntry(offset)
	if not uiPageLayout or offset == 0 then
		return
	end

	if offset &gt; 0 then
		uiPageLayout:Next()
	else
		uiPageLayout:Previous()
	end
end

function PlayerCarousel:GetSelectedPlayer()
	if not uiPageLayout or not uiPageLayout.CurrentPage then
		return nil
	end

	return buttonToPlayerMap[uiPageLayout.CurrentPage]
end

function PlayerCarousel.new()
	local self = setmetatable({}, PlayerCarousel)
	self.rbxGui = createMenuCarousel()
	self.PlayerChanged = playerChangedEvent.Event
	return self
end

return PlayerCarousel.new()</ProtectedString>
                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-462</int64>
                                    <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                                </Properties>
                            </Item>
                        </Item>
                        <Item class="Folder" referent="RBX568">
                            <Properties>
                                <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                <Color3 name="IconTint">
                                    <R>0</R>
                                    <G>0</G>
                                    <B>0</B>
                                </Color3>
                                <string name="Name">Common</string>
                                <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-102</int64>
                                <BinaryString name="Tags" />
                            </Properties>
                            <Item class="ModuleScript" referent="RBX569">
                                <Properties>
                                    <BinaryString name="AttributesSerialize" />
                                    <SecurityCapabilities name="Capabilities">0</SecurityCapabilities>
                                    <bool name="DefinesCapabilities">false</bool>
                                    <ContentId name="LinkedSource">
                                        <null />
                                    </ContentId>
                                    <string name="Name">ObjectPool</string>
                                    <string name="ScriptGuid">{39B5598E-D28F-480C-AEA1-458D003626D4}</string>
                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: ObjectPool.lua
    Written by: TheGamer101
    Yeku legacy CoreGui compatibility patch

    Description:
        Pools Instances to avoid unnecessary allocations.

    Compatibility:
        * Preserves module.new(poolSizePerType).
        * Preserves GetInstance(className) and ReturnInstance(instance).
        * Uses a default capacity when no pool size is supplied.
        * Unparents instances before pooling.
        * Avoids placing the same instance in the pool twice.
        * Skips invalid or externally reparented pooled instances.
        * Safely handles invalid return values and zero-capacity pools.
]]

local module = {}

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local DEFAULT_POOL_SIZE = 50

local function normalizePoolSize(value)
	if value == nil then
		return DEFAULT_POOL_SIZE
	end

	local numericValue = tonumber(value)

	if not numericValue
		or numericValue ~= numericValue
		or numericValue == math.huge
		or numericValue == -math.huge then
		return DEFAULT_POOL_SIZE
	end

	return math.max(0, math.floor(numericValue))
end

local function getClassName(instance)
	if typeof(instance) ~= "Instance" then
		return nil
	end

	local ok, className = pcall(function()
		return instance.ClassName
	end)

	if ok and type(className) == "string" then
		return className
	end

	return nil
end

local function detachInstance(instance)
	local ok = pcall(function()
		instance.Parent = nil
	end)

	return ok
end

local function safelyDestroy(instance)
	if typeof(instance) ~= "Instance" then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		instance:Destroy()
	end)
end

function methods:GetInstance(className)
	assert(
		type(className) == "string" and className ~= "",
		"ObjectPool:GetInstance expects a valid class name."
	)

	local pools = self.InstancePoolsByClass

	if pools[className] == nil then
		pools[className] = {}
	end

	local availableInstances = pools[className]

	while #availableInstances &gt; 0 do
		local index = #availableInstances
		local instance = availableInstances[index]

		availableInstances[index] = nil

		if instance then
			self.PooledInstances[instance] = nil

			if getClassName(instance) == className then
				-- A pooled instance must be detached and reusable.
				if detachInstance(instance) then
					return instance
				end
			end

			safelyDestroy(instance)
		end
	end

	return Instance.new(className)
end

function methods:ReturnInstance(instance)
	local className = getClassName(instance)

	if not className then
		return false
	end

	-- Returning the same instance twice must not create duplicate pool
	-- entries, which could otherwise hand out the same GUI twice.
	if self.PooledInstances[instance] then
		return false
	end

	-- Never leave a pooled chat label visible in its previous container.
	if not detachInstance(instance) then
		safelyDestroy(instance)
		return false
	end

	local pools = self.InstancePoolsByClass

	if pools[className] == nil then
		pools[className] = {}
	end

	local availableInstances = pools[className]

	if #availableInstances &lt; self.PoolSizePerType then
		availableInstances[#availableInstances + 1] = instance
		self.PooledInstances[instance] = true
		return true
	end

	safelyDestroy(instance)
	return false
end

function module.new(poolSizePerType)
	local object = setmetatable({}, methods)

	object.InstancePoolsByClass = {}
	object.PooledInstances = setmetatable({}, {
		__mode = "k",
	})

	object.Name = "ObjectPool"
	object.PoolSizePerType = normalizePoolSize(poolSizePerType)

	return object
end

                                    <int64 name="SourceAssetId">-463</int64>

                            </Item>

                                    <string name="ScriptGuid">{E111E964-7022-4292-8708-1FA6A07ED7A0}</string>
                                    <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                    <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    Filename: CommonUtil.lua
    Written by: dbanks
    Yeku legacy CoreGui compatibility patch

    Description:
        Common utility functions for historical Roblox CoreScripts.

    Compatibility:
        * Preserves TableConcat, SortByName, and Create.
        * Preserves the callable Create("ClassName") { ... } API.
        * Applies properties before parenting.
        * Attaches numeric-key child Instances in numeric-key order.
        * Attaches the requested Parent last.
        * Adds useful validation without changing valid call behavior.
]]

local CommonUtil = {}

-- Concatenate t2 onto t1 and return the modified t1.
function CommonUtil.TableConcat(t1, t2)
	assert(type(t1) == "table", "TableConcat expects its first argument to be a table.")
	assert(type(t2) == "table", "TableConcat expects its second argument to be a table.")

	for index = 1, #t2 do
		t1[#t1 + 1] = t2[index]
	end

	return t1
end

-- Sort Instances by their Name property and return the same table.
function CommonUtil.SortByName(items)
	assert(type(items) == "table", "SortByName expects a table.")

	local function compareInstanceNames(itemA, itemB)
		assert(itemA ~= nil and itemA.Name ~= nil,
			"SortByName found an item without a Name property.")

		assert(itemB ~= nil and itemB.Name ~= nil,
			"SortByName found an item without a Name property.")

		return itemA.Name &lt; itemB.Name
	end

	table.sort(items, compareInstanceNames)

	return items
end

--[[
    Creates Instances using the historical CoreScripts syntax:

    local newFrame = CommonUtil.Create("Frame") {
        Name = "Example",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,

        CommonUtil.Create("TextLabel") {
            Name = "Title",
            Text = "Hello",
        },

        Parent = someParent,
    }

    Numeric keys denote child Instances. Parent is assigned only after
    properties and child Instances have been initialized.
]]

function CommonUtil.Create(instanceType)
	assert(
		type(instanceType) == "string" and instanceType ~= "",
		"Create expects an Instance class name."
	)

	return function(data)
		assert(
			data == nil or type(data) == "table",
			"Create expects a property table."
		)

		data = data or {}

		local object = Instance.new(instanceType)
		local children = {}

		local parent = nil
		local parentProvided = false

		-- Separate properties, child Instances, and Parent. Do not parent
		-- the new object until all of its normal properties are assigned.
		for key, value in pairs(data) do
			if type(key) == "number" then
				children[#children + 1] = {
					Index = key,
					Instance = value,
				}
			elseif key == "Parent" then
				parent = value
				parentProvided = true
			else
				object[key] = value
			end
		end

		-- pairs() does not guarantee numeric-key order. Restore that order
		-- so the legacy constructor behaves consistently.
		table.sort(children, function(itemA, itemB)
			return itemA.Index &lt; itemB.Index
		end)

		for _, childEntry in ipairs(children) do
			local child = childEntry.Instance

			assert(
				typeof(child) == "Instance",
				"Create received a non-Instance at numeric child key "
					.. tostring(childEntry.Index)
					.. "."
			)

			child.Parent = object
		end

		if parentProvided then
			object.Parent = parent
		end

		return object
	end
end

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">--[[
    FileName: ChatScript.lua
    Yeku Legacy CoreGui chat startup/SetCore compatibility fix.

    Settings and constants are read ONLY from this package's
    script.Parent.DefaultClientChatModules folder.
