local StarterGui = game:GetService("StarterGui")
local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
local VRService = game:GetService("VRService")

local MAX_COREGUI_CONNECTION_ATTEMPTS = 10
local ClientChatFolder = script.Parent
local YEKU_CHAT_SCRIPT_REVISION = "YekuChatScript_BundledModules_GetCoreSelfFix_v2_2026-10-21"

local function findChildWithTimeout(parent, name, timeout)
	if not parent then return nil end
	local child = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
	if child then return child end
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		return parent:WaitForChild(name, timeout or 10)
	end)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end

local DefaultClientChatModules = findChildWithTimeout(
	ClientChatFolder,
	"DefaultClientChatModules",
	10
)
assert(
	DefaultClientChatModules and DefaultClientChatModules:IsA("Folder"),
	"[ChatScript] Missing bundled DefaultClientChatModules folder under "
		.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
)

local ChatSettingsModule = findChildWithTimeout(DefaultClientChatModules, "ChatSettings", 10)
assert(
	ChatSettingsModule and ChatSettingsModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[ChatScript] Missing DefaultClientChatModules.ChatSettings."
)
local settingsOK, ChatSettings = pcall(require, ChatSettingsModule)
assert(
	settingsOK and type(ChatSettings) == "table",
	"[ChatScript] Bundled ChatSettings failed or did not return a table: "
		.. tostring(ChatSettings)
)

local ChatConstantsModule = findChildWithTimeout(DefaultClientChatModules, "ChatConstants", 10)
assert(
	ChatConstantsModule and ChatConstantsModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[ChatScript] Missing DefaultClientChatModules.ChatConstants."
)
local constantsOK, ChatConstants = pcall(require, ChatConstantsModule)
assert(
	constantsOK and type(ChatConstants) == "table",
	"[ChatScript] Bundled ChatConstants failed or did not return a table: "
		.. tostring(ChatConstants)
)

local ChatMainModule = findChildWithTimeout(ClientChatFolder, "ChatMain", 10)
assert(
	ChatMainModule and ChatMainModule:IsA("ModuleScript"),
	"[ChatScript] Missing sibling ModuleScript ChatMain under "
		.. ClientChatFolder:GetFullName()
)

local function callLegacy(object, methodName, ...)
	if not object then return false, "missing chat API" end
	local ok, method = pcall(function() return object[methodName] end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return false, "missing Chat." .. tostring(methodName)
	end
	-- The ChatMain public API uses colon-style methods and expects its API
	-- table as self. Keep this consistent for both events and functions.
	return pcall(method, object, ...)
end

local function fireLegacy(signal, ...)
	if not signal then return false, "missing signal" end
	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return signal.fire or signal.Fire
	end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then
		return false, "signal has no fire/Fire method"
	end
	return pcall(method, signal, ...)
end

local function connectLegacy(signal, callback)
	if not signal then return nil end
	local ok, method = pcall(function()
		return signal.connect or signal.Connect
	end)
	if not ok or type(method) ~= "function" then return nil end
	local connected, connection = pcall(method, signal, callback)
	if connected then return connection end
	return nil
end

local function SetCoreGuiChatConnections(containerTable)
	local lastError
	for attempt = 1, MAX_COREGUI_CONNECTION_ATTEMPTS do
		local success, result = pcall(function()
			StarterGui:SetCore("CoreGuiChatConnections", containerTable)
		end)
		if success then return true end
		lastError = result
		if attempt &lt; MAX_COREGUI_CONNECTION_ATTEMPTS then
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end
	error(
		"[ChatScript] SetCore(CoreGuiChatConnections) failed after retries: "
			.. tostring(lastError),
		0
	)
end

local bubbleEnabled = ChatSettings.BubbleChatEnabled == true
local classicEnabled = ChatSettings.ClassicChatEnabled ~= false

local function chatTypesDisabled()
	return not bubbleEnabled and not classicEnabled
end

local function DoEverything()
	local requireOK, Chat = pcall(require, ChatMainModule)
	assert(requireOK, "[ChatScript] ChatMain failed to start: " .. tostring(Chat))
	assert(type(Chat) == "table", "[ChatScript] ChatMain did not return an API table.")

	local containerTable = {
		ChatWindow = {
			ChatTypes = {
				BubbleChatEnabled = bubbleEnabled,
				ClassicChatEnabled = classicEnabled,
			},
		},
		SetCore = {},
		GetCore = {},
	}

	local function connectEvent(name)
		local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		event.Name = name
		containerTable.ChatWindow[name] = event
		event.Event:Connect(function(...)
			local ok, err = callLegacy(Chat, name, ...)
			if not ok then
				warn("[ChatScript] Chat." .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)
	end

	local function connectFunction(name)
		local func = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
		func.Name = name
		containerTable.ChatWindow[name] = func
		func.OnInvoke = function(...)
			local ok, result = callLegacy(Chat, name, ...)
			if not ok then
				warn("[ChatScript] Chat." .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(result))
				return nil
			end
			return result
		end
	end

	local function reverseConnectEvent(name)
		local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		event.Name = name
		containerTable.ChatWindow[name] = event
		local connection = connectLegacy(Chat[name], function(...)
			event:Fire(...)
		end)
		if not connection then
			warn("[ChatScript] Chat signal is unavailable: " .. name)
		end
	end

	local function connectSignal(name)
		local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		event.Name = name
		containerTable.ChatWindow[name] = event
		event.Event:Connect(function(...)
			local ok, err = fireLegacy(Chat[name], ...)
			if not ok then
				warn("[ChatScript] Chat signal fire failed: " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)
	end

	local function connectSetCore(name)
		local event = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
		event.Name = name
		containerTable.SetCore[name] = event
		event.Event:Connect(function(...)
			local ok, err = fireLegacy(Chat[name .. "Event"], ...)
			if not ok then
				warn("[ChatScript] SetCore event forwarding failed: " .. name .. ": " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end)
	end

	local function connectGetCore(name)
		local func = Instance.new("BindableFunction")
		func.Name = name
		containerTable.GetCore[name] = func
		func.OnInvoke = function(...)
			local method
			local lookupOK, lookupErr = pcall(function()
				method = Chat["f" .. name]
			end)
			if not lookupOK or type(method) ~= "function" then
				warn("[ChatScript] Missing Chat.f" .. name .. ": " .. tostring(lookupErr))
				return nil
			end
			local args = table.pack(...)
			-- FIX: these ChatMain f* getters are colon-style methods too.
			-- Passing args without Chat as self made GuiObject lookups fail.
			local ok, result = pcall(function()
				return method(Chat, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
			end)
			if not ok then
				warn("[ChatScript] GetCore " .. name .. " failed: " .. tostring(result))
				return nil
			end
			return result
		end
	end

	connectEvent("ToggleVisibility")
	connectEvent("SetVisible")
	connectEvent("FocusChatBar")
	connectFunction("GetVisibility")
	connectFunction("GetMessageCount")
	connectEvent("TopbarEnabledChanged")
	connectFunction("IsFocused")

	reverseConnectEvent("ChatBarFocusChanged")
	reverseConnectEvent("VisibilityStateChanged")
	reverseConnectEvent("MessagesChanged")
	reverseConnectEvent("MessagePosted")

	connectSignal("CoreGuiEnabled")

	connectSetCore("ChatMakeSystemMessage")
	connectSetCore("ChatWindowPosition")
	connectSetCore("ChatWindowSize")
	connectGetCore("ChatWindowPosition")
	connectGetCore("ChatWindowSize")
	connectSetCore("ChatBarDisabled")
	connectGetCore("ChatBarDisabled")
	connectEvent("SpecialKeyPressed")

	SetCoreGuiChatConnections(containerTable)
	print("[ChatScript] Bundled legacy chat frontend registered.")
end

local isTenFootInterface = false
pcall(function() isTenFootInterface = GuiService:IsTenFootInterface() end)
local vrEnabled = false
pcall(function() vrEnabled = VRService.VREnabled end)

if not isTenFootInterface and not vrEnabled then
	if not chatTypesDisabled() then
		DoEverything()
	else
		SetCoreGuiChatConnections({
			ChatWindow = {
				ChatTypes = {
					BubbleChatEnabled = false,
					ClassicChatEnabled = false,
				},
			},
		})
		print("[ChatScript] Both chat types are disabled by the experience.")
	end
else
	print("[ChatScript] Legacy chat UI skipped for TV/VR layout.")
end

print("[ChatScript] source revision loaded: " .. YEKU_CHAT_SCRIPT_REVISION)

-- BOUNDED MODULE LOOKUPS

local function findChildWithTimeout(parent, childName, timeout)
	if not parent then
		return nil
	end

	local child = parent:FindFirstChild(childName)
	if child then
		return child
	end

	return parent:WaitForChild(childName, timeout or 10)
end

local function requireModule(parent, moduleName, timeout)
	local moduleScript = findChildWithTimeout(parent, moduleName, timeout or 10)

	assert(
		moduleScript ~= nil,
		string.format(
			"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Missing ModuleScript '%s' under %s.",
			moduleName,
			parent and parent:GetFullName() or "&lt;nil&gt;"
		)
	)

	assert(
		moduleScript:IsA("ModuleScript"),
		string.format(
			"[Yeku Legacy Chat] '%s' under %s is %s, not a ModuleScript.",
			moduleName,
			parent:GetFullName(),
			moduleScript.ClassName
		)
	)

	local success, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
	if not success then
		error(
			"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Failed requiring "
				.. moduleScript:GetFullName()
				.. ": "
				.. tostring(result),
			2
		)
	end

	return result
end

local ClientChatFolder = script.Parent

-- The bundled settings modules MUST be resolved relative to this ChatMain.
-- Do not fall back to a separate legacy chat folder or to an ancestor's modules:
-- those folders belong to other chat implementations and can return a nil or
-- incompatible settings table under the custom loader.

	"[Yeku Legacy Chat] DefaultClientChatModules.MessageCreatorModules.Util did not return a table")

-- Compatibility with older bundled ChatConstants revisions. Some copies omit
-- MessageTypeMeCommand; the legacy MeCommandMessage processor uses it as a table
-- key and otherwise fails with "table index is nil" during CommandProcessor.new().
local function ensureChatConstant(name, fallbackValue)
	if ChatConstants[name] == nil then
		local ok = pcall(function()
			ChatConstants[name] = fallbackValue
		end)
		if not ok then
			warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Could not supply missing ChatConstants." .. name)
		else
			print("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Restored missing ChatConstants." .. name)
		end
	end
end

ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeDefault", "Message")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeSay", ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault or "Message")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeSystem", "System")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeMeCommand", ChatConstants.MessageTypeMe or "MeCommand")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeMe", ChatConstants.MessageTypeMeCommand or "MeCommand")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeSetCore", "SetCore")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeWelcome", "Welcome")
ensureChatConstant("MessageTypeWhisper", "Whisper")

		ChatLocalization = result

	end

	ChatLocalization = {

			return defaultText
		end,
	}

local modulesRoot = ClientChatFolder.Parent and ClientChatFolder.Parent.Parent
local legacyBubbleChatModule = modulesRoot and modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("LegacyBubbleChat")
if legacyBubbleChatModule and legacyBubbleChatModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
	local requireOK, renderer = pcall(require, legacyBubbleChatModule)
	if requireOK and type(renderer) == "table" and type(renderer.Initialize) == "function" then
		local backend = nil
		local newChatModule = modulesRoot:FindFirstChild("NewChat")
		if newChatModule and newChatModule:IsA("ModuleScript") then
			local backendOK, result = pcall(require, newChatModule)
			if backendOK and type(result) == "table" then backend = result end
		end
		local initOK, initResult = pcall(renderer.Initialize, backend)
		if initOK and initResult ~= false then
			print("[Yeku Legacy Chat] 2015/2016 large-text LegacyBubbleChat initialized")
		else
			warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] 2015/2016 bubble renderer initialization failed: " .. tostring(initResult))
		end
	else
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Dedicated bubble renderer require failed or has no Initialize method: " .. tostring(renderer))
	end
else
	warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Modules.LegacyBubbleChat is missing")
end

local function isYekuBridgeFolder(instance)
	if not instance or not instance:IsA("Folder") then
		return false
	end
	local success, value = pcall(function()
		return instance:GetAttribute("YekuTextChatBridge") == true
	end)
	return success and value
end

local function isClientPlaceholder(instance)
	if not instance then
		return false
	end
	local success, value = pcall(function()
		return instance:GetAttribute("YekuClientPlaceholder") == true
	end)
	return success and value
end

local requiredRemoteClasses = {
	OnNewMessage = "RemoteEvent",
	OnMessageDoneFiltering = "RemoteEvent",
	OnNewSystemMessage = "RemoteEvent",
	OnChannelJoined = "RemoteEvent",
	OnChannelLeft = "RemoteEvent",
	OnMuted = "RemoteEvent",
	OnUnmuted = "RemoteEvent",
	OnMainChannelSet = "RemoteEvent",
	SayMessageRequest = "RemoteEvent",
	GetInitDataRequest = "RemoteFunction",
}

local function getCompleteLegacyRemoteSet(folder)
	if not folder or not folder:IsA("Folder") or isClientPlaceholder(folder) then
		return nil
	end

	local result = {}
	for name, className in pairs(requiredRemoteClasses) do
		local instance = folder:FindFirstChild(name)
		if not instance
			or not instance:IsA(className)
			or isClientPlaceholder(instance) then
			return nil
		end
		result[name] = instance
	end

	return result
end

local realLegacyRemotes = getCompleteLegacyRemoteSet(DefaultChatSystemChatEvents)
local usingYekuBridge = false
local usingLegacyRemotes = false
local usingInternalTextChatBridge = false
local EventFolder = {}

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- EVENT ADAPTERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function makeBindableEventProxy(bindable)
	assert(bindable and bindable:IsA("BindableEvent"), "Expected a BindableEvent")
	return {
		OnClientEvent = bindable.Event,
		Fire = function(_, ...)
			bindable:Fire(...)
		end,
	}
end

local function makeBridgeEventProxy(name)
	local event = BridgeFolder and BridgeFolder:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not event then
		error("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Missing bridge event: " .. name)
	end
	if not event:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		error(
			"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Bridge object "
				.. name
				.. " must be a BindableEvent, found "
				.. event.ClassName
		)
	end
	return makeBindableEventProxy(event)
end

local function makeLocalEventProxy(name)
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	bindable.Name = "Internal_" .. name
	bindable.Parent = script
	return makeBindableEventProxy(bindable)
end

local function getTextChannelsFolder()
	return TextChatService:FindFirstChild("TextChannels")
end

local function getDisplayChannelName(textChannel)
	if not textChannel then
		return ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
	end
	if textChannel.Name == "RBXGeneral" then
		return ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
	end
	if textChannel.Name == "RBXSystem" then
		return "System"
	end
	return textChannel.Name
end

local function listTextChannels()
	local result = {}
	local folder = getTextChannelsFolder()
	if not folder then
		return result
	end
	for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextChannel") then
			table.insert(result, child)
		end
	end
	table.sort(result, function(a, b)
		return a.Name &lt; b.Name
	end)
	return result
end

local function findTextChannel(channelName)
	local channels = listTextChannels()
	local generalName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"

	if not channelName or channelName == "" or channelName == generalName then
		for _, channel in ipairs(channels) do
			if channel.Name == "RBXGeneral" then
				return channel
			end
		end
	end

	if channelName == "System" then
		for _, channel in ipairs(channels) do
			if channel.Name == "RBXGeneral" then
				return channel
			end
		end
	end

	for _, channel in ipairs(channels) do
		if channel.Name == channelName or getDisplayChannelName(channel) == channelName then
			return channel
		end
	end

	for _, channel in ipairs(channels) do
		if channel.Name == "RBXGeneral" then
			return channel
		end
	end

	return channels[1]
end

local DEFAULT_GENERAL_WELCOME = ChatLocalization:Get(
	"GameChat_ChatCommandsTeller_AllChannelWelcomeMessage",
	"Chat '/?' or '/help' for a list of chat commands."
)

local function makeTextChatInitData()
	local rows, included = {}, {}
	local generalName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"

	local function addChannel(name)
		if not name or name == "" then
			return
		end
		local key = string.lower(name)
		if included[key] then
			return
		end
		included[key] = true
		local welcomeMessage = key == string.lower(generalName) and DEFAULT_GENERAL_WELCOME or ""
		table.insert(rows, { name, welcomeMessage, {}, nil })
	end

	addChannel(generalName)
	for _, channel in ipairs(listTextChannels()) do
		addChannel(getDisplayChannelName(channel))
	end
	return { Channels = rows }
end

local function sendUsingTextChat(message, channelName)
	if typeof(message) ~= "string" or message == "" then
		return false
	end

	local target = findTextChannel(channelName)
	if not target then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] No TextChannel exists; message not sent.")
		return false
	end

	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return target:SendAsync(message)
	end)
	if not success then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] TextChannel:SendAsync failed: " .. tostring(result))
		return false
	end

	if result and result.Status and result.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] TextChatService returned status: " .. tostring(result.Status))
	end
	return success
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- SELECT BACKEND
----------------------------------------------------------------

if textChatServiceActive and isYekuBridgeFolder(BridgeFolder) then
	usingYekuBridge = true

	for _, name in ipairs({
		"OnNewMessage", "OnMessageDoneFiltering", "OnNewSystemMessage",
		"OnChannelJoined", "OnChannelLeft", "OnMuted", "OnUnmuted", "OnMainChannelSet",
		}) do
		EventFolder[name] = makeBridgeEventProxy(name)
	end

	local bridgeSend = BridgeFolder:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
	local bridgeInit = BridgeFolder:FindFirstChild("GetInitDataRequest")
	assert(bridgeSend and bridgeSend:IsA("BindableEvent"),
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Bridge SayMessageRequest BindableEvent is missing")
	assert(bridgeInit and bridgeInit:IsA("BindableFunction"),
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Bridge GetInitDataRequest BindableFunction is missing")

	EventFolder.SayMessageRequest = {
		FireServer = function(_, message, channelName)
			bridgeSend:Fire(message, channelName)
		end,
	}
	EventFolder.GetInitDataRequest = {
		InvokeServer = function()
			return bridgeInit:Invoke()
		end,
	}

	local colorEvent = BridgeFolder:FindFirstChild("ChannelNameColorUpdated")
	if colorEvent and colorEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
		EventFolder.ChannelNameColorUpdated = makeBindableEventProxy(colorEvent)
	end

elseif not textChatServiceActive and realLegacyRemotes then
	usingLegacyRemotes = true
	EventFolder = realLegacyRemotes

elseif textChatServiceActive then
	usingInternalTextChatBridge = true

	for _, name in ipairs({
		"OnNewMessage", "OnMessageDoneFiltering", "OnNewSystemMessage",
		"OnChannelJoined", "OnChannelLeft", "OnMuted", "OnUnmuted", "OnMainChannelSet",
		}) do
		EventFolder[name] = makeLocalEventProxy(name)
	end

	EventFolder.SayMessageRequest = {
		FireServer = function(_, message, channelName)
			sendUsingTextChat(message, channelName)
		end,
	}
	EventFolder.GetInitDataRequest = {
		InvokeServer = function()
			return makeTextChatInitData()
		end,
	}
	EventFolder.ChannelNameColorUpdated = nil

elseif isYekuBridgeFolder(BridgeFolder) then
	usingYekuBridge = true

	for _, name in ipairs({
		"OnNewMessage", "OnMessageDoneFiltering", "OnNewSystemMessage",
		"OnChannelJoined", "OnChannelLeft", "OnMuted", "OnUnmuted", "OnMainChannelSet",
		}) do
		EventFolder[name] = makeBridgeEventProxy(name)
	end

	local bridgeSend = findChildWithTimeout(BridgeFolder, "SayMessageRequest", 10)
	local bridgeInit = findChildWithTimeout(BridgeFolder, "GetInitDataRequest", 10)
	assert(bridgeSend and bridgeSend:IsA("BindableEvent"),
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Bridge SayMessageRequest is missing")
	assert(bridgeInit and bridgeInit:IsA("BindableFunction"),
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Bridge GetInitDataRequest is missing")

	EventFolder.SayMessageRequest = {
		FireServer = function(_, message, channelName)
			bridgeSend:Fire(message, channelName)
		end,
	}
	EventFolder.GetInitDataRequest = {
		InvokeServer = function()
			return bridgeInit:Invoke()
		end,
	}
else
	error(
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] No usable chat backend. "
			.. "Install YekuLegacyChatBridge for TextChatService, "
			.. "or supply the complete server-owned legacy remotes."
	)
end

print(
	"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Backend selected: "
		.. (usingYekuBridge and "YekuLegacyChatBridge"
			or usingLegacyRemotes and "Legacy server remotes"
			or "Internal TextChatService fallback")
)

end

	end
end

local moduleChatWindow = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "ChatWindow", 10)
local moduleChatBar = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "ChatBar", 10)
local moduleChannelsBar = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "ChannelsBar", 10)
local moduleMessageLabelCreator = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "MessageLabelCreator", 10)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- PATCH THE CREATOR BEFORE MESSAGES ARE MEASURED / INSERTED
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- The old DescendantAdded-only patch ran after MessageLogDisplay had started
-- positioning a new row. A creator could therefore calculate a one-line row
-- height first, then get TextWrapped=true too late. Intercept the creator's
-- factory BEFORE MessageLogDisplay is required and constructs its private creator instance.
-- Existing Font/TextSize/color settings from each creator remain intact.
local function applyCreatedMessageObjectTextSupport(messageObject, messageData)
	if not messageObject then
		return
	end

	local function configureTextObject(instance)
		if not instance or not (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton")) then
			return
		end

		pcall(function()
			instance.RichText = true
			instance.TextWrapped = true
			-- All message rows share the configured ChatWindow size.
			instance.TextSize = tonumber(ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize) or 19
		end)

		-- ChatMakeSystemMessage supports these style fields. Apply them after
		-- the legacy creator creates its labels, but before height measurement.
		-- Do not apply NameColor to every child; the legacy creator owns that.
		local extraData = type(messageData) == "table" and messageData.ExtraData or nil
		local setCoreType = ChatConstants.MessageTypeSetCore or "SetCore"
		if type(extraData) == "table" and messageData.MessageType == setCoreType then
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(extraData.Font) == "EnumItem" and extraData.Font.EnumType == Enum.Font then
					instance.Font = extraData.Font
				end
				-- Keep SetCore/system text the same size as player chat.
				instance.TextSize = ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize
				local color = extraData.Color or extraData.TextColor3 or extraData.MessageTextColor
				if typeof(color) == "Color3" then
					instance.TextColor3 = color
				end
				if typeof(extraData.TextStrokeColor3) == "Color3" then
					instance.TextStrokeColor3 = extraData.TextStrokeColor3
				end
				if type(extraData.TextTransparency) == "number" then
					instance.TextTransparency = math.clamp(extraData.TextTransparency, 0, 1)
				end
				if type(extraData.TextStrokeTransparency) == "number" then
					instance.TextStrokeTransparency = math.clamp(extraData.TextStrokeTransparency, 0, 1)
				end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function configureTree(root)
		if not root then
			return
		end
		configureTextObject(root)
		for _, descendant in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
			configureTextObject(descendant)
		end
	end

	configureTree(messageObject.BaseFrame)
	if messageObject.BaseMessage and not (messageObject.BaseFrame and messageObject.BaseMessage:IsDescendantOf(messageObject.BaseFrame)) then
		configureTree(messageObject.BaseMessage)
	end

	-- The legacy MessageLogDisplay asks this callback for the row height before
	-- parenting the message row. Some creators measure a single line using
	-- their default font settings. Measure the rendered body width with wrapping
	-- enabled as a lower bound, while preserving any existing padding from the
	-- creator's original height function.
	local baseMessage = messageObject.BaseMessage
	local originalGetHeightFunction = messageObject.GetHeightFunction
	if baseMessage and (baseMessage:IsA("TextLabel") or baseMessage:IsA("TextButton")) then
		local chatTextSize = tonumber(ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextSize) or 19
		local source = tostring(baseMessage.Text or "")
		local normalized = source:gsub("(&lt;font[^&gt;]-size%s*=%s*)['\"](%d+)['\"]", function(prefix)
			return prefix .. '"' .. tostring(chatTextSize) .. '"'
		end)
		normalized = normalized:gsub("(&lt;font[^&gt;]-size%s*=%s*)(%d+)", function(prefix)
			return prefix .. tostring(chatTextSize)
		end)
		if normalized ~= source then pcall(function() baseMessage.Text = normalized end) end
		pcall(function() baseMessage.TextSize = chatTextSize end)
	end
	if baseMessage
		and (baseMessage:IsA("TextLabel") or baseMessage:IsA("TextButton"))
		and type(originalGetHeightFunction) == "function" then
		messageObject.GetHeightFunction = function(width)
			local originalHeight = 0
			local heightOK, reportedHeight = pcall(originalGetHeightFunction, width)
			if heightOK and type(reportedHeight) == "number" then
				originalHeight = math.max(0, reportedHeight)
			end

			local sourceText = tostring(baseMessage.Text or "")
			local plainText = sourceText:gsub("&lt;[^&gt;]-&gt;", "")
			local measurementTextSize = math.max(1, tonumber(baseMessage.TextSize) or 14)
			local tagText = sourceText:gsub("[\"']", "")
			for sizeString in tagText:gmatch("&lt;font[^&gt;]-size%s*=%s*(%d+)") do
				measurementTextSize = math.max(measurementTextSize, tonumber(sizeString) or measurementTextSize)
			end

			local widthScale = baseMessage.Size.X.Scale
			local widthOffset = baseMessage.Size.X.Offset
			local textWidth = math.max(1, (tonumber(width) or 1) * widthScale + widthOffset)
			local measureOK, bounds = pcall(function()
				return TextService:GetTextSize(
					plainText,
					baseMessage.Font,
					measurementTextSize,
					Vector2.new(textWidth, 100000)
				)
			end)

			if measureOK and bounds and type(bounds.Y) == "number" then
				return math.max(originalHeight, bounds.Y + 2)
			end
			return originalHeight
		end
	end
end

if moduleMessageLabelCreator and type(moduleMessageLabelCreator.new) == "function"
	and not moduleMessageLabelCreator.__YekuEarlyRichTextWrapPatchV31 then
	local originalMessageLabelCreatorNew = moduleMessageLabelCreator.new
	moduleMessageLabelCreator.new = function(...)
		local creatorObject = originalMessageLabelCreatorNew(...)
		if creatorObject and type(creatorObject.CreateMessageLabel) == "function" then
			local originalCreateMessageLabel = creatorObject.CreateMessageLabel
			creatorObject.CreateMessageLabel = function(self, messageData, currentChannelName)
				local messageObject = originalCreateMessageLabel(self, messageData, currentChannelName)
				applyCreatedMessageObjectTextSupport(messageObject, messageData)
				return messageObject
			end
		end
		return creatorObject
	end
	moduleMessageLabelCreator.__YekuEarlyRichTextWrapPatchV31 = true
end

-- Require MessageLogDisplay only after patching MessageLabelCreator.new.
local moduleMessageLogDisplay = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "MessageLogDisplay", 10)
local moduleChatChannel = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "ChatChannel", 10)
local moduleCommandProcessor = requireModule(ClientChatFolder, "CommandProcessor", 10)

local ChannelsBar = moduleChannelsBar.new()
local MessageLogDisplay = moduleMessageLogDisplay.new()
local CommandProcessor = moduleCommandProcessor.new()

		return false
	end

		and child:FindFirstChild("ChannelsBarParentFrame", true) ~= nil

end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- MESSAGE TEXT FALLBACK: CATCH TEXT OBJECTS CREATED LAZILY BY A CREATOR
----------------------------------------------------------------
-- The early creator patch above is the primary path. This observer is retained
-- only for creators that add secondary labels after returning their message object.
local messageTextPatchConnections = {}
local messageArea = ChatWindow.GuiObjects and ChatWindow.GuiObjects.ChatChannelParentFrame

local function enableRichTextAndWrapping(instance)
	if not instance or not (instance:IsA("TextLabel") or instance:IsA("TextButton")) then
		return
	end
	if not messageArea or not instance:IsDescendantOf(messageArea) then
		return
	end
	pcall(function()
		instance.RichText = true
		instance.TextWrapped = true
	end)
end

local function patchExistingMessageText()
	if not messageArea then return end
	for _, descendant in ipairs(messageArea:GetDescendants()) do
		enableRichTextAndWrapping(descendant)
	end
end

if messageArea then
	patchExistingMessageText()
	table.insert(messageTextPatchConnections, messageArea.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(descendant)
		enableRichTextAndWrapping(descendant)
		for _, child in ipairs(descendant:GetDescendants()) do
			enableRichTextAndWrapping(child)
		end
	end))
end

MessageSender:RegisterSayMessageFunction(EventFolder.SayMessageRequest)

-- Never put the welcome string inside the editable ChatBar.
ChatBar:SetTextLabelText("")

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TEXT HELPERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function trimTrailingSpaces(str)
	local lastSpace = #str
	while lastSpace &gt; 0 do
		local char = string.sub(str, lastSpace, lastSpace)
		if not char:match("%s") then
			break
		end
		lastSpace -= 1
	end
	return string.sub(str, 1, lastSpace)
end

local function getMessageTypeDefault()
	return ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault
		or ChatConstants.MessageTypeSay
		or ChatConstants.MessageTypeSystem
end

local function getMessageTypeSystem()
	return ChatConstants.MessageTypeSystem
		or ChatConstants.MessageTypeDefault
		or ChatConstants.MessageTypeSay
end

local function getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
	if ChatSettings.EchoMessagesInGeneralChannel == nil then
		return true
	end
	return ChatSettings.EchoMessagesInGeneralChannel
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LEGACY SOLARCRANE USERNAME COLORS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local LEGACY_CHAT_COLORS = {
	Color3.new(253 / 255, 41 / 255, 67 / 255),
	Color3.new(1 / 255, 162 / 255, 255 / 255),
	Color3.new(2 / 255, 184 / 255, 87 / 255),
	BrickColor.new("Bright violet").Color,
	BrickColor.new("Bright orange").Color,
	BrickColor.new("Bright yellow").Color,
	BrickColor.new("Light reddish violet").Color,
	BrickColor.new("Brick yellow").Color,
}

local function legacyNameValue(playerName)
	local value = 0
	for index = 1, #playerName do
		local characterValue = string.byte(string.sub(playerName, index, index))
		local reverseIndex = #playerName - index + 1
		if #playerName % 2 == 1 then
			reverseIndex -= 1
		end
		if reverseIndex % 4 &gt;= 2 then
			characterValue = -characterValue
		end
		value += characterValue
	end
	return value
end

local function computeLegacyChatColor(playerName)
	if type(playerName) ~= "string" or playerName == "" then
		return ChatSettings.DefaultNameColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	end
	local index = (legacyNameValue(playerName) % #LEGACY_CHAT_COLORS) + 1
	return LEGACY_CHAT_COLORS[index]
end

-- Restored legacy ComputeChatColors entry point.
--
-- Accepts either:
--   * a player name string (returns the legacy/team name color), or
--   * a legacy messageData table (fills ExtraData.NameColor when it has not
--     already been supplied, then returns the resulting Color3).
--
-- The color algorithm is the original SolarCrane name hash, with team colors
-- taking priority for non-neutral players. Existing explicit NameColor values
-- are respected rather than overwritten.
local function ComputeChatColors(value, speakerUserId)
	local messageData = nil
	local playerName = nil

	if type(value) == "string" then
		playerName = value
	elseif type(value) == "table" then
		messageData = value
		playerName = messageData.FromSpeaker
		if type(playerName) ~= "string" or playerName == "" then
			return nil
		end

		local extraData = messageData.ExtraData
		if type(extraData) ~= "table" then
			extraData = {}
			messageData.ExtraData = extraData
		end
		if typeof(extraData.NameColor) == "Color3" then
			return extraData.NameColor
		end

		speakerUserId = tonumber(messageData.SpeakerUserId) or speakerUserId
	else
		return nil
	end

	local speaker = nil
	local userId = tonumber(speakerUserId)
	if userId and userId &gt; 0 then
		speaker = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(userId)
	end
	if not speaker and playerName then
		speaker = Players:FindFirstChild(playerName)
	end

	local nameColor
	if speaker and speaker.Neutral == false then
		nameColor = speaker.TeamColor.Color
	else
		nameColor = computeLegacyChatColor(playerName)
	end

	if messageData then
		local extraData = messageData.ExtraData
		if type(extraData) ~= "table" then
			extraData = {}
			messageData.ExtraData = extraData
		end
		extraData.NameColor = nameColor
	end

	return nameColor
end

-- Keep the existing internal call name while routing all uses through the
-- restored ComputeChatColors implementation.
local function applyLegacyChatNameColor(messageData)
	return ComputeChatColors(messageData)
end

-- Public compatibility entry points. Support both dot and colon calls so
-- old callers can use ChatMain.ComputeChatColors(name) or
-- ChatMain:ComputeChatColors(messageData).
local function callComputeChatColors(first, second, third)
	if first == moduleApiTable then
		return ComputeChatColors(second, third)
	end
	return ComputeChatColors(first, second)
end

moduleApiTable.ComputeChatColors = callComputeChatColors
moduleApiTable.ComputeChatColor = callComputeChatColors

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT BAR-ONLY MODE
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function chatBarOnlyMode()
	local classicOK, classicEnabled = pcall(function()
		return Players.ClassicChat
	end)
	if classicOK and type(classicEnabled) == "boolean" and classicEnabled == false then
		return true
	end

	local windowConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatWindowConfiguration")
	local inputConfig = TextChatService:FindFirstChild("ChatInputBarConfiguration")
	if windowConfig and inputConfig then
		local windowOK, windowEnabled = pcall(function()
			return windowConfig.Enabled
		end)
		local inputOK, inputEnabled = pcall(function()
			return inputConfig.Enabled
		end)
		if windowOK and inputOK
			and type(windowEnabled) == "boolean"
			and type(inputEnabled) == "boolean"
			and windowEnabled == false
			and inputEnabled == true then
			return true
		end
	end

	return false
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FADING AND MOUSE TRACKING
----------------------------------------------------------------

local backgroundIsFaded = false
local textIsFaded = false
local lastTextFadeTime = 0
local lastBackgroundFadeTime = 0

local fadedChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local mouseStateChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
local chatBarFocusChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")

local function resolveFadeDuration(requestedDuration)
	if requestedDuration == 0 then
		return 0
	end
	local duration = tonumber(requestedDuration)
	if duration == nil then
		duration = tonumber(ChatSettings.ChatDefaultFadeDuration)
	end
	if duration == nil then
		duration = 0.6
	end
	return math.max(duration, 0.65)
end

local function DoBackgroundFadeIn(setFadingTime)
	lastBackgroundFadeTime = os.clock()
	backgroundIsFaded = false
	fadedChanged:Fire()
	ChatWindow:FadeInBackground(resolveFadeDuration(setFadingTime))
end

local function DoBackgroundFadeOut(setFadingTime)
	lastBackgroundFadeTime = os.clock()
	backgroundIsFaded = true
	if not textIsFaded then
		lastTextFadeTime = lastBackgroundFadeTime
	end
	fadedChanged:Fire()
	ChatWindow:FadeOutBackground(resolveFadeDuration(setFadingTime))
end

local function DoTextFadeIn(setFadingTime)
	lastTextFadeTime = os.clock()
	textIsFaded = false
	fadedChanged:Fire()
	local duration = resolveFadeDuration(setFadingTime)
	ChatWindow:FadeInText(duration)
	if ChatBar then
		ChatBar:FadeInText(duration)
	end
end

local function DoTextFadeOut(setFadingTime)
	lastTextFadeTime = os.clock()
	textIsFaded = true
	fadedChanged:Fire()
	local duration = resolveFadeDuration(setFadingTime)
	ChatWindow:FadeOutText(duration)
	if ChatBar then
		ChatBar:FadeOutText(duration)
	end
end

local function DoFadeInFromNewInformation()
	DoTextFadeIn()
	if ChatSettings.ChatShouldFadeInFromNewInformation then
		DoBackgroundFadeIn()
	end
end

local function InstantFadeIn()
	DoBackgroundFadeIn(0)
	DoTextFadeIn(0)
end

local function InstantFadeOut()
	DoBackgroundFadeOut(0)
	DoTextFadeOut(0)
end

local mouseIsInWindow = nil

local function CheckIfPointIsInSquare(checkPos, topLeft, bottomRight)
	return topLeft.X &lt;= checkPos.X
		and checkPos.X &lt;= bottomRight.X
		and topLeft.Y &lt;= checkPos.Y
		and checkPos.Y &lt;= bottomRight.Y
end

local function UpdateFadingForMouseState(mouseState)
	mouseIsInWindow = mouseState
	mouseStateChanged:Fire()
	if ChatBar:IsFocused() then
		return
	end
	if mouseState then
		DoBackgroundFadeIn()
		DoTextFadeIn()
	else
		DoBackgroundFadeIn()
	end
end

local function UpdateMousePosition(mousePos)
	if not (
		moduleApiTable.Visible
			and moduleApiTable.IsCoreGuiEnabled
			and (moduleApiTable.TopbarEnabled or ChatSettings.ChatOnWithTopBarOff)
		) then
		return
	end
	if chatBarOnlyMode() or not ChatWindow.GuiObject then
		return
	end

	local windowPos = ChatWindow.GuiObject.AbsolutePosition
	local windowSize = ChatWindow.GuiObject.AbsoluteSize
	local newMouseState = CheckIfPointIsInSquare(mousePos, windowPos, windowPos + windowSize)
	if newMouseState ~= mouseIsInWindow then
		UpdateFadingForMouseState(newMouseState)
	end
end

UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(inputObject)
	if inputObject.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
		UpdateMousePosition(Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.X, inputObject.Position.Y))
	end
end)

UserInputService.TouchTap:Connect(function(tapPositions)
	if tapPositions and tapPositions[1] then
		UpdateMousePosition(tapPositions[1])
	end
end)

UserInputService.TouchMoved:Connect(function(inputObject)
	UpdateMousePosition(Vector2.new(inputObject.Position.X, inputObject.Position.Y))
end)

UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseBehavior"):Connect(function()
	if UserInputService.MouseBehavior ~= Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter
		or not ChatWindow.GuiObject then
		return
	end

	local windowPos = ChatWindow.GuiObject.AbsolutePosition
	local windowSize = ChatWindow.GuiObject.AbsoluteSize
	local screenSize = GuiParent.AbsoluteSize
	if CheckIfPointIsInSquare(screenSize / 2, windowPos, windowPos + windowSize) then
		UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while GuiParent.Parent do
		RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

		while mouseIsInWindow or ChatBar:IsFocused() do
			if mouseIsInWindow then
				mouseStateChanged.Event:Wait()
			end
			if ChatBar:IsFocused() then
				chatBarFocusChanged.Event:Wait()
			end
		end

		local now = os.clock()
		local backgroundFadeDelay = math.max(tonumber(ChatSettings.ChatWindowBackgroundFadeOutTime) or 30, 12)
		local textFadeDelay = math.max(tonumber(ChatSettings.ChatWindowTextFadeOutTime) or 30, 20)

		if not backgroundIsFaded then
			if now - lastBackgroundFadeTime &gt; backgroundFadeDelay then
				DoBackgroundFadeOut()
			end
		elseif not textIsFaded then
			if now - lastTextFadeTime &gt; textFadeDelay then
				DoTextFadeOut()
			end
		else
			fadedChanged.Event:Wait()
		end
	end
end)

UpdateFadingForMouseState(true)
UpdateFadingForMouseState(false)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LEGACY SIGNAL IMPLEMENTATION
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function Signal()
	local bindable = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	local args, argCount = nil, 0
	local signal = {}

	function signal:fire(...)
		args = table.pack(...)
		argCount = args.n
		bindable:Fire()
	end

	function signal:connect(callback)
		assert(type(callback) == "function", "connect expects a function")
		return bindable.Event:Connect(function()
			if args then
				callback(table.unpack(args, 1, argCount))
			end
		end)
	end

	function signal:wait()
		bindable.Event:Wait()
		if not args then
			return
		end
		return table.unpack(args, 1, argCount)
	end

	return signal
end

moduleApiTable.TopbarEnabled = true
moduleApiTable.MessageCount = 0
moduleApiTable.Visible = true
moduleApiTable.IsCoreGuiEnabled = true

moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged = Signal()
moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged = Signal()
moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged = Signal()
moduleApiTable.MessagePosted = Signal()
moduleApiTable.CoreGuiEnabled = Signal()
moduleApiTable.ChatMakeSystemMessageEvent = Signal()
moduleApiTable.ChatWindowPositionEvent = Signal()
moduleApiTable.ChatWindowSizeEvent = Signal()
moduleApiTable.ChatBarDisabledEvent = Signal()

	return GuiParent
end

	return ChatWindow.GuiObject
end

	return ChatBar:GetTextBox()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT VISIBILITY: UPDATE REAL UI BEFORE FIRING THE SIGNAL
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function isYekuChatUiAllowed()
	local root = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
	if root then
		if root:GetAttribute("YekuCustomChatEnabled") == false
			or root:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled") == false
			or root:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled") == false then
			return false
		end
	end
	local chatOK, chatEnabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)
	local allAllowed = not root or root:GetAttribute("YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled") ~= false
	return (not chatOK or chatEnabled == true) and allAllowed
end

local function actualChatWindowVisible()
	local window = ChatWindow.GuiObject
	return GuiParent.Parent ~= nil
		and GuiParent.Enabled
		and window ~= nil
		and window.Visible == true
end

local function SetVisibility(visible)
	visible = visible == true
	if visible and not isYekuChatUiAllowed() then
		return false
	end
	local textBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()

	if visible then
		GuiParent.Enabled = true
	else
		if textBox then
			pcall(function()
				textBox:ReleaseFocus(false)
			end)
		end
		ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
		ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = false
	end

	ChatWindow:SetVisible(visible)
	moduleApiTable.Visible = visible

	if moduleApiTable.IsCoreGuiEnabled then
		if visible then
			InstantFadeIn()
		else
			InstantFadeOut()
		end
	end

	if not visible then
		GuiParent.Enabled = false
	end

	moduleApiTable.VisibilityStateChanged:fire(actualChatWindowVisible())
	return actualChatWindowVisible() == visible
end

local rootForChatGate = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("NewRobloxGui")
if rootForChatGate then
	for _, attributeName in ipairs({"YekuCustomChatEnabled", "YekuCoreGuiChatEnabled", "YekuCoreGuiAllEnabled"}) do
		rootForChatGate:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attributeName):Connect(function()
			if not isYekuChatUiAllowed() then
				SetVisibility(false)
			end
		end)
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:ToggleVisibility()
	return SetVisibility(not actualChatWindowVisible())
end

function moduleApiTable:SetVisible(visible)
	return SetVisibility(visible)
end

function moduleApiTable:GetVisibility()
	return actualChatWindowVisible()
end

function moduleApiTable:GetMessageCount()
	return self.MessageCount
end

function moduleApiTable:TopbarEnabledChanged(enabled)
	self.TopbarEnabled = enabled
	local success, chatEnabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)
	if success then
		self.CoreGuiEnabled:fire(chatEnabled)
	end
end

function moduleApiTable:IsFocused(_useWasFocused)
	return ChatBar:IsFocused()
end

function moduleApiTable:fChatWindowPosition()
	return ChatWindow.GuiObject.Position
end

function moduleApiTable:fChatWindowSize()
	return ChatWindow.GuiObject.Size
end

function moduleApiTable:fChatBarDisabled()
	return not ChatBar:GetEnabled()
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT FOCUS: OPEN FIRST, THEN CAPTURE THE REAL TEXTBOX
----------------------------------------------------------------

DoChatBarFocus = function()
	if not isYekuChatUiAllowed() or not ChatWindow:GetCoreGuiEnabled() then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Focus refused: ChatWindow CoreGui is disabled.")
		return false
	end
	if not ChatBar:GetEnabled() then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Focus refused: ChatBar is disabled.")
		return false
	end

	local textBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	if not textBox or not textBox:IsA("TextBox") then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] The actual ChatBar TextBox is missing.")
		return false
	end
	if not textBox:IsDescendantOf(GuiParent) then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] The ChatBar TextBox is outside the Chat ScreenGui.")
		return false
	end

	local wasVisible = actualChatWindowVisible()

	-- Like SolarCrane Chat.lua, reveal the widget only if it was closed.
	-- Re-setting true on an already-visible window needlessly restarts fades.
	if not wasVisible then
		if not moduleApiTable:SetVisible(true) then
			warn(
				"[Yeku Legacy Chat] SetVisible(true) failed. RootEnabled="
					.. tostring(GuiParent.Enabled)
					.. ", WindowVisible="
					.. tostring(ChatWindow.GuiObject and ChatWindow.GuiObject.Visible)
					.. ", CoreGuiEnabled="
					.. tostring(ChatWindow:GetCoreGuiEnabled())
			)
			return false
		end

		RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
	end

	if not actualChatWindowVisible() then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Chat was hidden again before CaptureFocus.")
		return false
	end

	if ChatBar.TextLabel then
		ChatBar.TextLabel.Visible = false
		ChatBar.TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
	end

	textBox.ZIndex = 4
	textBox.Active = true
	textBox.TextEditable = true
	textBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	textBox.MultiLine = false
	textBox.TextWrapped = false

	ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = true
	ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = false
	ChatBar:RevealTextImmediately()
	ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()

	if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox then
		ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = true
		ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
		ChatBar:RevealTextImmediately()
		DoTextFadeIn(0)
		ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()
	else
		local focusOK, focusResult = pcall(function()
			return ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
		end)
		if not focusOK or focusResult == false then
			ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
			ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()
			warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] CaptureFocus failed: " .. tostring(focusResult))
			return false
		end
	end

	for attempt = 1, 2 do
		for _ = 1, 5 do
			RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()

			if not GuiParent.Parent or not textBox:IsDescendantOf(GuiParent) then
				return false
			end

			if not GuiParent.Enabled then
				moduleApiTable:SetVisible(true)
			end
			if not ChatWindow.GuiObject.Visible then
				ChatWindow:SetVisible(true)
			end

			if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox then
				ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = true
				ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false

				-- Reset both the current value and the animation target. This
				-- covers the already-focused case where Roblox does not fire a
				-- second Focused event and a previous fade target was still 1.
				ChatBar:RevealTextImmediately()
				DoTextFadeIn(0)
				ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()
				pcall(function() ChatBar:CalculateSize() end)
				return true
			end
		end

		if attempt == 1 then
			local currentTextBox = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
			if currentTextBox and currentTextBox ~= textBox
				and currentTextBox.Name == "ChatBar" then
				pcall(function()
					currentTextBox:ReleaseFocus(false)
				end)
			end

			ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = true
			ChatBar:RevealTextImmediately()
			ChatBar:CaptureFocus()
		end
	end

	ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
	ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()
	warn(
		"[Yeku Legacy Chat] Focus verification failed. Visible="
			.. tostring(actualChatWindowVisible())
			.. ", focusedTarget="
			.. tostring(UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox)
	)
	return false
end

function moduleApiTable:FocusChatBar()
	-- Compatibility-only observer. ChatMain must not launch a second focus
	-- routine in response to the legacy CoreGuiChatConnections.FocusChatBar
	-- event: Topbar owns the '/' open/focus path, and direct user clicks on the
	-- actual ChatBar TextBox are handled by Roblox's TextBox focus events.
	-- Return true only when that real TextBox already owns keyboard focus.
	local textBox = ChatBar and ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	if not textBox or not textBox:IsA("TextBox") then
		return false
	end

	local focused = false
	pcall(function()
		focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox
			or textBox:IsFocused()
	end)
	return focused == true
end

function moduleApiTable:SpecialKeyPressed(key, _modifiers)
	-- Compatibility observer only. Visibility/focus changes are owned by Topbar;
	-- this method must never start a second open/focus flow.
	if key ~= Enum.SpecialKey.ChatHotkey or not canChat then
		return false
	end

	local textBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	if actualChatWindowVisible() and textBox
		and UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox then
		ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = true
		ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
		ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()
		pcall(function() ChatBar:CalculateSize() end)
	end

	return false
end

chatBarFocusChanged.Event:Connect(function(focused)
	moduleApiTable.ChatBarFocusChanged:fire(focused)
end)

-- PASSIVE COREGUI STATE

moduleApiTable.CoreGuiEnabled:connect(function(enabled)
	moduleApiTable.IsCoreGuiEnabled = enabled
	local effectiveEnabled = enabled
		and (moduleApiTable.TopbarEnabled or ChatSettings.ChatOnWithTopBarOff)

	ChatWindow:SetCoreGuiEnabled(effectiveEnabled)

	if not effectiveEnabled then
		ChatBar:ReleaseFocus()
		InstantFadeOut()
		GuiParent.Enabled = false
	else
		GuiParent.Enabled = true
		InstantFadeIn()
		ChatWindow:UpdateFrameVisibility()
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	local success, enabled = pcall(function()
		return StarterGui:GetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.Chat)
	end)
	if success then
		moduleApiTable.CoreGuiEnabled:fire(enabled)
	end
end)

-- SETCORE: SYSTEM MESSAGE

moduleApiTable.ChatMakeSystemMessageEvent:connect(function(valueTable)
	if not valueTable or type(valueTable.Text) ~= "string" then
		return
	end

	local channelName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
	local channelObj = ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName)
	if not channelObj then
		return
	end

	local messageObject = {
		ID = -1,
		FromSpeaker = nil,
		SpeakerUserId = 0,
		OriginalChannel = channelName,
		IsFiltered = true,
		MessageLength = #valueTable.Text,
		Message = trimTrailingSpaces(valueTable.Text),
		MessageType = ChatConstants.MessageTypeSetCore or getMessageTypeSystem(),
		Time = os.time(),
		ExtraData = valueTable,
	}

	channelObj:AddMessageToChannel(messageObject)
	ChannelsBar:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(channelName)
	moduleApiTable.MessageCount += 1
	moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged:fire(moduleApiTable.MessageCount)
end)

moduleApiTable.ChatBarDisabledEvent:connect(function(disabled)
	ChatBar:SetEnabled(not disabled)
	if disabled then
		ChatBar:ReleaseFocus()
	end
end)

moduleApiTable.ChatWindowSizeEvent:connect(function(size)
	ChatWindow.GuiObject.Size = size
end)

moduleApiTable.ChatWindowPositionEvent:connect(function(position)
	ChatWindow.GuiObject.Position = position
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHANNEL HELPERS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function DoSwitchCurrentChannel(targetChannel)
	if ChatWindow:GetChannel(targetChannel) then
		ChatWindow:SwitchCurrentChannel(targetChannel)
	end
end

local function SendSystemMessageToSelf(message)
	local currentChannel = ChatWindow:GetCurrentChannel()
	if not currentChannel then
		return
	end

	local messageData = {
		ID = -1,
		FromSpeaker = nil,
		SpeakerUserId = 0,
		OriginalChannel = currentChannel.Name,
		IsFiltered = true,
		MessageLength = #message,
		Message = trimTrailingSpaces(message),
		MessageType = getMessageTypeSystem(),
		Time = os.time(),
		ExtraData = nil,
	}
	currentChannel:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHAT BAR MESSAGE SENDING
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function chatBarFocused()
	-- Focus does not control visibility here. Roblox's native slash route may
	-- focus this TextBox just before Topbar reveals the ScreenGui; preserve that
	-- exact focus instead of releasing it or starting a second focus sequence.
	-- Topbar alone opens the window, through the same shared route as the button.
	ChatBar.HasTextBoxFocus = true
	ChatBar.FocusRequestPending = false
	ChatBar:UpdatePlaceholder()

	-- Don't restart fades while the containing window is hidden. ChatBar.Update()
	-- reconciles the existing shared typed-text/placeholder alpha as soon as
	-- Topbar makes the actual window visible.
	if actualChatWindowVisible() then
		DoBackgroundFadeIn(0)
		DoTextFadeIn(0)
	end

	chatBarFocusChanged:Fire(true)
end

local function chatBarFocusLost(enterPressed, _inputObject)
	DoBackgroundFadeIn()
	chatBarFocusChanged:Fire(false)
	if not enterPressed then
		return
	end

	local message = ChatBar:GetTextBox().Text
	if ChatBar:IsInCustomState() then
		local customMessage = ChatBar:GetCustomMessage()
		if customMessage then
			message = customMessage
		end
		local messageSunk = ChatBar:CustomStateProcessCompletedMessage(message)
		ChatBar:ResetCustomState()
		if messageSunk then
			return
		end
	end

	message = string.sub(message, 1, ChatSettings.MaximumMessageLength)
	ChatBar:GetTextBox().Text = ""
	if message == "" then
		return
	end

	moduleApiTable.MessagePosted:fire(message)
	if CommandProcessor:ProcessCompletedChatMessage(message, ChatWindow) then
		return
	end

	if ChatSettings.DisallowedWhiteSpace then
		for _, disallowed in ipairs(ChatSettings.DisallowedWhiteSpace) do
			if disallowed == "\t" then
				message = string.gsub(message, disallowed, " ")
			else
				message = string.gsub(message, disallowed, "")
			end
		end
	end

	message = string.gsub(message, "\n", "")
	message = string.gsub(message, "[ ]+", " ")
	local targetChannel = ChatWindow:GetTargetMessageChannel()
	MessageSender:SendMessage(message, targetChannel)
end

local ChatBarConnections = {}

local function setupChatBarConnections()
	for _, connection in ipairs(ChatBarConnections) do
		if connection then
			connection:Disconnect()
		end
	end
	table.clear(ChatBarConnections)

	local liveTextBox = ChatBar:GetTextBox()
	if not liveTextBox then
		return
	end

	table.insert(ChatBarConnections, liveTextBox.FocusLost:Connect(chatBarFocusLost))
	table.insert(ChatBarConnections, liveTextBox.Focused:Connect(chatBarFocused))


end

setupChatBarConnections()
ChatBar.GuiObjectsChanged:Connect(setupChatBarConnections)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FOCUS OWNERSHIP
----------------------------------------------------------------

-- Focus repair is event-driven. Do not poll IsFocused every RenderStepped: the
-- TextBox can report stale focus for a frame after ReleaseFocus, which used to
-- reopen a window that was intentionally closed and fight its fade animation.

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INCOMING FILTERED MESSAGES
----------------------------------------------------------------

-- DefaultChatSystemChatEvents can also carry game/NPC speaker names on
-- modified clients. Only player-originated Message/Whisper rows belong in
-- the 2015/2016 conversation history; NPC speech is bubble-only.
local function isActualPlayerSpeaker(messageData)
	local messageType = messageData and messageData.MessageType
	if messageType ~= "Message" and messageType ~= "Whisper" then
		return true -- preserve system, status, and command messages
	end
	local speaker = messageData and messageData.FromSpeaker
	if type(speaker) ~= "string" then return false end
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player.Name == speaker then return true end
	end
	return false
end

EventFolder.OnMessageDoneFiltering.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(messageData)
	if not messageData or not isActualPlayerSpeaker(messageData) then
		return
	end
	if not ChatSettings.ShowUserOwnFilteredMessage
		and messageData.FromSpeaker == LocalPlayer.Name then
		return
	end

	local channelName = messageData.OriginalChannel
	local channelObj = ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName)
	if channelObj then
		channelObj:UpdateMessageFiltered(messageData)
	end

	if getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
		and ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
		and channelName ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		local generalChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
		if generalChannel then
			generalChannel:UpdateMessageFiltered(messageData)
		end
	end
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INCOMING CHAT MESSAGES
----------------------------------------------------------------

EventFolder.OnNewMessage.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(messageData, channelName)
	if not messageData or not isActualPlayerSpeaker(messageData) then
		return
	end

	channelName = channelName
		or messageData.OriginalChannel
		or ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
		or "All"

	if (usingYekuBridge or usingInternalTextChatBridge)
		and not ChatSettings.ShowUserOwnFilteredMessage
		and messageData.FromSpeaker == LocalPlayer.Name then
		return
	end

	local channelObj = ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName)
	if not channelObj then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] Received a message for missing channel: " .. tostring(channelName))
		return
	end

	applyLegacyChatNameColor(messageData)
	channelObj:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)

	if messageData.FromSpeaker ~= LocalPlayer.Name then
		ChannelsBar:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(channelName)
	end

	if getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
		and ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
		and channelName ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		local generalChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
		if generalChannel then
			generalChannel:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
		end
	end

	moduleApiTable.MessageCount += 1
	moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged:fire(moduleApiTable.MessageCount)
	DoFadeInFromNewInformation()
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INCOMING SYSTEM MESSAGES
----------------------------------------------------------------

EventFolder.OnNewSystemMessage.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(messageData, channelName)
	if not messageData then
		return
	end
	channelName = channelName or "System"

	local channelObj = ChatWindow:GetChannel(channelName)
	if not channelObj then
		warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] System message references missing channel: " .. tostring(channelName))
		return
	end

	channelObj:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
	ChannelsBar:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(channelName)
	moduleApiTable.MessageCount += 1
	moduleApiTable.MessagesChanged:fire(moduleApiTable.MessageCount)
	DoFadeInFromNewInformation()

	if getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
		and ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
		and channelName ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		local generalChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
		if generalChannel then
			generalChannel:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
		end
	end
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHANNEL JOIN / LEAVE
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function HandleChannelJoined(
	channel,
	welcomeMessage,
	messageLog,
	channelNameColor,
	addHistoryToGeneralChannel,
	addWelcomeMessageToGeneralChannel
)
	if not channel or channel == "" then
		return
	end

	local generalChannelName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
	local displayChannelName = channel
	if channel == "RBXSystem" then
		displayChannelName = "System"
	elseif channel == "RBXGeneral" then
		displayChannelName = generalChannelName
	end

	if (usingYekuBridge or usingInternalTextChatBridge)
		and string.lower(tostring(displayChannelName)) == string.lower(generalChannelName)
		and (type(welcomeMessage) ~= "string" or welcomeMessage == "") then
		welcomeMessage = DEFAULT_GENERAL_WELCOME
	end

	if ChatWindow:GetChannel(channel) then
		ChatWindow:RemoveChannel(channel)
	end
	if channel == ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		DidFirstChannelsLoads = true
	end
	if channelNameColor then
		ChatBar:SetChannelNameColor(channel, channelNameColor)
	end

	local channelObj = ChatWindow:AddChannel(channel)
	if not channelObj then
		return
	end
	if channel == ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		DoSwitchCurrentChannel(channel)
	end

	if type(messageLog) == "table" then
		local historyLength = ChatSettings.MessageHistoryLengthPerChannel or #messageLog
		-- Historical clients exposed UserSettings().GameSettings.ChatHistory.
		-- Zero means do not replay messages from before this channel was joined.
		local historySetting
		pcall(function()
			historySetting = UserSettings().GameSettings.ChatHistory
		end)
		if type(historySetting) == "number" then
			historyLength = math.max(0, math.floor(historySetting))
		end
		if historyLength &gt; 0 then
			local startIndex = math.max(1, #messageLog - historyLength + 1)
			for index = startIndex, #messageLog do
				applyLegacyChatNameColor(messageLog[index])
				channelObj:AddMessageToChannel(messageLog[index])
			end

			if getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
				and addHistoryToGeneralChannel
				and ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
				and channel ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
				local generalChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
				if generalChannel then
					generalChannel:AddMessagesToChannelByTimeStamp(messageLog, startIndex)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if type(welcomeMessage) == "string" and welcomeMessage ~= "" then
		local welcomeMessageObject = {
			ID = -1,
			FromSpeaker = nil,
			SpeakerUserId = 0,
			OriginalChannel = channel,
			IsFiltered = true,
			MessageLength = #welcomeMessage,
			Message = trimTrailingSpaces(welcomeMessage),
			MessageType = ChatConstants.MessageTypeWelcome or getMessageTypeSystem(),
			Time = os.time(),
			ExtraData = nil,
		}
		channelObj:AddMessageToChannel(welcomeMessageObject)

		if getEchoMessagesInGeneral()
			and addWelcomeMessageToGeneralChannel
			and not ChatSettings.ShowChannelsBar
			and channel ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
			local generalChannel = ChatWindow:GetChannel(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
			if generalChannel then
				generalChannel:AddMessageToChannel(welcomeMessageObject)
			end
		end
	end

	DoFadeInFromNewInformation()
end

EventFolder.OnChannelJoined.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(channel, welcomeMessage, messageLog, channelNameColor)
	HandleChannelJoined(channel, welcomeMessage, messageLog, channelNameColor, false, true)
end)

EventFolder.OnChannelLeft.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(channel)
	if ChatWindow:GetChannel(channel) then
		ChatWindow:RemoveChannel(channel)
		DoFadeInFromNewInformation()
	end
end)

EventFolder.OnMuted.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_channel)
	-- Keep input available for chat commands, matching legacy behavior.
end)

EventFolder.OnUnmuted.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_channel)
end)

EventFolder.OnMainChannelSet.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(channel)
	DoSwitchCurrentChannel(channel)
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- OPTIONAL CHANNEL COLORS
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function connectChannelNameColorUpdated()
	if usingLegacyRemotes then
		local colorEvent = DefaultChatSystemChatEvents
			and DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("ChannelNameColorUpdated")
		if colorEvent and colorEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			colorEvent.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(channelName, channelNameColor)
				ChatBar:SetChannelNameColor(channelName, channelNameColor)
			end)
		end
	elseif usingYekuBridge then
		local colorEvent = BridgeFolder and BridgeFolder:FindFirstChild("ChannelNameColorUpdated")
		if colorEvent and colorEvent:IsA("BindableEvent") then
			colorEvent.Event:Connect(function(channelName, channelNameColor)
				ChatBar:SetChannelNameColor(channelName, channelNameColor)
			end)
		end
	end
end

connectChannelNameColorUpdated()

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- PLAYER BLOCK / MUTE COMPATIBILITY
----------------------------------------------------------------

local function safeLegacyPlayerRequest(remoteName, player)
	if not usingLegacyRemotes or not DefaultChatSystemChatEvents or not player then
		return false
	end
	local request = DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild(remoteName)
	if not request or not request:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
		return false
	end
	local success, result = pcall(function()
		return request:InvokeServer(player.Name)
	end)
	return success and result == true
end

local PlayerBlockedEvent
local PlayerMutedEvent
local PlayerUnBlockedEvent
local PlayerUnMutedEvent

pcall(function()
	PlayerBlockedEvent = StarterGui:GetCore("PlayerBlockedEvent")
	PlayerMutedEvent = StarterGui:GetCore("PlayerMutedEvent")
	PlayerUnBlockedEvent = StarterGui:GetCore("PlayerUnblockedEvent")
	PlayerUnMutedEvent = StarterGui:GetCore("PlayerUnmutedEvent")
end)

if PlayerBlockedEvent and PlayerBlockedEvent.Event then
	PlayerBlockedEvent.Event:Connect(function(player)
		if safeLegacyPlayerRequest("MutePlayerRequest", player) then
			SendSystemMessageToSelf(string.format("Speaker '%s' has been blocked.", player.Name))
		end
	end)
end
if PlayerMutedEvent and PlayerMutedEvent.Event then
	PlayerMutedEvent.Event:Connect(function(player)
		if safeLegacyPlayerRequest("MutePlayerRequest", player) then
			SendSystemMessageToSelf(string.format("Speaker '%s' has been muted.", player.Name))
		end
	end)
end
if PlayerUnBlockedEvent and PlayerUnBlockedEvent.Event then
	PlayerUnBlockedEvent.Event:Connect(function(player)
		if safeLegacyPlayerRequest("UnMutePlayerRequest", player) then
			SendSystemMessageToSelf(string.format("Speaker '%s' has been unblocked.", player.Name))
		end
	end)
end
if PlayerUnMutedEvent and PlayerUnMutedEvent.Event then
	PlayerUnMutedEvent.Event:Connect(function(player)
		if safeLegacyPlayerRequest("UnMutePlayerRequest", player) then
			SendSystemMessageToSelf(string.format("Speaker '%s' has been unmuted.", player.Name))
		end
	end)
end

	if LocalPlayer.UserId &lt;= 0 or not usingLegacyRemotes then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		local blockedUserIds = StarterGui:GetCore("GetBlockedUserIds")
		if type(blockedUserIds) ~= "table" or #blockedUserIds == 0 then
			return
		end
		local request = DefaultChatSystemChatEvents
			and DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SetBlockedUserIdsRequest")
		if request and request:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
			request:FireServer(blockedUserIds)
		end
	end)
end)

		canChat = RunService:IsStudio() or canLocalUserChat
	end
end)

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INTERNAL TEXTCHATSERVICE BRIDGE
----------------------------------------------------------------

if usingInternalTextChatBridge then
	local nextMessageId = 0
	local knownChannels = {}

	local function ensureTextChannel(textChannel)
		local displayName = getDisplayChannelName(textChannel)
		local key = string.lower(displayName)
		if not ChatWindow:GetChannel(displayName) and not knownChannels[key] then
			knownChannels[key] = true
			EventFolder.OnChannelJoined:Fire(displayName, "", {}, nil)
		end
		return displayName
	end

	local function handleTextChatMessage(textChatMessage)
		if not textChatMessage then
			return
		end
		if textChatMessage.Status
			and textChatMessage.Status ~= Enum.TextChatMessageStatus.Success then
			return
		end

		local messageText = textChatMessage.Text
		if typeof(messageText) ~= "string" or messageText == "" then
			return
		end

		local textChannel = textChatMessage.TextChannel
		local channelName = ensureTextChannel(textChannel)
		local textSource = textChatMessage.TextSource
		if not textSource then
			local messageType
			pcall(function() messageType = textChatMessage.MessageType end)
			if not Enum.TextChatMessageType or messageType ~= Enum.TextChatMessageType.System then
				return -- NPC/dialogue text is reserved for the legacy bubble renderer.
			end
		end
		local speakerUserId = textSource and textSource.UserId or 0
		local speakerName

		if speakerUserId &gt; 0 then
			local speaker = Players:GetPlayerByUserId(speakerUserId)
			if speaker then
				speakerName = speaker.Name
			end
		end

		nextMessageId += 1
		local messageData = {
			ID = nextMessageId,
			FromSpeaker = speakerName,
			SpeakerUserId = speakerUserId,
			OriginalChannel = channelName,
			IsFiltered = true,
			MessageLength = #messageText,
			Message = messageText,
			MessageType = textSource and getMessageTypeDefault() or getMessageTypeSystem(),
			Time = os.time(),
			ExtraData = nil,
		}

		if textSource then
			EventFolder.OnNewMessage:Fire(messageData, channelName)
		else
			EventFolder.OnNewSystemMessage:Fire(messageData, channelName)
		end
	end

	TextChatService.MessageReceived:Connect(handleTextChatMessage)

	local textChannels = getTextChannelsFolder()
	if textChannels then
		for _, textChannel in ipairs(textChannels:GetChildren()) do
			if textChannel:IsA("TextChannel") then
				local displayName = getDisplayChannelName(textChannel)
				knownChannels[string.lower(displayName)] = true
			end
		end

		textChannels.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
			if not child:IsA("TextChannel") then
				return
			end
			local displayName = getDisplayChannelName(child)
			local key = string.lower(displayName)
			if not knownChannels[key] and not ChatWindow:GetChannel(displayName) then
				knownChannels[key] = true
				EventFolder.OnChannelJoined:Fire(displayName, "", {}, nil)
			end
		end)

		textChannels.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
			if not child:IsA("TextChannel") then
				return
			end
			local displayName = getDisplayChannelName(child)
			local key = string.lower(displayName)
			if displayName ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName
				and ChatWindow:GetChannel(displayName) then
				knownChannels[key] = nil
				EventFolder.OnChannelLeft:Fire(displayName)
			end
		end)
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INITIAL CHANNEL DATA
----------------------------------------------------------------

local initDataSuccess, initData = pcall(function()
	return EventFolder.GetInitDataRequest:InvokeServer()
end)

local generalChannelName = ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName or "All"
if initDataSuccess and type(initData) == "table" and type(initData.Channels) == "table" then
	for _, channelData in pairs(initData.Channels) do
		if type(channelData) == "table" and channelData[1] == generalChannelName then
			HandleChannelJoined(channelData[1], channelData[2], channelData[3], channelData[4], true, false)
		end
	end
	for _, channelData in pairs(initData.Channels) do
		if type(channelData) == "table" and channelData[1] ~= generalChannelName then
			HandleChannelJoined(channelData[1], channelData[2], channelData[3], channelData[4], true, false)
		end
	end
else
	warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] GetInitDataRequest failed or returned invalid data: " .. tostring(initData))
end

if not ChatWindow:GetChannel(generalChannelName) then
	HandleChannelJoined(generalChannelName, "", {}, nil, false, false)
end
if ChatWindow:GetChannel(generalChannelName) then
	DoSwitchCurrentChannel(generalChannelName)
	DidFirstChannelsLoads = true
end

-- ANIMATION UPDATE LOOP
----------------------------------------------------------------

task.spawn(function()
	local animationFps = ChatSettings.ChatAnimationFPS or 20
	local updateWaitTime = 1 / math.max(1, animationFps)
	local lastTick = os.clock()

	while GuiParent.Parent do
		task.wait(updateWaitTime)
		local currentTick = os.clock()
		local tickDelta = currentTick - lastTick
		local dtScale = tickDelta / (1 / 60)

		if dtScale ~= 0 then
			local success, err = pcall(function()
				ChatWindow:Update(dtScale)
			end)
			if not success then
				warn("[Yeku Legacy Chat] ChatWindow update failed: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end
		lastTick = currentTick
	end
end)

print(
	"[Yeku Legacy Chat] ChatMain started successfully. GUI: "
		.. GuiParent:GetFullName()
		.. "; general channel: "
		.. generalChannelName
)
print("[Yeku Legacy ChatMain] loaded revision: YekuChatMain_TopbarOwnsSlashFocus_v37_2026-10-21")

                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: ChatChannel.lua

local module = {}

local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local ClientChatFolder = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

local ClientChatModules =
	ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
local ChatSettings = require(ClientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

function methods:Destroy()
	self.Destroyed = true
end

function methods:SetActive(active)
	if active == self.Active then
		return
	end

	if not active then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:Clear()
	else
		self.MessageLogDisplay:SetCurrentChannelName(self.Name)

		for _, message in ipairs(self.MessageLog) do
			self.MessageLogDisplay:AddMessage(message)
		end
	end

	self.Active = active
end

function methods:UpdateMessageFiltered(messageData)
	for _, messageObj in ipairs(self.MessageLog) do
		if messageObj.ID == messageData.ID then
			messageObj.Message = messageData.Message
			messageObj.IsFiltered = true

			if self.Active then
				self.MessageLogDisplay:UpdateMessageFiltered(messageObj)
			end

			return
		end
	end

	self:AddMessageToChannelByTimeStamp(messageData)
end

function methods:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
	table.insert(self.MessageLog, messageData)

	if self.Active then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:AddMessage(messageData)
	end

	if #self.MessageLog &gt; ChatSettings.MessageHistoryLengthPerChannel then
		self:RemoveLastMessageFromChannel()
	end
end

function methods:InternalAddMessageAtTimeStamp(messageData)
	for i, currentMessage in ipairs(self.MessageLog) do
		if messageData.Time &lt; currentMessage.Time then
			table.insert(self.MessageLog, i, messageData)
			return
		end
	end

	table.insert(self.MessageLog, messageData)
end

function methods:AddMessagesToChannelByTimeStamp(messageLog, startIndex)
	for i = startIndex, #messageLog do
		self:InternalAddMessageAtTimeStamp(messageLog[i])
	end

	while #self.MessageLog &gt; ChatSettings.MessageHistoryLengthPerChannel do
		table.remove(self.MessageLog, 1)
	end

	if self.Active then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:Clear()

		for _, messageData in ipairs(self.MessageLog) do
			self.MessageLogDisplay:AddMessage(messageData)
		end
	end
end

function methods:AddMessageToChannelByTimeStamp(messageData)
	if #self.MessageLog == 0 then
		self:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
		return
	end

	if self.MessageLog[1].Time &gt; messageData.Time then
		return
	end

	if messageData.Time &gt;= self.MessageLog[#self.MessageLog].Time then
		self:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
		return
	end

	for i, currentMessage in ipairs(self.MessageLog) do
		if messageData.Time &lt; currentMessage.Time then
			table.insert(self.MessageLog, i, messageData)

			if #self.MessageLog &gt; ChatSettings.MessageHistoryLengthPerChannel then
				self:RemoveLastMessageFromChannel()
			end

			if self.Active then
				self.MessageLogDisplay:AddMessageAtIndex(messageData, i)
			end

			return
		end
	end

	self:AddMessageToChannel(messageData)
end

function methods:RemoveLastMessageFromChannel()
	if #self.MessageLog == 0 then
		return
	end

	table.remove(self.MessageLog, 1)

	if self.Active then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:RemoveLastMessage()
	end
end

function methods:ClearMessageLog()
	self.MessageLog = {}

	if self.Active then
		self.MessageLogDisplay:Clear()
	end
end

function methods:RegisterChannelTab(tab)
	self.ChannelTab = tab
end

function module.new(channelName, messageLogDisplay)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.Destroyed = false
	obj.Active = false
	obj.MessageLog = {}
	obj.MessageLogDisplay = messageLogDisplay
	obj.ChannelTab = nil
	obj.Name = channelName

	return obj
end

		0,
		backgroundImagePixelOffset
