----------------------------------------------------------------
-- DISABLED CHAT
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:DoLockChatBar()
	if self.TextLabel then
		if LocalPlayer.UserId &gt; 0 then
			self.TextLabel.Text = ChatLocalization:Get(
				"GameChat_ChatMessageValidator_SettingsError",
				"To chat in game, turn on chat in your Privacy Settings."
			)
		else
			self.TextLabel.Text = ChatLocalization:Get(
				"GameChat_SwallowGuestChat_Message",
				"Sign up to chat in game."
			)
		end

		self.TextLabel.Visible = true
		self:CalculateSize()
	end

	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.Active = false

		self.TextBox.Focused:Connect(function()
			self.TextBox:ReleaseFocus()
		end)
	end
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- INPUT EVENTS
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:SetUpTextBoxEvents(TextBox, TextLabel, MessageModeTextButton)
	for name, connection in pairs(self.TextBoxConnections) do
		if connection then
			pcall(function()
				connection:Disconnect()
			end)
		end

		self.TextBoxConnections[name] = nil
	end

	self.TextBoxConnections.UserInputBegan =
		UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inputObj, _gameProcessed)
			if inputObj.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace
				and self:IsFocused()
				and TextBox.Text == "" then
				self:SetChannelTarget(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
			end
		end)

	self.TextBoxConnections.TextBoxChanged = TextBox.Changed:Connect(function(prop)
		if prop == "AbsoluteSize" then
			self:CalculateSize()
			return
		end

		if prop ~= "Text" then
			return
		end

		-- Do not override the legacy text transparency when draft text changes.
		-- Typed text and the idle prompt are rendered with the same ChatBar style;
		-- AnimGuiObjects applies the same current fade value to both.
		local ownsInputFocus = false
		pcall(function()
			ownsInputFocus = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == TextBox
				or TextBox:IsFocused()
		end)
		if ownsInputFocus then
			self.HasTextBoxFocus = true
			self.FocusRequestPending = false

			-- Actual typing is active input. Reveal it after an idle text-only
			-- fade, but never cancel an intentional whole-window fade-out.
			if not self.BackgroundFadeOutActive then
				self:RevealTextImmediately()
			end
		end

		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
		self:CalculateSize()

		if string.len(TextBox.Text) &gt; ChatSettings.MaximumMessageLength then
			TextBox.Text = string.sub(
				TextBox.Text,
				1,
				ChatSettings.MaximumMessageLength
			)

			self:UpdatePlaceholder()
			return
		end

		if not self.InCustomState then
			local customState = self.CommandProcessor:ProcessInProgressChatMessage(
				TextBox.Text,
				self.ChatWindow,
				self
			)

			if customState then
				self.InCustomState = true
				self.CustomState = customState
			end
		else
			self.CustomState:TextUpdated()
		end
	end)

	local function UpdateOnFocusStatusChanged(isFocused)
		self.HasTextBoxFocus = isFocused == true
		self.FocusRequestPending = false
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
	end

	self.TextBoxConnections.MessageModeClick =
		MessageModeTextButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if MessageModeTextButton.Text ~= "" then
				self:SetChannelTarget(ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName)
			end
		end)

	self.TextBoxConnections.TextBoxFocused = TextBox.Focused:Connect(function()
		UpdateOnFocusStatusChanged(true)

		-- Use the same legacy transparency and typography as the placeholder.
		-- Focus after an idle fade reveals the input; focus arriving during a
		-- deliberate whole-window fade-out must not reverse that fade.
		if self.BackgroundFadeOutActive then
			self:UpdatePlaceholder()
		else
			self:RevealTextImmediately()
		end
		if self.TextLabel then
			self.TextLabel.Visible = false
			self.TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
		end
		self:CalculateSize()
	end)

	self.TextBoxConnections.TextBoxFocusLost = TextBox.FocusLost:Connect(
		function(_enterPressed, inputObject)
			if inputObject and inputObject.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
				TextBox.Text = ""
			end

			UpdateOnFocusStatusChanged(false)
			self:CalculateSize()
		end
	)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- PROMPT VISIBILITY
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:UpdatePlaceholder()
	local textBox = self.TextBox
	local textLabel = self.TextLabel

	if not textBox or not textLabel then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		textBox.PlaceholderText = ""
	end)

	-- Keep the compatibility label visually identical to the real TextBox.
	-- It is only the idle prompt; it is never overlaid on typed characters.
	textLabel.Font = textBox.Font
	textLabel.TextSize = textBox.TextSize
	textLabel.TextColor3 = textBox.TextColor3
	textLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = textBox.TextStrokeColor3
	textLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = textBox.TextStrokeTransparency
	textLabel.TextWrapped = textBox.TextWrapped
	textLabel.TextScaled = textBox.TextScaled
	textLabel.RichText = textBox.RichText
	textLabel.TextXAlignment = textBox.TextXAlignment
	textLabel.TextYAlignment = textBox.TextYAlignment
	textLabel.TextTransparency = self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency

	if self.UserHasChatOff then
		textLabel.Text = textLabel.Text or ""
		textLabel.Visible = true
		return
	end

	local focused = self.HasTextBoxFocus == true
		or self.FocusRequestPending == true

	if not focused then
		pcall(function()
			focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox
				or textBox:IsFocused()
		end)
	end

	textLabel.Text = self.PlaceholderText or ""
	textLabel.ZIndex = 1
	textLabel.Visible = textBox.Text == "" and not focused
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- TEXT ACCESSORS
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:IsChatWindowVisible()
	if not self.ChatWindow then
		return false
	end

	local ok, visible = pcall(function()
		return self.ChatWindow:GetVisible()
	end)
	return ok and visible == true
end

function methods:RevealTextImmediately()
	if not self.TextBox then
		return false
	end

	-- Roblox can focus this TextBox before Topbar opens the custom window.
	-- Do not force a hidden widget to reveal itself here; Update() reconciles
	-- it on the first frame after the window becomes visible.
	if not self:IsChatWindowVisible() then
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
		return true
	end

	-- A visible, focused input must never inherit a stale close/idle fade target.
	-- The prompt and typed text still share the same 0.4 legacy transparency;
	-- this changes only the shared alpha, not either text style.
	local focused = self.HasTextBoxFocus == true or self.FocusRequestPending == true
	if not focused then
		focused = self:IsFocused()
	end
	if not focused then
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
		return true
	end

	self.BackgroundFadeOutActive = false
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0.4
	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = 0.4
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue = 1

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
	self:UpdatePlaceholder()

	return true
end

function methods:GetTextBox()
	return self.TextBox
end

function methods:GetMessageModeTextButton()
	return self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton
end

function methods:GetMessageModeTextLabel()
	return self:GetMessageModeTextButton()
end

function methods:IsFocused()
	if self.UserHasChatOff or not self.TextBox then
		return false
	end

	local ok, focused = pcall(function()
		return UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == self.TextBox
			or self.TextBox:IsFocused()
	end)

	return ok and focused == true
end

function methods:GetVisible()
	return self.GuiObject ~= nil and self.GuiObject.Visible
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FOCUS AND RELEASE
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:CaptureFocus()
	if self.UserHasChatOff or not self.TextBox then
		return false
	end

	local textBox = self.TextBox

	self.FocusRequestPending = true
	self.HasTextBoxFocus = false

	textBox.Active = true
	textBox.TextEditable = true
	textBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	textBox.MultiLine = false
	textBox.TextWrapped = false
	textBox.ZIndex = 4

	if self.TextLabel then
		self.TextLabel.Visible = false
		self.TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
	end

	self:RevealTextImmediately()

	local ok, err = pcall(function()
		textBox:CaptureFocus()
	end)

	if not ok then
		self.FocusRequestPending = false
		self.HasTextBoxFocus = false
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()

		warn("[ChatBar] CaptureFocus failed: " .. tostring(err))
		return false
	end

	local focusedImmediately = false

	pcall(function()
		focusedImmediately = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox
	end)

	if focusedImmediately then
		self.HasTextBoxFocus = true
		self.FocusRequestPending = false
		self:RevealTextImmediately()
	end

	task.delay(0.15, function()
		if self.TextBox ~= textBox or not self.FocusRequestPending then
			return
		end

		local focused = false

		pcall(function()
			focused = UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() == textBox
		end)

		self.HasTextBoxFocus = focused
		self.FocusRequestPending = false

		if focused then
			self:RevealTextImmediately()
		else
			self:UpdatePlaceholder()
		end
	end)

	return true
end

function methods:ReleaseFocus(didRelease)
	if not self.TextBox then
		return
	end

	pcall(function()
		self.TextBox:ReleaseFocus(didRelease)
	end)

	self.HasTextBoxFocus = false
	self.FocusRequestPending = false
	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
end

function methods:ResetText()
	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.Text = ""
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
	end
end

function methods:SetText(text)
	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.Text = text
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
	end
end

function methods:GetEnabled()
	return self.GuiObject ~= nil and self.GuiObject.Visible
end

function methods:SetEnabled(enabled)
	if not self.GuiObject then
		return
	end

	if self.UserHasChatOff then
		self.GuiObject.Visible = true
	else
		self.GuiObject.Visible = enabled == true
	end
end

function methods:SetTextLabelText(text)
	if self.UserHasChatOff then
		return
	end

	self.PlaceholderText = tostring(text or "")

	if self.TextLabel then
		self.TextLabel.Text = self.PlaceholderText
	end

	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
end

function methods:SetTextBoxText(text)
	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.Text = text
		self:UpdatePlaceholder()
	end
end

function methods:GetTextBoxText()
	return self.TextBox and self.TextBox.Text or ""
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- LEGACY SINGLE-LINE SIZE BEHAVIOR
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:ResetSize()
	self.TargetYSize = 0
	self:TweenToTargetYSize()
end

function methods:CalculateSize()
	if self.CalculatingSizeLock
		or not self.GuiObject
		or not self.TextBox
		or not self.TextLabel then
		return
	end

	self.CalculatingSizeLock = true

	-- Keep this legacy bar single-line and fixed-height.
	local newTargetYSize = 0

	if self.TargetYSize ~= newTargetYSize then
		self.TargetYSize = newTargetYSize
		self:TweenToTargetYSize()
	end

	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
	self.CalculatingSizeLock = false
end

function methods:TweenToTargetYSize()
	self.TargetYSize = 0

	if not self.GuiObject then
		return
	end

	local endSize = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	local current = self.GuiObject.Size

	if current.X.Scale == endSize.X.Scale
		and current.X.Offset == endSize.X.Offset
		and current.Y.Scale == endSize.Y.Scale
		and current.Y.Offset == endSize.Y.Offset then
		return
	end

	local ok = pcall(function()
		self.GuiObject:TweenSize(
			endSize,
			Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
			Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
			0.12,
			true
		)
	end)

	if not ok then
		self.GuiObject.Size = endSize
	end
end

function methods:SetTextSize(textSize)
	if self:IsInCustomState() then
		return
	end

	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.TextSize = textSize
	end

	if self.TextLabel then
		self.TextLabel.TextSize = textSize
	end

	local messageMode = self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton

	if messageMode then
		messageMode.TextSize = textSize
	end
end

function methods:GetDefaultChannelNameColor()
	if ChatSettings.DefaultChannelNameColor then
		return ChatSettings.DefaultChannelNameColor
	end

	return Color3.fromRGB(35, 76, 142)
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CHANNEL PREFIX LAYOUT
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:SetChannelTarget(targetChannel)
	local messageModeTextButton = self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton
	local textBox = self.TextBox
	local textLabel = self.TextLabel

	self.TargetChannel = targetChannel

	if self:IsInCustomState() then
		return
	end

	if targetChannel ~= ChatSettings.GeneralChannelName then
		messageModeTextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1000, 1, 0)
		messageModeTextButton.Text = string.format("[%s] ", targetChannel)

		local channelNameColor = self:GetChannelNameColor(targetChannel)

		messageModeTextButton.TextColor3 = channelNameColor
			or self:GetDefaultChannelNameColor()

		local xSize = messageModeTextButton.TextBounds.X
		messageModeTextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 1, 0)

		textBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -xSize, 1, 0)
		textBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 0, 0)

		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -xSize, 1, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, xSize, 0, 0)
	else
		messageModeTextButton.Text = ""
		messageModeTextButton.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		textBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		textBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)

		textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		textLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	end

	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
end

function methods:IsInCustomState()
	return self.InCustomState
end

function methods:ResetCustomState()
	if not self.InCustomState then
		return
	end

	self.CustomState:Destroy()
	self.CustomState = nil
	self.InCustomState = false

	self.ChatBarParentFrame:ClearAllChildren()
	self:CreateGuiObjects(self.ChatBarParentFrame)

	-- Welcome text is a ChatWindow system message, not a ChatBar placeholder.
	self:SetTextLabelText("")
end

function methods:GetCustomMessage()
	if self.InCustomState then
		return self.CustomState:GetMessage()
	end

	return nil
end

function methods:CustomStateProcessCompletedMessage(message)
	if self.InCustomState then
		return self.CustomState:ProcessCompletedMessage()
	end

	return false
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- FADING
----------------------------------------------------------------

function methods:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	-- Mark a whole-window fade separately from the idle text fade. Delayed focus
	-- or TextBox.Changed events must not reverse a chat-window closing animation.
	self.BackgroundFadeOutActive = true
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self:FadeOutText(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(duration)
	-- The chat window is being shown again; allow both input and prompt to follow
	-- the normal text fade-in together.
	self.BackgroundFadeOutActive = false
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self:FadeInText(duration)
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0.4
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:AnimGuiObjects()
	if self.GuiObject then
		self.GuiObject.BackgroundTransparency =
			self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency
	end

	if self.GuiObjects.TextBoxFrame then
		self.GuiObjects.TextBoxFrame.BackgroundTransparency =
			self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency
	end

	local textTransparency = self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency

	-- Never override TextTransparency based on focus or whether the draft is
	-- nonempty. The animation controls both typed characters and the prompt.
	if self.TextBox then
		self.TextBox.TextTransparency = textTransparency
	end

	if self.TextLabel then
		if self.TextBox then
			self.TextLabel.Font = self.TextBox.Font
			self.TextLabel.TextSize = self.TextBox.TextSize
			self.TextLabel.TextColor3 = self.TextBox.TextColor3
			self.TextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = self.TextBox.TextStrokeColor3
			self.TextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = self.TextBox.TextStrokeTransparency
			self.TextLabel.TextWrapped = self.TextBox.TextWrapped
			self.TextLabel.TextScaled = self.TextBox.TextScaled
			self.TextLabel.RichText = self.TextBox.RichText
			self.TextLabel.TextXAlignment = self.TextBox.TextXAlignment
			self.TextLabel.TextYAlignment = self.TextBox.TextYAlignment
		end
		self.TextLabel.TextTransparency = textTransparency
		self.TextLabel.ZIndex = 1
	end

	if self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton then
		self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton.TextTransparency = textTransparency
		self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton.ZIndex = 2
	end

	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
end

function methods:InitializeAnimParams()
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0.4
	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = 0.4
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue = 1

	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue = 1
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	-- The native slash handler may focus the hidden TextBox before ChatMain
	-- opens its ScreenGui. In that order TextBox.Focused has already fired, so
	-- no second focus event is guaranteed after opening. Reconcile that state
	-- here. Once the actual window is visible, focused text returns to the same
	-- legacy 0.4 transparency as the placeholder instead of staying faded out.
	local windowVisible = self:IsChatWindowVisible()
	local focused = windowVisible and self:IsFocused()

	if focused then
		self.HasTextBoxFocus = true
		self.FocusRequestPending = false

		if self.BackgroundFadeOutActive
			or self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency &gt; 0.4
			or self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency &gt; 0.4 then
			self.BackgroundFadeOutActive = false
			self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0.4
			self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = 0.4
			self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue = 1
		end
	end

	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
	self:UpdatePlaceholder()
end

function methods:SetChannelNameColor(channelName, channelNameColor)
	self.ChannelNameColors[channelName] = channelNameColor

	if self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton.Text == channelName then
		self.GuiObjects.MessageModeTextButton.TextColor3 = channelNameColor
	end
end

function methods:GetChannelNameColor(channelName)
	return self.ChannelNameColors[channelName]
end

----------------------------------------------------------------
-- CONSTRUCTOR
----------------------------------------------------------------

function module.new(CommandProcessor, ChatWindow)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	obj.GuiObject = nil
	obj.ChatBarParentFrame = nil
	obj.TextBox = nil
	obj.TextLabel = nil
	obj.GuiObjects = {}

	obj.eGuiObjectsChanged = Instance.new("BindableEvent")
	obj.GuiObjectsChanged = obj.eGuiObjectsChanged.Event

	obj.TextBoxConnections = {}

	obj.InCustomState = false
	obj.CustomState = nil

	obj.TargetChannel = nil
	obj.CommandProcessor = CommandProcessor
	obj.ChatWindow = ChatWindow

	obj.TweenPixelsPerSecond = 500
	obj.TargetYSize = 0
	obj.AnimParams = {}
	obj.BackgroundFadeOutActive = false

	obj.CalculatingSizeLock = false
	obj.HasTextBoxFocus = false
	obj.FocusRequestPending = false
	obj.PlaceholderText = ""
	obj.UsesNativePlaceholder = false
	obj.ChannelNameColors = {}
	obj.UserHasChatOff = false

	obj:InitializeAnimParams()

	-- SettingsChanged exists in the bundled Yeku settings proxy. Keep this
	-- connection defensive so an incomplete settings module cannot prevent the
	-- entire ChatMain GUI from starting.
	local settingsChanged = ChatSettings.SettingsChanged
	if settingsChanged and type(settingsChanged.Connect) == "function" then
		settingsChanged:Connect(function(setting, value)
			if setting == "ChatBarTextSize" then
				obj:SetTextSize(value)
			end
		end)
	else
		warn(
			"[Yeku ChatBar] DefaultClientChatModules.ChatSettings.SettingsChanged "
				.. "is missing; chat will continue without live text-size updates."
		)
	end

	coroutine.wrap(function()
		local success, canLocalUserChat = pcall(function()
			return Chat:CanUserChatAsync(LocalPlayer.UserId)
		end)

		local permitted = success
			and (RunService:IsStudio() or canLocalUserChat)

		if not permitted then
			obj.UserHasChatOff = true
			obj:DoLockChatBar()
		end
	end)()

	return obj
end

print("[Yeku Legacy ChatBar] source revision loaded: " .. YEKU_CHATBAR_SOURCE_REVISION)

return module

                                        <BinaryString name="SerializedOverrides" />
                                        <ProtectedString name="Source">-- FileName: ChannelsTab.lua
-- Patched legacy channel tab.

local module = {}

local Chat = game:GetService("Chat")
local ClientChatFolder = script:FindFirstAncestor("ClientChat")

local clientChatModules =
	ClientChatFolder:FindFirstChild("DefaultClientChatModules")
local modulesFolder = script.Parent

local ChatSettings = require(clientChatModules:WaitForChild("ChatSettings", 10))
local CurveUtil = require(modulesFolder:WaitForChild("CurveUtil", 10))

local methods = {}
methods.__index = methods

local function CreateGuiObjects()
	local BaseFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BaseFrame.Name = "BaseFrame"
	BaseFrame.Selectable = false
	BaseFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	BaseFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1

	local BackgroundFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	BackgroundFrame.Name = "BackgroundFrame"
	BackgroundFrame.Selectable = false
	BackgroundFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
	BackgroundFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
	BackgroundFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BackgroundFrame.Parent = BaseFrame

	local UnselectedFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	UnselectedFrame.Name = "UnselectedFrame"
	UnselectedFrame.Selectable = false
	UnselectedFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	UnselectedFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	UnselectedFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.ChannelsTabUnselectedColor
	UnselectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
	UnselectedFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local SelectedFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	SelectedFrame.Name = "SelectedFrame"
	SelectedFrame.Selectable = false
	SelectedFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	SelectedFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
	SelectedFrame.BackgroundColor3 = ChatSettings.ChannelsTabSelectedColor
	SelectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	SelectedFrame.Visible = false
	SelectedFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local SelectedFrameBackgroundImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Name = "BackgroundImage"
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Selectable = false
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(94, 101, 115)
	SelectedFrameBackgroundImage.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local borderXOffset = 2
	local blueBarYSize = 4

	local BlueBarLeft = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	BlueBarLeft.Name = "BlueBarLeft"
	BlueBarLeft.Selectable = false
	BlueBarLeft.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -borderXOffset, 0, blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarLeft.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BlueBarLeft.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
	BlueBarLeft.SliceCenter = Rect.new(3, 3, 32, 21)
	BlueBarLeft.Position = UDim2.new(0, borderXOffset, 1, -blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarLeft.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarLeft.png"
	BlueBarLeft.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local BlueBarRight = BlueBarLeft:Clone()
	BlueBarRight.Name = "BlueBarRight"
	BlueBarRight.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarRight.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -blueBarYSize)
	BlueBarRight.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Settings/Slider/SelectedBarRight.png"
	BlueBarRight.Parent = SelectedFrame

	local NameTag = Instance.new("TextButton")
	NameTag.Name = "NameTag"
	NameTag.Selectable = ChatSettings.GamepadNavigationEnabled
	NameTag.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	NameTag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NameTag.Font = ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	NameTag.TextSize = ChatSettings.ChatChannelsTabTextSize
	NameTag.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	NameTag.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.75
	NameTag.Text = ""
	NameTag.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local NameTagNonSelect = NameTag:Clone()
	NameTagNonSelect.Name = "NameTagNonSelect"
	NameTagNonSelect.Parent = UnselectedFrame
	NameTagNonSelect.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	NameTagNonSelect.Active = false

	local NameTagSelect = NameTag:Clone()
	NameTagSelect.Name = "NameTagSelect"
	NameTagSelect.Parent = SelectedFrame
	NameTagSelect.Active = false

	local NewMessageIconFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
	NewMessageIconFrame.Name = "NewMessageIconFrame"
	NewMessageIconFrame.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIconFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
	NewMessageIconFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.8, -9, 0.5, -9)
	NewMessageIconFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIconFrame.Parent = BackgroundFrame

	local NewMessageIcon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
	NewMessageIcon.Name = "NewMessageIcon"
	NewMessageIcon.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIcon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	NewMessageIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIcon.Image = "rbxasset://textures/ui/Chat/MessageCounter.png"
	NewMessageIcon.Visible = false
	NewMessageIcon.Parent = NewMessageIconFrame

	local NewMessageIconText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	NewMessageIconText.Name = "TextLabel"
	NewMessageIconText.Selectable = false
	NewMessageIconText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	NewMessageIconText.Size = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 9)
	NewMessageIconText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -7, 0.5, -7)
	NewMessageIconText.Font = ChatSettings.DefaultFont
	NewMessageIconText.TextSize = 14
	NewMessageIconText.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
	NewMessageIconText.Text = ""
	NewMessageIconText.Parent = NewMessageIcon

	return
		BaseFrame,
		NameTag,
		NameTagNonSelect,
		NameTagSelect,
		NewMessageIcon,
		UnselectedFrame,
		SelectedFrame
end

function methods:Destroy()
	if self.GuiObject then
		self.GuiObject:Destroy()
	end
end

function methods:UpdateMessagePostedInChannel(ignoreActive)
	if self.Active and ignoreActive ~= true then
		return
	end

	self.UnreadMessageCount += 1

	local label = self.NewMessageIcon
	label.Visible = true

	local countLabel = label:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
	if countLabel then
		countLabel.Text = self.UnreadMessageCount &lt; 100
			and tostring(self.UnreadMessageCount)
			or "!"
	end

	local outPos = label.Position
	label.Position = UDim2.new(outPos.X.Scale, outPos.X.Offset, -0.15, 0)

	label:TweenPosition(
		outPos,
		Enum.EasingDirection.Out,
		Enum.EasingStyle.Quad,
		0.15,
		true
	)
end

function methods:SetActive(active)
	self.Active = active
	self.UnselectedFrame.Visible = not active
	self.SelectedFrame.Visible = active

	if active then
		self.UnreadMessageCount = 0
		self.NewMessageIcon.Visible = false
		self.NameTag.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	else
		self.NameTag.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	end
end

function methods:SetTextSize(textSize)
	self.NameTag.TextSize = textSize
	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextSize = textSize
	self.NameTagSelect.TextSize = textSize
end

function methods:FadeOutBackground(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInBackground(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeOutText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 1
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:FadeInText(duration)
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(duration)
end

function methods:AnimGuiObjects()
	local backgroundTransparency = self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency
	local textTransparency = self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency
	local strokeTransparency = self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency

	if self.UnselectedFrame then
		self.UnselectedFrame.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	end

	-- The tab can receive its first animation tick while SelectedFrame's
	-- image children are still being recreated. Resolve each child safely so
	-- an absent BackgroundImage cannot abort the chat/topbar startup thread.
	local selectedFrame = self.SelectedFrame
	if selectedFrame then
		local selectedBackground = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BackgroundImage")
		if selectedBackground and selectedBackground:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			selectedBackground.BackgroundTransparency = backgroundTransparency
			selectedBackground.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
		local blueBarLeft = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarLeft")
		if blueBarLeft and blueBarLeft:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			blueBarLeft.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
		local blueBarRight = selectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarRight")
		if blueBarRight and blueBarRight:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			blueBarRight.ImageTransparency = backgroundTransparency
		end
	end

	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextTransparency = backgroundTransparency
	self.NameTagNonSelect.TextStrokeTransparency = backgroundTransparency

	self.NameTag.TextTransparency = textTransparency
	self.NameTagSelect.TextTransparency = textTransparency
	self.NameTag.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency
	self.NameTagSelect.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency

	local countLabel = self.NewMessageIcon:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
	if countLabel then
		countLabel.TextTransparency = textTransparency
		countLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = strokeTransparency
	end

	self.NewMessageIcon.ImageTransparency = textTransparency
end

function methods:InitializeAnimParams()
	self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = 0
	self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency = 0.75
	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)

	self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = 0.6
	self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue =
		CurveUtil:NormalizedDefaultExptValueInSeconds(0)
end

function methods:Update(dtScale)
	self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Background_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Background_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.Text_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.Text_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency = CurveUtil:Expt(
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_CurrentTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_TargetTransparency,
		self.AnimParams.TextStroke_NormalizedExptValue,
		dtScale
	)

	self:AnimGuiObjects()
end

function module.new(channelName)
	local obj = setmetatable({}, methods)

	local BaseFrame, NameTag, NameTagNonSelect, NameTagSelect,
		NewMessageIcon, UnselectedFrame, SelectedFrame = CreateGuiObjects()

	obj.GuiObject = BaseFrame
	obj.NameTag = NameTag
	obj.NameTagNonSelect = NameTagNonSelect
	obj.NameTagSelect = NameTagSelect
	obj.NewMessageIcon = NewMessageIcon
	obj.UnselectedFrame = UnselectedFrame
	obj.SelectedFrame = SelectedFrame

	obj.BlueBarLeft = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarLeft")
	obj.BlueBarRight = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BlueBarRight")
	obj.BackgroundImage = SelectedFrame:FindFirstChild("BackgroundImage")
	obj.WhiteTextNewMessageNotification =
		NewMessageIcon:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")

	obj.ChannelName = channelName
	obj.UnreadMessageCount = 0
	obj.Active = false

	obj.GuiObject.Name = "Frame_" .. channelName

	local displayName = channelName
	if #displayName &gt; ChatSettings.MaxChannelNameLength then
		displayName = string.sub(
			displayName,
			1,
			math.max(1, ChatSettings.MaxChannelNameLength - 3)
		) .. "..."
	end

	obj.NameTag.Text = ""
	obj.NameTagNonSelect.Text = displayName
	obj.NameTagSelect.Text = displayName

	obj.AnimParams = {}
	obj:InitializeAnimParams()
	obj:AnimGuiObjects()
	obj:SetActive(false)

	return obj
end
